Abstract: Heterogeneous Face Recognition (HFR) aims at matching face images captured across different sensing modalities, such as thermal-to-visible or near-infrared-to-visible, enhancing the usability of face recognition systems in challenging real-world conditions. Although recent HFR methods have achieved significant improvements in performance, many rely on computationally expensive models, making them impractical for deployment on resource-limited edge devices. In this work, we introduce a lightweight yet effective HFR framework by adapting a hybrid CNN-Transformer model originally developed for RGB homogeneous face recognition. Our approach enables efficient end-to-end training with only a small amount of paired heterogeneous data, while still maintaining strong performance on standard RGB face recognition benchmarks. This makes it suitable for both homogeneous and heterogeneous settings. Comprehensive experiments on several challenging HFR and face recognition benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance while keeping computational requirements low.

</details>


### [56] [MIRAGE: Retrieval and Generation of Multimodal Images and Texts for Medical Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04772)
*Miguel Diaz Benito,Cecilia Diana Albelda,Alvaro Garcia Martin,Jesus Bescos Cano,Marcos Escudero-Vinolo,Juan C. SanMiguel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIRAGE是一个基于公开预训练模型的多模态医学图像检索与生成系统，通过CLIP微调模型、医学扩散模型和大语言模型，为医学生提供交互式、可视化的学习工具，无需编程技能和本地计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统医学图谱缺乏交互性且不便携带，在线图像搜索则存在标注错误或内容不完整的问题，因此需要一种可访问、交互性强且来源可信的医学图像学习工具。

Method: 基于PubMed Central的ROCO数据集微调医学版CLIP（MedICaT-ROCO）将文本和图像映射到共享潜在空间；使用医学扩散模型（Prompt2MedImage）生成合成图像；利用大语言模型（Dolly-v2-3b）生成丰富描述；支持双搜索选项用于比较不同医学病症。

Result: 构建了一个完全基于公开预训练模型的可复现系统，具备图像检索、合成图像生成、病症对比等功能，已在Kaggle上部署。

Conclusion: MIRAGE为医学生提供了一个免费、透明、易用的交互式视觉学习工具，无需编程技能或本地计算资源，全球可访问。

Abstract: Access to diverse, well-annotated medical images with interactive learning tools is fundamental for training practitioners in medicine and related fields to improve their diagnostic skills and understanding of anatomical structures. While medical atlases are valuable, they are often impractical due to their size and lack of interactivity, whereas online image search may provide mislabeled or incomplete material. To address this, we propose MIRAGE, a multimodal medical text and image retrieval and generation system that allows users to find and generate clinically relevant images from trustworthy sources by mapping both text and images to a shared latent space, enabling semantically meaningful queries. The system is based on a fine-tuned medical version of CLIP (MedICaT-ROCO), trained with the ROCO dataset, obtained from PubMed Central. MIRAGE allows users to give prompts to retrieve images, generate synthetic ones through a medical diffusion model (Prompt2MedImage) and receive enriched descriptions from a large language model (Dolly-v2-3b). It also supports a dual search option, enabling the visual comparison of different medical conditions. A key advantage of the system is that it relies entirely on publicly available pretrained models, ensuring reproducibility and accessibility. Our goal is to provide a free, transparent and easy-to-use didactic tool for medical students, especially those without programming skills. The system features an interface that enables interactive and personalized visual learning through medical image retrieval and generation. The system is accessible to medical students worldwide without requiring local computational resources or technical expertise, and is currently deployed on Kaggle: http://www-vpu.eps.uam.es/mirage

</details>


### [57] [QuadBox: Accelerating 3D Gaussian Splatting with Geometry-Aware Boxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04844)
*Xinze Li,Bohan Yang,Pengxu Chen,Yiyuan Wang,Hongcheng Luo,Wentao Cheng,Weifeng Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QuadBox通过四个轴对齐包围盒紧密包裹投影高斯体，结合QPass单遍瓦片遍历算法，将3DGS渲染速度提升1.85倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在光栅化管线中高效计算高斯-瓦片精确交集仍是关键挑战，现有方法计算效率不足。

Method: 提出几何感知拉伸因子构建瓦片对齐的QuadBox；设计QPass单遍瓦片遍历算法，利用QuadBox的离散特性将交集检查简化为区间测试。

Result: 在公开数据集上，渲染速度提升1.85倍。

Conclusion: QuadBox有效加速了3DGS的渲染速度，代码已开源。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an advanced technique for real-time novel view synthesis by representing scene geometry and appearance using differentiable Gaussian primitives. However, efficiently computing precise Gaussian-tile intersections remains a critical task in the rasterization pipeline. To this end, we propose QuadBox, a method that leverages four axis-aligned bounding boxes to tightly encapsulate projected Gaussians in a discrete manner. First, we derive a geometry-aware stretching factor that enables the construction of a tile-aligned QuadBox, which covers the elliptical projection and largely excludes irrelevant tiles. Second, we introduce QPass, a single-pass tile traversal algorithm that exhaustively exploits the discrete nature of QuadBox, ensuring that the tile intersection check is performed with simple interval tests. Experiments on public datasets show that our method accelerates the rendering speed of 3DGS by 1.85$\times$. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/Powertony102/QuadBox}{https://github.com/Powertony102/QuadBox}.

</details>


### [58] [Local Intrinsic Dimension Unveils Hallucinations in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05026)
*Bartlomiej Sobieski,Matthew Tivnan,Dawid Płudowski,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Pengfei Jin,Przemyslaw Biecek,Quanzheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型诱导流形不稳定性的视角来解释扩散模型中的结构幻觉，发现局部内在维度（LID）是其主要驱动因素，并提出Intrinsic Quenching（IQ）机制通过降低LID来缓解幻觉，在多个基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成的结构性幻觉（如手部多指等违反结构规则的异常）虽然已被多视角研究（如模式插值），但缺乏统一的解释框架和有效的纠正机制。

Method: 1) 证明基于流形不稳定性构建的幻觉过滤器性能优于或等同于时序过滤器；2) 追溯不稳定性的根源为局部内在维度（LID）；3) 提出Intrinsic Quenching（IQ），通过直接降低LID来缓解幻觉。

Result: IQ在多个基准测试中一致优于标准幻觉缓解基线，并能有效保障下游医学成像任务中的解剖一致性。

Conclusion: 流形不稳定性是扩散模型结构性幻觉的重要成因，LID是核心驱动因素，所提IQ机制为缓解幻觉提供了高效且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models are prone to generating structural hallucinations - samples that match the statistical properties of the training data yet defy underlying structural rules, resulting in anomalies like hands with more than five fingers. Recent research studied this failure mode from several viewpoints, offering partial explanations to their occurrence, such as mode interpolation. In this work, we propose a complementary perspective that treats hallucinations as instabilities on the model-induced manifold. We begin by showing that a hallucination filter based on such instabilities matches or exceeds the performance of the recently proposed temporal one. By tracing the source of these instabilities, we identify local intrinsic dimension (LID) as their primary driver and propose Intrinsic Quenching (IQ), a direct corrective mechanism that deflates it to alleviate hallucinations. IQ consistently outperforms standard hallucination reduction baselines across a wide array of benchmarks and offers a highly promising solution for enforcing anatomical consistency in downstream medical imaging tasks.

</details>


### [59] [Direct Product Flow Matching: Decoupling Radial and Angular Dynamics for Few-Shot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05054)
*Hongxu Chen,Yanghao Wang,Bowei Zhu,Hongxiang Li,Zhen Wang,Ziqi Jiang,Lin Li,Rui Liu,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对现有流匹配方法在视觉-语言模型小样本适配中的几何先验不兼容问题，提出基于黎曼几何的扭曲积流匹配框架(WP-FM)，并推导出直接积流匹配(DP-FM)。该方法通过解耦径向-角向流形、引入常数扭曲度量，实现了恒速角向测地线传输与独立径向演化，同时利用分类器无引导注入缺失的数据集信息，在11个基准上达到新最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法在预训练跨模态特征上存在不兼容的几何先验，导致适配性能次优。本文从极分解（径向和角向子流形）视角分析了三个被忽视的局限性：1) 径向-角向耦合导致角向流形上的非均匀速度，造成回归训练困难和额外截断误差；2) 特征归一化丢弃了模态置信度信息，无法区分分布内外数据；3) 无条件的上下文无关流动无法恢复数据集特定信息的丢失。

Method: 提出扭曲积流匹配(WP-FM)统一黎曼框架，推导出直接积流匹配(DP-FM)：引入常数扭曲度量，构建解耦的圆柱形流形（直积流形），实现独立径向演化和恒速角向测地线传输；同时采用分类器无引导，以预训练VLM的隐状态为条件注入缺失的数据集特定信息。

Result: 在11个基准数据集上，DP-FM在多步小样本适配任务中取得了新的最优性能（state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: 通过几何视角揭示现有流匹配方法的根本局限，并提出基于黎曼几何的解耦框架有效解决了径向-角向耦合、特征归一化信息丢失和上下文无关流动等问题，为多步小样本适配提供了更优的几何建模方案。

Abstract: Recent flow matching (FM) methods improve the few-shot adaptation of vision-language models, by modeling cross-modal alignment as a continuous multi-step flow. In this paper, we argue that existing FM methods are inherently constrained by incompatible geometric priors on pre-trained cross-modal features, resulting in suboptimal adaptation performance. We first analyze these methods from a polar decomposition perspective (i.e., radial and angular sub-manifolds). Under this new geometric view, we identify three overlooked limitations in them: 1) Angular dynamics distortion: The radial-angular coupling induces non-uniform speed on the angular sub-manifold, leading to regression training difficulty and extra truncation errors. 2) Radial dynamics neglect: Feature normalization discards modality confidence, failing to distinguish out-of-distribution and in-distribution data, and abandoning crucial radial dynamics. 3) Context-agnostic unconditional flow: Dataset-specific information loss during pre-trained cross-modal feature extraction remains unrecovered. To resolve these issues, we propose warped product flow matching (WP-FM), a unified Riemannian framework that reformulates alignment on a warped product manifold. Within this framework, we derive direct product flow matching (DP-FM) by introducing a constant-warping metric, which yields a decoupled cylindrical manifold (i.e., direct product manifold). DP-FM enables independent radial evolution and constant-speed angular geodesic transport, effectively eliminating angular dynamics distortion while preserving radial consistency. Meanwhile, we incorporate classifier-free guidance by conditioning the flow on the pre-trained VLMs' hidden states to inject missing dataset-specific information. Extensive results across 11 benchmarks have demonstrated that DP-FM achieves a new state-of-the-art for multi-step few-shot adaptation.

</details>


### [60] [ICPR 2026 Competition on Privacy-Preserving Person Re-Identification from Top-View RGB-Depth Camera (TVRID)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04977)
*Raphaël Delécluse,Hazem Wannous,Laurent Guimas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文报告了ICPR 2026 TVRID竞赛，该竞赛专注于隐私感知的俯视行人重识别，发布了包含86个身份、4台同步顶置Intel RealSense D455相机采集的RGB-深度数据集，并设置RGB Re-ID、Depth Re-ID和RGB↔Depth跨模态检索三个赛道，最终结果表明RGB最难、深度次之、跨模态检索最具挑战性，同时验证了模态不变学习的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在俯视行人重识别场景下，传统RGB方法面临隐私泄露风险，亟需探索深度模态及跨模态方法的可行性。TVRID竞赛旨在提供一个标准化基准，系统评估RGB、深度及跨模态检索在俯视角下的性能差异。

Method: 发布TVRID数据集（86个身份，4台顶置RealSense D455相机同步采集RGB/深度流，包含平坦、上升、下降、倾斜四种视角几何变化）；设置三个评测赛道（RGB Re-ID、Depth Re-ID、RGB↔Depth跨模态检索）；采用统一服务端评估，以mAP和CMC-1为排名指标。

Result: 难度排序为RGB > Depth > 跨模态检索；模态约束条件下的检索更具挑战性；模态不变学习方法可实现强性能。

Conclusion: TVRID为俯视、深度及跨模态行人重识别建立了可复现的基准，数据集、评估脚本和文档均已开源发布。

Abstract: This companion paper reports the ICPR 2026 TVRID competition on privacy-aware top-view person re-identification. We present the competition setting, the released RGB-Depth dataset, and a summary of final results with descriptions of the top entries. TVRID contains 86 identities captured by four synchronized overhead Intel RealSense D455 cameras, with paired RGB/Depth streams and structured geometric variation across flat, ascent, descent, and oblique viewpoints. The evaluation protocol includes three tracks: RGB Re-ID, Depth Re-ID, and RGB$\leftrightarrow$Depth cross-modal retrieval. Submissions are ranked using mAP and CMC-1 under a unified server-side evaluation. The final results show a clear difficulty ordering (RGB $>$ Depth $>$ Cross-Modal), highlighting both the challenge of modality-constrained retrieval and the feasibility of strong performance with modality-invariant learning. By releasing the dataset at https://zenodo.org/records/17909410, the evaluation scripts at https://github.com/RaphaelDel/ICPR-TVRID, and the accompanying documentation, TVRID establishes a reproducible benchmark for top-view, depth-based, and cross-modal person re-id.

</details>


### [61] [Attention-Based Chaotic Self-Supervision for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04985)
*Joao Batista Florindo,Amanda Pontes de Oliveira Ornelas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出混沌去噪自编码器（CDAE），通过混沌变换替代随机掩码进行自监督预训练，并融合注意力机制结合通用与域特异性特征，在皮肤病变和糖尿病视网膜病变数据集上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自监督学习方法（如MAE）使用随机掩码可能破坏医学图像中的细粒度诊断特征，需要一种能保留关键特征的预训练策略。

Method: 1) 混沌去噪自编码器（CDAE）：对输入图像施加混沌变换，训练自编码器重建原始图像，迫使编码器学习鲁棒的域特异性特征；2) 注意力融合机制：将CDAE训练的编码器与标准编码器的特征进行融合，兼顾通用与域特异性表示。

Result: ISIC 2018：准确率0.9221，F1-macro 0.8530；APTOS 2019：准确率0.8644，F1-macro 0.7433。

Conclusion: CDAE通过混沌变换替代掩码能有效保留医学图像的细粒度特征，结合注意力融合机制能提升医学图像分类性能。

Abstract: Deep learning models for medical image classification usually achieve promising results but typically rely on large, annotated datasets or standard transfer learning from ImageNet. Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) has emerged as a powerful alternative, yet common methods like masked autoencoders (MAEs) may inadvertently destroy fine-grained diagnostic features by using random masking. In this paper, we propose a novel SSL pre-training strategy, the Chaotic Denoising Autoencoder (CDAE). Instead of masking, we apply a chaotic transformation to the input image, tasking an autoencoder to reconstruct the original. We hypothesize this forces the encoder to learn robust, domain-specific features by "inverting the chaos". Furthermore, we propose an attentive fusion mechanism that combines features from our CDAE-trained encoder with a standard encoder, leveraging the strengths of both general and domain-specific representations. Our method is evaluated on two public medical datasets: ISIC 2018 (skin lesions) and APTOS 2019 (diabetic retinopathy). The proposed model achieves high performance, with an accuracy of 0.9221 and an F1-macro of 0.8530 on ISIC 2018, and an accuracy of 0.8644 and F1-macro of 0.7433 on APTOS 2019, demonstrating the efficacy of our approach.

</details>


### [62] [What Matters in Practical Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05148)
*Kedar Tatwawadi,Parisa Rahimzadeh,Zhanghao Sun,Zhiqi Chen,Ziyun Yang,Sanjay Nair,Divija Hasteer,Oren Rippel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在感知质量和运行时间上联合优化的实用型学习图像编解码器，通过神经架构搜索和多项技术创新，在iPhone 17 Pro Max上实现了比AV1等传统编解码器2.3-3x的码率节省，同时编码速度达230ms，解码速度150ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学习型编解码器具有直接优化以迎合人眼视觉系统的潜力，但目前尚缺乏兼顾感知质量和实用性的图像编解码器方案。

Method: 对影响实用型学习图像编解码器设计的关键建模选择进行全面研究（包括多项新颖技术消融），结合性能感知的神经架构搜索，在数百万骨干配置中寻找在目标设备运行时下最大化感知压缩性能的模型。

Result: 在主观用户研究中，相比AV1、AV2、VVC、ECM和JPEG-AI实现2.3-3x码率节省；相比最佳学习型编解码器节省20-40%码率。在iPhone 17 Pro Max上，12MP图像编码仅需230ms，解码150ms，速度快于多数顶级ML编解码器在V100 GPU上的运行速度。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化感知质量和运行时效率，提出的编解码器在速度与感知质量之间取得了显著优于现有方案（包括传统标准和学习型方法）的权衡，推动了实用化感知图像编码的发展。

Abstract: One of the major differentiators unlocked by learned codecs relative to their hard-coded traditional counterparts is their ability to be optimized directly to appeal to the human visual system. Despite this potential, a perceptual yet practical image codec is yet to be proposed.
  In this work, we aim to close this gap. We conduct a comprehensive study of the key modeling choices that govern the design of a practical learned image codec, jointly optimized for perceptual quality and runtime -- including within the ablations several novel techniques. We then perform performance-aware neural architecture search over millions of backbone configurations to identify models that achieve the target on-device runtime while maximizing compression performance as captured by perceptual metrics.
  We combine the various optimizations to construct a new codec that achieves a significantly improved tradeoff between speed and perceptual quality. Based on rigorous subjective user studies, it provides 2.3-3x bitrate savings against AV1, AV2, VVC, ECM and JPEG-AI, and 20-40% bitrate savings against the best learned codec alternatives. At the same time, on an iPhone 17 Pro Max, it encodes 12MP images as fast as 230ms, and decodes them in 150ms -- faster than most top ML-based codecs run on a V100 GPU.

</details>


### [63] [Low-Rank Adaptation of Geospatial Foundation Models for Wildfire Mapping Using Sentinel-2 Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04989)
*Ali Shibli,Andrea Nascetti,Yifang Ban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较了三种地理空间基础模型（Terramind、DINOv3、Prithvi-v2）在不同微调策略（全微调、仅解码器微调、LoRA）下对美国及加拿大野火烧毁区域进行制图的性能。基于2017-2023年间3,820个野火事件的Sentinel-2数据，LoRA方法在更新不到1%参数的情况下实现了最强的跨域泛化能力，其中Prithvi-v2结合LoRA取得了最高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火烧毁区域制图对于灾害评估、排放建模及理解火-气候相互作用至关重要。尽管近期地理空间基础模型为卫星图像提供了强大的通用表征，但在地理和时间域迁移下如何高效适配这些模型用于下游地球观测任务仍不清楚。

Method: 利用2017-2023年北美3,820个野火事件的Sentinel-2数据，在多样化生物群落中进行空间和时间泛化测试。系统比较三种GFMs（Terramind、DINOv3、Prithvi-v2）的全微调、仅解码器微调和LoRA三种适配策略。

Result: LoRA在所有实验中提供了最强的跨域泛化能力，仅更新不到1%的参数；Prithvi-v2结合LoRA取得了最高总体精度，相比全微调提升最大。

Conclusion: 地理空间基础模型通过LoRA等轻量级参数高效方法适配后，可为大规模野火烧毁区域制图提供鲁棒且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Wildfire burned-area mapping is essential for damage assessment, emissions modeling, and understanding fire-climate interactions across diverse ecological regions. Recent geospatial foundation models provide strong general-purpose representations for satellite imagery, yet there is still no clear understanding of how to efficiently adapt these models for downstream Earth observation tasks, particularly under geographic and temporal domain shift. This study evaluates three state-of-the-art Geospatial Foundation Models (GFMs) - Terramind, DINOv3, and Prithvi-v2 - for burned-area mapping across the United States and Canada using Sentinel-2 data. Leveraging 3,820 wildfire events from 2017-2023, we conduct spatial and temporal generalization tests across diverse biomes. We systematically compare full fine-tuning, decoder-only fine-tuning, and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for adapting each model. Across all experiments, LoRA provides the strongest cross-domain generalization while updating less than 1% of parameters, demonstrating a favorable trade-off between accuracy and efficiency. Prithvi-v2 with LoRA achieves the highest overall accuracy and the largest improvement compared to full fine-tuning. These findings indicate that geospatial foundation models, when adapted using lightweight parameter-efficient methods such as LoRA, offer a robust and scalable solution for large-scale burned-area mapping. Code is available at https://github.com/alishibli97/wildfire-lora-gfm.

</details>


### [64] [CARD: A Multi-Modal Automotive Dataset for Dense 3D Reconstruction in Challenging Road Topography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05014)
*Gasser Elazab,Frank Neuhaus,Tilman Koß,Malte Splietker,Aditya Date,Michael Unterreiner,Maximilian Jansen,Olaf Hellwich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CARD是一个多模态驾驶数据集，提供稠密3D地面真值，覆盖减速带、坑洼、不规则路面和越野路段等场景，包含立体相机、前后LiDAR等多传感器数据，深度点云密度是KITTI的6.5倍，总里程约110公里，并针对路面不规则性提供2D检测框和评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶数据集大多采集于平坦路面，且提供的稀疏LiDAR真值不足以评估细粒度几何结构，缺乏针对路面不平整区域的场景和数据。

Method: 采用多LiDAR融合（前后LiDAR）与全局快门立体相机、LiDAR惯导里程计、轮速计等传感器套件，通过融合生成每帧约50万有效深度像素的准稠密3D真值，并标注路面不规则区域的2D边界框。

Result: 每帧深度点密度约50万有效像素，比KITTI深度补全多6.5倍，比其他公开数据集平均多10倍；数据集总长110公里、时长4.7小时，覆盖德国和意大利。

Conclusion: CARD为自动驾驶在复杂路面场景下的深度估计和感知任务提供了高密度真值和标准化评估协议，可推动相关领域研究。

Abstract: Autonomous driving must operate across diverse surfaces to enable safe mobility. However, most driving datasets are captured on well-paved flat roads. Moreover, recent driving datasets primarily provide sparse LiDAR ground truth for images, which is insufficient for assessing fine-grained geometry in depth estimation and completion. To address these gaps, we introduce CARD, a multi-modal driving dataset that delivers quasi-dense 3D ground truth across continuous sequences rich in speed bumps, potholes, irregular surfaces and off-road segments. Our sensor suite includes synchronized global-shutter stereo cameras, front and rear LiDARs, 6-DoF poses from LiDAR-inertial odometry, per-wheel motion traces, and full calibration. Notably, our multi-LiDAR fusion yields ~500K valid depth pixels per frame, about 6.5x more than KITTI Depth Completion and 10x more on average than other public driving datasets. The dataset spans ~110 km and 4.7 hours across Germany and Italy. In addition, CARD provides 2D bounding boxes targeting road-topography irregularities, enabling accurate benchmarking for both geometry and perception tasks. Furthermore, we establish a standardized evaluation protocol for road surface irregularities on CARD and benchmark state-of-the-art depth estimation models to provide strong baselines. The CARD dataset is hosted on https://huggingface.co/CARD-Data.

</details>


### [65] [Prompt-Anchored Vision-Text Distillation for Lifelong Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05027)
*Wen Wen,Hao Chen,Shiliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Prompt-Anchored vision-text Distillation (PAD)框架，利用预训练视觉语言模型中冻结的文本编码器作为语义锚点，通过不对称的视觉-文本蒸馏实现终身行人重识别（LReID），在保持语义稳定性的同时实现跨领域泛化，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无范例LReID方法仅依赖纯视觉蒸馏或参数正则化，忽视了文本等辅助模态在保持语义稳定性和增量可塑性方面的潜力。冻结的文本编码器可作为跨领域的稳定语义锚点。

Method: 提出PAD框架：文本侧通过可学习提示蒸馏维持视觉-文本对齐；视觉侧采用EMA教师模型与自适应提示池，为新领域分配新槽位同时冻结旧槽位，实现领域级自适应。

Result: 在多个已见和未见领域上大幅超越现有最先进方法，实现了稳定性与可塑性的良好平衡。

Conclusion: 利用预训练视觉语言模型中冻结文本编码器的语义锚点能力，通过不对称视觉-文本蒸馏框架可有效解决LReID中的语义漂移和灾难性遗忘问题。

Abstract: Lifelong person re-identification (LReID) aims to train a generalizable model with sequentially collected data. However, such models often suffer from semantic drift, limited adaptability, and catastrophic forgetting as new domains emerge. Existing exemplar-free approaches largely rely on visual-only distillation or parameter regularization, while overlooking the potential of auxiliary modalities, such as text, to preserve semantic stability and enable incremental plasticity. We observe that the frozen text encoder in pretrained vision-language models can serve as a stable semantic anchor across domains. To decouple the roles of vision and text, we propose Prompt-Anchored vision-text Distillation (PAD), an asymmetric vision-text framework for semantic alignment and cross-domain generalization. On the textual side, we distill prompts to preserve vision-text alignment under a fixed semantic space, acting as a global semantic reference rather than a dominant learning signal. On the visual side, an EMA-based teacher with an adaptive prompt pool enables domain-wise adaptation by allocating new slots while freezing past ones. Extensive experiments show that PAD substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods across seen and unseen domains, achieving a strong balance between stability and plasticity. Project page is available at https://github.com/zu-zi/PAD.

</details>


### [66] [Taming Outlier Tokens in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05206)
*Xiaoyu Wu,Yifei Wang,Tsu-Jui Fu,Liang-Chieh Chen,Zhe Gan,Chen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现扩散Transformer（DiT）在图像生成中会出现异常token，并提出双阶段寄存器（DSR）方法在编码器和去噪器两端进行干预，有效减少异常伪影并提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Vision Transformer（ViT）中已知存在少量高范数异常token，但它们在生成模型（DiT）中的作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出双阶段寄存器（DSR）方法：在可用时使用训练好的寄存器，否则使用递归测试时寄存器，并为去噪器引入扩散寄存器，同时对编码器和去噪器进行干预。

Result: 在ImageNet和文生图任务上，DSR方法一致减少了异常伪影并提升了生成质量。

Conclusion: 异常token控制是构建更强DiT的重要组成部分。

Abstract: We study outlier tokens in Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for image generation. Prior work has shown that Vision Transformers (ViTs) can produce a small number of high-norm tokens that attract disproportionate attention while carrying limited local information, but their role in generative models remains underexplored. We show that this phenomenon appears in both the encoder and denoiser of modern Representation Autoencoder (RAE)-DiT pipelines: pretrained ViT encoders can produce outlier representations, and DiTs themselves can develop internal outlier tokens, especially in intermediate layers. Moreover, simply masking high-norm tokens does not improve performance, indicating that the problem is not only caused by a few extreme values, but is more closely related to corrupted local patch semantics. To address this issue, we introduce Dual-Stage Registers (DSR), a register-based intervention for both components: trained registers when available, recursive test-time registers otherwise, and diffusion registers for the denoiser. Across ImageNet and large-scale text-to-image generation, these interventions consistently reduce outlier artifacts and improve generation quality. Our results highlight outlier-token control as an important ingredient in building stronger DiTs.

</details>


### [67] [Few-Shot Learning Pipeline for Monkeypox Skin Disease Classification Using CNN Feature Extractors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05034)
*Md. Safirur Rashid,Sabbir Ahmed,Muhammad Usama Islam,Sumona Hoque Mumu,Md. Hasanul Kabir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于SimpleShot的小样本学习框架，利用冻结的预训练CNN骨干提取特征并通过最近质心分类进行猴痘及类痘皮肤病识别。在三个数据集上系统评估六种CNN骨干，发现MobileNetV2_100在2/4/6-way及1/5/10-shot设置下一致最优。跨数据集实验表明二分类Mpox-vs-Others迁移较稳定，多分类在域偏移下性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN在疾病分类中依赖大规模标注数据，这对猴痘等新兴/罕见疾病不切实际，因此需要小样本学习方法来解决标注数据稀缺问题。

Method: 采用SimpleShot（轻量级非参数归纳分类器）作为小样本学习框架：使用冻结的预训练CNN骨干提取皮肤病变图像的特征嵌入，在归一化嵌入空间中进行最近质心比较分类。系统评估了六种CNN骨干，在三个公开数据集上涵盖2/4/6-way和1/5/10-shot多种任务配置。

Result: MobileNetV2_100在所有实验配置下一致取得最高准确率。跨数据集评估显示二分类Mpox-vs-Others迁移相对稳定，而多分类在域偏移下性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 归纳式小样本方法与轻量级CNN骨干结合具有实际应用价值，域鲁棒性对临床部署至关重要。

Abstract: Despite the strong performance of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) in disease classification, their effectiveness often depends on access to large annotated datasets, which is an impractical requirement for emerging or rare conditions such as Monkeypox. To overcome this limitation, we propose a few-shot learning (FSL) framework that employs SimpleShot, a lightweight, non-parametric, inductive classifier, for Monkeypox and pox-like skin disease recognition from limited labeled examples. The proposed pipeline passes the skin lesion images through a frozen, pretrained CNN backbone to obtain feature embeddings, which are then classified via SimpleShot using nearest-centroid comparisons in a normalized embedding space. We systematically benchmark six widely used CNN backbones as feature extractors under consistent experimental settings, enabling fair comparison. Experiments on three publicly available datasets (MSLD v1.0, MSID, and MSLD v2.0) are conducted across 2-way, 4-way, and 6-way tasks with 1-shot, 5-shot, and 10-shot configurations. Among all models, MobileNetV2_100 consistently achieves the highest accuracy. In addition, we present a cross-dataset evaluation for Monkeypox classification, revealing that binary Mpox-vs-Others transfer remains comparatively stable while multi-class performance degrades significantly under domain shift. Together, these results demonstrate the practical utility of combining inductive FSL methods with lightweight CNN backbones and highlight the importance of domain robustness for reliable real-world clinical deployment.

</details>


### [68] [ScriptHOI: Learning Scripted State Transitions for Open-Vocabulary Human-Object Interaction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05057)
*Minh Anh Nguyen,Quang Huy Tran,Bao Ngoc Le,SuiYang Guang,Tuan Kiet Pham,Linh Chi Vo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScriptHOI提出将交互短语分解为软脚本化状态转移（包含身体角色、接触、几何、 affordance、运动和物体状态槽位），通过视觉状态分词器解析人-物对的状态令牌，利用槽位匹配器计算脚本覆盖度和冲突度来校准HOI logits，并结合区间部分标签学习和反事实脚本对比损失，显著提升了稀有和未见交互的识别能力并减少假阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇HOI检测方法主要依赖视觉-语言特征匹配，但预测常被物体 affordance 和短语级共现主导，未能验证手、工具、目标、接触模式和物体状态是否共同支持动作，导致模型仅从刀和蛋糕的存在就预测"切蛋糕"。

Method: 1) 将交互短语分解为身体角色、接触、几何、affordance、运动和物体状态六个槽位的软脚本；2) 视觉状态分词器将检测到的人-物对解析为对应的状态令牌；3) 槽位匹配器计算脚本覆盖度和冲突度来校准HOI logits；4) 区间部分标签学习为未标注候选分配脚本导出的概率上下界；5) 反事实脚本对比损失交换单个脚本槽位以消除物体捷径。

Result: 在HICO-DET、V-COCO和开放词汇HOI分割上，ScriptHOI提升了稀有和未见交互的识别，同时显著减少了affordance冲突的假阳性。

Conclusion: 通过结构化脚本分解和多种约束机制，ScriptHOI有效解决了开放词汇HOI检测中因物体affordance和短语共现导致的错误预测问题，验证了细粒度状态验证对于交互识别的必要性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary human-object interaction (HOI) detection requires recognizing interaction phrases that may not appear as annotated categories during training. Recent vision-language HOI detectors improve semantic transfer by matching human-object features with text embeddings, but their predictions are often dominated by object affordance and phrase-level co-occurrence. As a result, a model may predict \textit{cut cake} from the presence of a knife and a cake without verifying whether the hand, tool, target, contact pattern, and object state jointly support the action. We propose \textbf{ScriptHOI}, a structured framework that represents each interaction phrase as a soft scripted state transition. Rather than treating a phrase as a single class token, ScriptHOI decomposes it into body-role, contact, geometry, affordance, motion, and object-state slots. A visual state tokenizer parses each detected human-object pair into corresponding state tokens, and a slot-wise matcher estimates both script coverage and script conflict. These two quantities calibrate HOI logits, expose missing visual evidence, and provide training constraints for incomplete annotations. To avoid suppressing valid but unannotated interactions, we further introduce interval partial-label learning, which constrains unannotated candidates with script-derived lower and upper probability bounds instead of assigning closed-world negatives. A counterfactual script contrast loss swaps individual script slots to discourage object-only shortcuts. Experiments on HICO-DET, V-COCO, and open-vocabulary HOI splits show that ScriptHOI improves rare and unseen interaction recognition while substantially reducing affordance-conflict false positives.

</details>


### [69] [Height-Guided Projection Reparameterization for Camera-LiDAR Occupancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05072)
*Yuan Wu,Zhiqiang Yan,Jiawei Lian,Zhengxue Wang,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiPR提出了一种高度引导的投影重参数化方法，利用激光雷达生成BEV高度图自适应调整3D参考点采样范围，解决固定柱状投影空间难以捕捉场景稀疏性和高度变化的问题，在3D占据预测任务上实现了SOTA性能并保持实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于固定柱状投影的2D-to-3D视图变换方法，因均匀采样无法捕捉真实场景的稀疏性和高度变化，导致特征对应模糊和聚合不可靠。

Method: 1) 利用激光雷达编码BEV高度图，获取点云最大高度先验；2) 基于高度先验对每个柱体的采样范围进行自适应重参数化，将投影点重分布至几何有意义区域；3) 掩码去除高度图中的无效部分；4) 提出渐进式高度条件训练策略，从真值高度逐渐过渡到激光雷达高度，缓解训练不稳定性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上一致超越现有SOTA方法，且保持实时推理能力。

Conclusion: 高度引导的自适应投影空间重参数化有效提升了3D占据预测中特征聚合的质量，代码和预训练模型已开源。

Abstract: 3D occupancy prediction aims to infer dense, voxel-wise scene semantics from sensor observations, where the 2D-to-3D view transformation serves as a crucial step in bridging image features and volumetric representations. Most previous methods rely on a fixed projection space, where 3D reference points are uniformly sampled along pillars. However, such sampling struggles to capture the sparsity and height variations of real-world scenes, leading to ambiguous correspondences and unreliable feature aggregation. To address these challenges, we propose HiPR, a camera-LiDAR occupancy framework with Height-Guided Projection Reparameterization. HiPR first encodes LiDAR into a BEV height map to capture the maximum height of the point cloud. HiPR then adjusts the sampling range of each pillar using the height prior, enabling adaptive reparameterization of the projection space. As a result, the projected points are redistributed into geometrically meaningful regions rather than fixed ranges. Meanwhile, we mask out the invalid parts of the height map to avoid misleading the feature aggregation. In addition, to alleviate the training instability caused by noisy LiDAR-derived heights, we introduce a training-time Progressive Height Conditioning strategy, which gradually transitions the conditioning signal from ground-truth heights to LiDAR heights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HiPR consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods while maintaining real-time inference. The code and pretrained models can be found at https://github.com/Rayn-Wu/HiPR.

</details>


### [70] [FlowDIS: Language-Guided Dichotomous Image Segmentation with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05077)
*Andranik Sargsyan,Shant Navasardyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowDIS是一种基于流匹配框架的二值图像分割方法，通过学习时间相关向量场将图像分布转换为掩码分布，并采用位置感知实例配对训练策略实现文本提示控制，在DIS-TE测试集上相比最优方法F_β^ω提升5.5%，MAE降低43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二值图像分割方法在保留细粒度细节和捕捉前景语义结构方面存在不足，需要一种能够同时保持高精度分割和具备可控性的方法。

Method: 基于流匹配框架学习时间相关向量场，将图像分布传输到对应掩码分布；提出位置感知实例配对训练策略实现文本提示控制，支持精确的像素级目标分割。

Result: 在DIS-TE测试集上，相比最优DIS方法，FlowDIS的F_β^ω度量提升5.5%，MAE降低43%。

Conclusion: FlowDIS在有无语言引导的情况下均显著优于现有方法，验证了流匹配框架在二值图像分割任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate image segmentation is essential for modern computer vision applications such as image editing, autonomous driving, and medical image analysis. In recent years, Dichotomous Image Segmentation (DIS) has become a standard task for training and evaluating highly accurate segmentation models. Existing DIS approaches often fail to preserve fine-grained details or fully capture the semantic structure of the foreground. To address these challenges, we present FlowDIS, a novel dichotomous image segmentation method built on the flow matching framework, which learns a time-dependent vector field to transport the image distribution to the corresponding mask distribution, optionally conditioned on a text prompt. Moreover, with our Position-Aware Instance Pairing (PAIP) training strategy, FlowDIS offers strong controllability through text prompts, enabling precise, pixel-level object segmentation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches both with and without language guidance. Compared with the best prior DIS method, FlowDIS achieves a 5.5% higher $F_β^ω$ measure and 43% lower MAE ($\mathcal{M}$) on the DIS-TE test set. The code is available at: https://github.com/Picsart-AI-Research/FlowDIS

</details>


### [71] [A unified Benchmark for Multi-Frame Image Restoration under Severe Refractive Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05079)
*Maxim V. Shugaev,Md Reshad Ul Hoque,Bridget Kennedy,Joseph T. Riley,Fiona Hwang,Justin Hagen,Harvir Ghuman,Ethan Garcia-O'Donnell,Syed Noor Qadri,Freddie Santiago,Mun Wai Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向强非均匀折射性几何畸变视频恢复的综合基准，涵盖从湍流般轻微扭曲到强不连续折射变形，包含实验室真实数据和基于物理模型的合成数据，评估了从经典配准到扩散模型的多种方法，并建立了像素级和感知级的多维度评价体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要针对轻度大气湍流，缺乏在强且高度非均匀折射条件（如通过湍流空气或水面拍摄）下系统评估几何畸变恢复方法的基准。

Method: 构建了包含实验室真实数据和基于物理光线折射建模的合成数据的基准，涵盖四种畸变等级和多种表面波类型；评估方法包括简单基线、经典配准、DATUM以及提出的基于扩散的V-cache方法；使用像素级（PSNR, SSIM）和感知级（LPIPS, DINO, CLIP）指标进行评价。

Result: 建立了首个大规模几何畸变去除分析框架，为高度畸变光学环境下的视频重建算法开发和评估提供了基础。

Conclusion: 该基准填补了强折射性几何畸变视频恢复领域缺乏系统评估的空白，为未来算法研究奠定了新基础。

Abstract: Video sequence capturing through refractive dynamic media, such as a turbulent air or water surface, often suffer from severe geometric distortions and temporal instability. While recent advances address mild atmospheric turbulence, no existing benchmarks systematically evaluate restoration methods under strong and highly nonuniform refractive conditions. We present a comprehensive benchmark for geometric distortion removal in video, covering a range from turbulence-like mild warping to strong discontinuous refractive deformations. The benchmark includes both laboratory-captured real data and synthetic sequences generated for static scenes via physics-based light refraction modeling across four distortion levels and multiple surface wave types. We evaluate a spectrum of methods from simple baselines and classical registration algorithms to advanced learning-based approaches including DATUM and our proposed diffusion based V-cache for high and extreme distortions regimes. Evaluation uses both pixel-level (PSNR, SSIM), and perceptual (LPIPS, DINO, CLIP) metrics providing the first large scale analysis of geometric distortion removal. Our benchmark establishes a new foundation for developing and evaluating algorithms capable of reconstructing video from highly distorted optical environments. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/iafoss/refractive-mfir-benchmark.

</details>


### [72] [Wasserstein-Aligned Localisation for VLM-Based Distributional OOD Detection in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05161)
*Bernhard Kainz,Johanna P Mueller,Matthew Baugh,Cosmin Bercea*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WALDO框架，将零样本异常定位重构为基于最优传输理论的比较推理问题，通过熵加权Sliced Wasserstein距离、Goldilocks区域采样和自一致性聚合三大模块，在NOVA脑MRI基准上相比零样本基线实现了19%的相对提升（mAP@30达43.5%），且跨模型验证统计显著（p<0.01）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的零样本异常定位方法缺乏健康解剖背景的上下文信息，性能受限。作者将问题重新定义为比较推理，通过结构与正常参考分布的对比来识别异常。

Method: 提出WALDO框架，包含三个核心模块：(i) 基于熵加权Sliced Wasserstein距离，从DINOv2 patch分布中选择解剖感知的参考样本；(ii) Goldilocks区域采样，利用参考相似度与定位精度之间的非单调关系；(iii) 通过加权非极大值抑制进行自一致性聚合。理论层面通过分布散度分析了Goldilocks效应，证明中等相似度的参考能最小化比较视觉推理中的偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 在NOVA脑MRI基准上，WALDO+Qwen2.5-VL-72B达到43.5±1.6% mAP@30（95% CI: [40.4, 46.7]），相对零样本基线提升19%。GPT-4o达32.0±6.5%，Qwen3-VL-32B达32.0±6.6%。配对McNemar检验确认统计显著性（p<0.01）。

Conclusion: 通过引入比较推理和最优传输理论，WALDO在无需训练的条件下显著提升了零样本异常定位性能，为稀有病理检测提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly localisation via vision-language models (VLMs) offers a compelling approach for rare pathology detection, yet its performance is fundamentally limited by the absence of healthy anatomical context. We reformulate zero-shot localisation as a comparative inference problem in which anomalies are identified through structured comparison against reference distributions of normal anatomy. We introduce WALDO, a training-free framework grounded in optimal transport theory that enables comparative reasoning through: (i) entropy-weighted Sliced Wasserstein distances for anatomically-aware reference selection from DINOv2 patch distributions, (ii) Goldilocks zone sampling exploiting the non-monotonic relationship between reference similarity and localisation accuracy, and (iii) self-consistency aggregation via weighted non-maximum suppression. We theoretically analyse the Goldilocks effect through distributional divergence, and show that references with moderate similarity minimize a bias-variance trade-off in comparative visual reasoning. On the NOVA brain MRI benchmark, WALDO with Qwen2.5-VL-72B achieves $43.5_{\pm1.6}\%$ mAP@30 (95\% CI: [40.4, 46.7]), representing a 19\% relative improvement over zero-shot baselines. Cross-model evaluation shows consistent gains: GPT-4o achieves $32.0_{\pm6.5}\%$ and Qwen3-VL-32B achieves $32.0_{\pm6.6}\%$ mAP@30. Paired McNemar tests confirm statistical significance ($p<0.01$). Source code is available at https://github.com/bkainz/WALDO_MICCAI26_demo .

</details>


### [73] [PhysForge: Generating Physics-Grounded 3D Assets for Interactive Virtual World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05163)
*Yunhan Yang,Chunshi Wang,Junliang Ye,Yang Li,Zanxin Chen,Zehuan Huang,Yao Mu,Zhuo Chen,Chunchao Guo,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysForge提出了一种解耦的两阶段框架，结合大规模物理标注数据集PhysDB，利用VLM规划层级物理蓝图，再通过物理扩散模型和KineVoxel Injection机制生成具有物理合理性的可交互3D资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D资产生成方法主要关注静态几何，忽略了物体在交互中所需的物理功能属性，导致生成的资产无法直接用于仿真和具身AI。

Method: 采用解耦两阶段框架：(1) VLM作为"物理建筑师"，规划包含材质、功能、运动学约束的层级物理蓝图；(2) 物理扩散模型通过KineVoxel Injection (KVI)机制在生成高保真几何的同时精确合成运动学参数。

Result: PhysForge能够生成功能上合理、可直接用于仿真的资产，为交互式3D内容和具身智能体提供鲁棒的数据引擎。

Conclusion: 物理驱动的3D资产生成需要基于功能逻辑和层级物理，PhysForge通过解耦设计和KVI机制有效弥合了静态生成与交互仿真之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Synthesizing physics-grounded 3D assets is a critical bottleneck for interactive virtual worlds and embodied AI. Existing methods predominantly focus on static geometry, overlooking the functional properties essential for interaction. We propose that interactive asset generation must be rooted in functional logic and hierarchical physics. To bridge this gap, we introduce PhysForge, a decoupled two-stage framework supported by PhysDB, a large-scale dataset of 150,000 assets with four-tier physical annotations. First, a VLM acts as a "physical architect" to plan a "Hierarchical Physical Blueprint" defining material, functional, and kinematic constraints. Second, a physics-grounded diffusion model realizes this blueprint by synthesizing high-fidelity geometry alongside precise kinematic parameters via a novel KineVoxel Injection (KVI) mechanism. Experiments demonstrate that PhysForge produces functionally plausible, simulation-ready assets, providing a robust data engine for interactive 3D content and embodied agents.

</details>


### [74] [OpenSearch-VL: An Open Recipe for Frontier Multimodal Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05185)
*Shuang Chen,Kaituo Feng,Hangting Chen,Wenxuan Huang,Dasen Dai,Quanxin Shou,Yunlong Lin,Xiangyu Yue,Shenghua Gao,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenSearch-VL是一个完全开源的多模态深度搜索智能体训练方案，通过智能体强化学习、高质量数据构建流水线（Wikipedia路径采样、模糊实体重写、源锚点视觉定位）、多样化工具环境（文本搜索、图像搜索、OCR等）以及多轮致命感知GRPO训练算法，在七个基准测试上平均提升超过10个点，效果媲美商业闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顶级多模态搜索智能体难以复现，缺乏开源的高质量训练数据、透明的轨迹合成流水线和详细的训练方案。

Method: (1) 构建高质量训练数据流水线：Wikipedia路径采样、模糊实体重写、源锚点视觉定位，减少捷径和一步检索崩溃；(2) 设计多样化工具环境：统一文本搜索、图像搜索、OCR、裁剪、锐化、超分辨率、透视校正；(3) 提出多轮致命感知GRPO训练算法：掩码失败后token、单边优势钳制保留失败前有用推理。

Result: 在七个基准测试上平均提升超过10个点，多项任务效果媲美商业闭源模型。

Conclusion: OpenSearch-VL提供了完全开源的训练方案（数据、代码、模型），推动了多模态深度搜索智能体的开放研究。

Abstract: Deep search has become a crucial capability for frontier multimodal agents, enabling models to solve complex questions through active search, evidence verification, and multi-step reasoning. Despite rapid progress, top-tier multimodal search agents remain difficult to reproduce, largely due to the absence of open high-quality training data, transparent trajectory synthesis pipelines, or detailed training recipes. To this end, we introduce OpenSearch-VL, a fully open-source recipe for training frontier multimodal deep search agents with agentic reinforcement learning. First, we curated a dedicated pipeline to construct high-quality training data through Wikipedia path sampling, fuzzy entity rewriting, and source-anchor visual grounding, which jointly reduce shortcuts and one-step retrieval collapse. Based on this pipeline, we curate two training datasets, SearchVL-SFT-36k for SFT and SearchVL-RL-8k for RL. Besides, we design a diverse tool environment that unifies text search, image search, OCR, cropping, sharpening, super-resolution, and perspective correction, enabling agents to combine active perception with external knowledge acquisition. Finally, we propose a multi-turn fatal-aware GRPO training algorithm that handles cascading tool failures by masking post-failure tokens while preserving useful pre-failure reasoning through one-sided advantage clamping. Built on this recipe, OpenSearch-VL delivers substantial performance gains, with over 10-point average improvements across seven benchmarks, and achieves results comparable to proprietary commercial models on several tasks. We will release all data, code, and models to support open research on multimodal deep search agents.

</details>


### [75] [LoViF 2026 The First Challenge on Holistic Quality Assessment for 4D World Model (PhyScore)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05187)
*Wei Luo,Yiting Lu,Xin Li,Haoran Li,Fengbin Guan,Chen Gao,Xin Jin,Yong Li,Zhibo Chen,Sijing Wu,Kang Fu,Yunhao Li,Ziang Xiao,Huiyu Duan,Jing Liu,Qiang Hu,Xiongkuo Min,Guangtao Zhai,Manxi Sun,Zixuan Guo,Yun Li,Ziyang Chen,Manabu Tsukada,Zhengyang Li,Zhenglin Du,Yi Wen,Licheng Jiao,Fang Liu,Lingling Li,Yiwen Ren,Zhilong Song,Dubing Chen,Yucheng Zhou,Tianyi Yan,Huan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoViF 2026 PhyScore挑战赛聚焦于对世界模型生成视频进行全方位质量评估，包含2D和4D两种生成场景。参赛者需构建联合预测视频质量、物理真实感、条件-视频对齐和时间一致性四个维度的指标，并定位物理异常时间戳。基准数据集包含1554个视频，涵盖26个类别，采用人工标注+自动质控确保标签可靠性。评估综合时间戳IOU和SRCC/PLCC指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估实践中，仅凭感知质量不足以判断生成动态的物理合理性、时间连贯性及与输入条件的一致性，存在评估缺口。

Method: 构建联合预测四个维度（视频质量、物理真实感、条件-视频对齐、时间一致性）的指标，并定位物理异常时间戳。采用人工标注+自动质控生成标签，使用TimeStamp_IOU和SRCC/PLCC复合评估协议。

Result: 构建了包含1554个视频（由7个代表性世界模型生成）的基准数据集，覆盖3个赛道（文本→2D、图像→4D、视频→4D）和26个物理相关类别。提交方案在评分预测和异常定位上均有方法层面的洞察。

Conclusion: 该挑战赛提供了世界模型生成视频的全面评估框架，验证了多维度联合评估和异常定位的有效性，为未来物理感知视频质量评估提供了基准和方法参考。

Abstract: This paper reports on the LoViF 2026 PhyScore challenge, a competition on holistic quality assessment of world-model-generated videos across both 2D and 4D generation settings. The challenge is motivated by a central gap in current evaluation practice: perceptual quality alone is insufficient to judge whether generated dynamics are physically plausible, temporally coherent, and consistent with input conditions. Participants are required to build a metric that jointly predicts four dimensions, i.e., Video Quality, Physical Realism, Condition-Video Alignment, and Temporal Consistency. Depart from that, participants also need to localize physical anomaly timestamps for fine-grained diagnosis.
  The benchmark dataset contains 1,554 videos generated by seven representative world generative models, organized into three tracks (text-2D, image-to-4D, and video-to-4D) and spanning 26 categories. These categories explicitly cover physics-relevant scenarios, including dynamics, optics, and thermodynamics, together with diverse real-world and creative content. To ensure label reliability, scores and anomaly timestamps are produced through trained human annotation with an additional automated quality-control pass.
  Evaluation is based on both score prediction and anomaly localization, with a composite protocol that combines TimeStamp_IOU and SRCC/PLCC. This report summarizes the challenge design and provides method-level insights from submitted solutions.

</details>


### [76] [D-OPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Continuously Tuning Step-Distilled Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05204)
*Dengyang Jiang,Xin Jin,Dongyang Liu,Zanyi Wang,Mingzhe Zheng,Ruoyi Du,Xiangpeng Yang,Qilong Wu,Zhen Li,Peng Gao,Harry Yang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出D-OPSD训练范式，通过让模型在自身轨迹上进行自蒸馏（学生仅条件于文本特征，教师条件于多模态特征），解决了少步扩散模型在监督微调中保持少步推理能力的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高效少步图像生成模型（如Z-Image-Turbo、FLUX.2-klein）在直接连续监督微调时，会损害其固有的少步推理能力，需要一种能保持该能力的微调方法。

Method: 利用现代扩散模型（以LLM/VLM为编码器）的上下文继承能力，实现on-policy自蒸馏训练：学生模型仅以文本特征为条件，教师模型以文本和图像多模态特征为条件，在模型自身roll-out轨迹上最小化两个预测分布。

Result: D-OPSD使模型能够在自身轨迹上学习新概念、风格等，同时不牺牲原始的少步推理能力。

Conclusion: D-OPSD为少步蒸馏扩散模型的监督微调提供了一种有效的on-policy学习范式，在不损失推理效率的前提下实现新知识注入。

Abstract: The landscape of high-performance image generation models is currently shifting from the inefficient multi-step ones to the efficient few-step counterparts (e.g, Z-Image-Turbo and FLUX.2-klein). However, these models present significant challenges for directly continuous supervised fine-tuning. For example, applying the commonly used fine-tuning technique would compromises their inherent few-step inference capability. To address this, we propose D-OPSD, a novel training paradigm for step-distilled diffusion models that enables on-policy learning during supervised fine-tuning. We first find that the modern diffusion model where the LLM/VLM serves as the encoder can inherit its encoder's in-context capabilities. This enables us to make the training as an on-policy self-distillation process. Specifically, during training, we make the model acts as both the teacher and the student with different contexts, where the student is conditioned only on the text feature, while the teacher is conditioned on the multimodal feature of both the text prompt and the target image. Training minimizes the two predicted distributions over the student's own roll-outs. By optimized on the model's own trajectory and under it's own supervision, D-OPSD enables the model to learn new concept, style, etc. without sacrificing the original few-step capacity.

</details>


### [77] [Syn4D: A Multiview Synthetic 4D Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05207)
*Zeren Jiang,Yushi Lan,Yihang Luo,Yufan Deng,Zihang Lai,Edgar Sucar,Christian Rupprecht,Iro Laina,Diane Larlus,Chuanxia Zheng,Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Syn4D是一个面向动态场景的多视角合成数据集，提供完整的3D几何标注（包括相机运动、深度图、密集跟踪和人体姿态），支持任意像素到任意时间/相机的3D反投影，在4D重建、3D点跟踪、相机重定向和人体姿态估计等任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态场景密集3D重建与跟踪研究受限于高质量数据集的匮乏，缺乏具有密集、完整且精确几何标注的公开数据。

Method: 构建多视角合成动态场景数据集Syn4D，包含地面真值相机运动、深度图、密集跟踪和参数化人体姿态标注，支持任意像素到任意时刻和任意相机的3D反投影。

Result: 在4D场景重建、3D点跟踪、几何感知相机重定向和人体姿态估计等多个下游任务中均取得良好效果。

Conclusion: Syn4D能够有效促进动态场景理解和时空建模领域的研究。

Abstract: Dense 3D reconstruction and tracking of dynamic scenes from monocular video remains an important open challenge in computer vision. Progress in this area has been constrained by the scarcity of high-quality datasets with dense, complete, and accurate geometric annotations. To address this limitation, we introduce Syn4D, a multiview synthetic dataset of dynamic scenes that includes ground-truth camera motion, depth maps, dense tracking, and parametric human pose annotations. A key feature of Syn4D is the ability to unproject any pixel into 3D to any time and to any camera. We conduct extensive evaluations across multiple downstream tasks to demonstrate the utility and effectiveness of the proposed dataset, including 4D scene reconstruction, 3D point tracking, geometry-aware camera retargeting, and human pose estimation. The experimental results highlight Syn4D's potential to facilitate research in dynamic scene understanding and spatiotemporal modeling.

</details>

### [78] [Free Energy-Driven Reinforcement Learning with Adaptive Advantage Shaping for Unsupervised Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04065)
*Yiming Huang,Zhenbo Shi,Xin-Cheng Wen,Jichuan Zeng,Cuiyun Gao,Peiyi Han,Chuanyi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FREIA是一种基于自由能原理和自适应优势塑造的无监督强化学习算法，通过动态调整奖励和学习信号来改进大语言模型的推理能力，在9个数据集上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督RL方法无法适应模型推理能力的动态变化，在缺乏真实监督信号时可能导致策略优化方向错误。

Method: (1) 自由能驱动奖励(FER)：基于自由能原理平衡共识与探索；(2) 自适应优势塑造(AAS)：根据采样奖励的统计特性动态调整学习信号。

Result: 在9个数据集、3类推理任务上超越其他无监督RL基线，数学推理任务中Pass@1提升0.5-3.5个百分点。

Conclusion: FREIA通过自适应奖励和学习信号机制，有效解决了无监督RL中策略优化被误导的问题，提升了LLM的自我改进能力。

Abstract: Unsupervised reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling self-improvement in large language models (LLMs). However, existing unsupervised RL-based methods often lack the capacity to adapt to the model's evolving reasoning capabilities during training. Therefore, these methods can misdirect policy optimization in the absence of ground-truth supervision. To address this issue, we introduce FREIA, a novel RL-based algorithm built on two key innovations: (1) Free Energy-Driven Reward (FER) adapts rewards to balance consensus and exploration based on the Free Energy Principle. (2) Adaptive Advantage Shaping (AAS) adaptively adjusts learning signals based on the statistical characteristics of sampled rewards. Empirical evaluations on nine datasets across three reasoning tasks showcase that FREIA outperforms other unsupervised RL-based baselines. Notably, in mathematical reasoning tasks, FREIA surpasses other methods by an average of 0.5 to 3.5 points in Pass@1 using the DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B model.

</details>


### [79] [Adapt to Thrive! Adaptive Power-Mean Policy Optimization for Improved LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04066)
*Yiming Huang,Zhenbo Shi,Shuzheng Gao,Cuiyun Gao,Peiyi Han,Chuanyi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出APMPO，一种自适应策略优化方法，通过Power-Mean策略优化（PMPO）和反馈自适应裁剪（FAC）来改进基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）范式，使LLM能够根据自身推理能力动态调整学习策略，在数学推理等任务上显著优于GRPO等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法通常采用静态策略优化方案，无法与模型不断演化的推理能力对齐，导致学习动态与模型实际能力不匹配，限制了推理性能的提升。

Method: APMPO包含两个核心创新：(1) Power-Mean策略优化（PMPO），引入广义幂均值目标函数，使模型能从算术均值的信号放大行为自适应过渡到几何均值的一致性强化行为；(2) 反馈自适应裁剪（FAC），基于实时奖励统计信息动态调整裁剪边界，克服静态裁剪机制的局限性。

Result: 在9个数据集、3类推理任务上的实验表明，APMPO优于现有最先进的RLVR基线方法。例如，在使用Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct时，APMPO在数学推理基准上的平均Pass@1分数相比GRPO提升了3.0个百分点。

Conclusion: APMPO通过自适应策略优化有效提升了LLM在RLVR范式下的推理性能，证明了动态调整学习策略以匹配模型能力演变的重要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is an essential paradigm that enhances the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing methods typically rely on static policy optimization schemes that misalign with the model's evolving reasoning capabilities. To address this issue, we propose Adaptive Power-Mean Policy Optimization (APMPO), which comprises two main innovations: Power-Mean Policy Optimization (PMPO) and Feedback-Adaptive Clipping (FAC). Specifically, PMPO introduces a generalized power-mean objective. This enables the model to adaptively transition from the signal-amplifying behavior of the arithmetic mean to the consistency-enforcing behavior of the geometric mean. FAC adaptively adjusts clipping bounds based on real-time reward statistics to overcome the limitations of static mechanisms. Capitalizing on these innovations, APMPO improves learning dynamics and reasoning performance. Extensive experiments on nine datasets across three reasoning tasks showcase the superiority of APMPO over state-of-the-art RLVR-based baselines. For instance, APMPO boosts the average Pass@1 score on mathematical reasoning benchmarks by 3.0 points compared to GRPO when using Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct.

</details>


### [80] [FMI_SU_Yotkova_Kastreva at SemEval-2026 Task 13: Lightweight Detection of LLM-Generated Code via Stylometric Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04157)
*Elitsa Yotkova,Violeta Kastreva,Dimitar Dimitrov,Ivan Koychev,Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究参与SemEval-2026 Task 13子任务A（机器生成代码二分类），提出了一种结合基于比率特征、解析引擎、编程语言分类器和代码-文本行分类器的轻量级方法，使用浅层决策树与启发式规则进行预测，仅需CPU资源即可实现高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨多种编程语言和应用场景的机器生成代码检测任务，要求系统能够泛化到未见过的语言和领域，现有方法依赖大模型计算成本高。

Method: 设计对片段长度不敏感的比率特征；使用解析引擎和编程语言分类器提取描述性相关信号；训练独立的代码-文本行分类器识别嵌入的自然语言片段；结合浅层决策树与基于数据分析的启发式规则生成最终预测。

Result: 方法计算高效，仅需CPU资源训练，推理时间接近瞬时，可作为大型预训练模型的轻量级替代方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的轻量级方法在机器生成代码检测任务上具有竞争力，兼顾效率与性能，为资源受限场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: SemEval-2026 Task 13 investigates machine-generated code detection across multiple programming languages and application scenarios, asking participating systems to generalize to unseen languages and domains. This paper describes our participation in Subtask A (binary classification) and explores both pretrained code encoders and lightweight feature-based methods. We design ratio-based features that are less sensitive to snippet length. To support the extraction of descriptiveness-related signals, we use parsing engines and a programming-language classifier. Additionally, we train a separate code-vs-text line classifier to identify raw natural language segments embedded within samples. We combine a shallow decision tree with heuristic rules derived from data analysis to produce the final predictions. Our approach is computationally efficient, requires only CPU resources for training, and achieves near-instant inference time, offering a lightweight alternative to large pretrained models.

</details>


### [81] [Not All That Is Fluent Is Factual: Investigating Hallucinations of Large Language Models in Academic Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04171)
*Humam Khan,Md Tabrez Nafis,Shahab Saquib Sohail,Aqeel Khalique,Rehan Hasan Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了ChatGPT、Grok、Gemini和Copilot四种主流LLM在学术写作中的幻觉问题，通过80个提示词在四个类别上的评估，引入新的加权指标"幻觉指数(HI)"，发现Grok和Copilot在参考文献生成任务上表现较好但抽象/风格提示困难，而Gemini和ChatGPT语气控制更强但事实性任务中幻觉风险更高，且幻觉行为不仅取决于模型架构，还与任务类型和提示条件相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在学术内容生成中存在严重的幻觉问题，现有评估指标往往无法有效检测这类错误，因此需要系统性地评估不同LLM在学术写作场景下的幻觉表现，并引入更精确的量化指标。

Method: 设计80个提示词，涵盖参考文献生成、事实解释、摘要生成和写作改进四个类别；采用0-5分制评分标准评估事实准确性、参考文献有效性、连贯性、风格一致性和学术语调；提出新的加权指标"幻觉指数(HI)"来量化模型响应中的幻觉程度。

Result: Grok和Copilot在参考文献生成任务上表现较好但抽象/风格提示困难，HI值分别为0.67和0.70；Gemini和ChatGPT语气控制更强但在事实性任务中表现不足、幻觉风险更高，HI值分别为0.53和0.57；幻觉行为不仅取决于模型架构，还与任务类型和提示条件相关。

Conclusion: 不同LLM在学术写作幻觉表现上各有优劣，任务类型和提示条件对幻觉行为有显著影响，该工作为未来研究开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: Large Language models (LLMs) show extraordinary abilities, but they are still prone to hallucinations, especially when we use them for generating Academic content. We have investigated four popular LLMs, ChatGPT, Grok, Gemini, and Copilot for hallucinations specifically for academic writing. We have designed 80 prompts across four categories, namely, reference generation, factual explanation, abstract generation, and writing improvement. We evaluated the model using a 0-5 rubric score, which checks factual accuracy, reference validity, coherence, style consistency, and academic tone. A novel weighted metric, Hallucination Index (HI), was introduced to measure hallucination in the responses generated by the models. Some of the most widely used evaluation metrics often fail to check errors which alter sentiment in machine-translated text. We found that Grok and Copilot perform better on reference generation tasks, but they often struggle with abstract or stylistic prompts, with HI values of 0.67 and 0.70, respectively. Whereas, Gemini and ChatGPT have done well with having stronger tone control, but they lack in writing factual tasks and higher hallucination risk with HI scores of 0.53 and 0.57, respectively. Our study found that hallucination behavior does not depend solely on model architecture but also on the type of task and the prompting conditions we are providing. We propose that our work opens new research dimensions for future researchers.

</details>


### [82] [MedFabric and EtHER: A Data-Centric Framework for Word-Level Fabrication Generation and Detection in Medical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04180)
*Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Qian Qian,Xiaofeng Lin,Dongxu Zhang,Jun Han,Zhichao Yang,Davin Hill,Tamer Soliman,Sanjit Singh Batra,Robert Tillman,Guang Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MedFabric 数据集和 ETHER 检测器，用于解决大语言模型在医疗领域中的捏造（fabrication）问题，通过生成真实的词级捏造数据并设计模块化检测框架，在词级捏造基准上超过现有方法 15% 以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗幻觉数据集难以捕捉捏造现象，存在捏造覆盖有限、人类与LLM文本风格差异、以及幻觉样本合成过程中的分布漂移等问题，亟需更真实且细粒度的词级捏造检测方案。

Method: 提出数据驱动的流水线生成 MedFabric 数据集，保留语法和风格忠实度的同时引入细微事实偏差的词级捏造；进而提出 ETHER 检测器，包含 Text2Table 分解、词掩码填充和混合句对评估三个模块。

Result: 在词级捏造基准上，MedFabric 驱动的 ETHER 检测器相比最先进方法性能提升超过 15%，且在结构相似性上保持一致的检测表现。

Conclusion: MedFabric 和 ETHER 为医疗领域提供了一套可靠、全面且领域特定的词级事实性检测框架，有效缓解了LLM在专业知识场景下的捏造问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models exhibit strong reasoning and semantic understanding capabilities but often hallucinate in domains that require expert knowledge, among which fabrications, the generation of factually incorrect yet fluent statements, pose the greatest risk in medical contexts. Existing medical hallucination datasets inadequately capture fabrication phenomena due to limited fabrication coverage, stylistic disparities between human and LLM-authored texts, and distributional drift during hallucinated sample synthesis. To address this, we propose a data-centric pipeline to generate realistic and word-level fabrications that preserve syntactic and stylistic fidelity while introducing subtle factual deviations, resulting in MedFabric. Building upon this dataset, we introduce ETHER, a modular word-level fabrication detector integrating Text2Table Decomposition, Word Masking and Filling and Hybrid Sentence Pair Evaluation to enhance factual alignment. Empirical results demonstrate that MedFabric outperforms state-of-the-art detectors by over 15% on word-level fabrication benchmarks while maintaining consistent performance across structural similarities, offering a comprehensive framework for reliable and domain-specific factuality detection.

</details>


### [83] [The Impact of Vocabulary Overlaps on Knowledge Transfer in Multilingual Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04196)
*Oona Itkonen,Jörg Tiedemann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了词汇重叠对多语言神经机器翻译中知识迁移的影响，发现领域匹配和语言关联性比共享词汇表更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MNMT中，联合词汇表被广泛使用，但词汇不重叠对模型性能的影响研究不足，关于知识迁移多大程度归因于词汇重叠尚无共识。

Method: 设计了联合词汇表与分离词汇表的系统对比实验，结合与源语言相关/无关的辅助语言，在跨领域设定下进行以强调迁移效果。

Result: 相关语言的更大词汇重叠带来了更好的结果，但实验表明领域匹配和语言关联性比联合词汇表更重要。

Conclusion: 词汇重叠有助于迁移，但领域匹配和语言关联性是更关键的因素。

Abstract: Knowledge transfer, especially across related languages, has been found beneficial for multilingual neural machine translation (MNMT), but some aspects are still under-explored and deserve further investigation. A joint vocabulary is most often applied to form a uniform word embedding space, but since the impact of a disjoint vocabulary on model performance is far less studied, there is no consensus on how much knowledge transfer is mainly due to vocabulary overlap. In this paper, we present systematic experiments with joint and disjoint vocabularies, and auxiliary languages related and unrelated to the source language. We design this experiment in an out-of-domain setup in order to emphasize transfer and the impact of the auxiliary language. As expected, we yield better results with more extensive vocabulary overlaps typical for related languages, but our experiments also show that domain-match and language relatedness are more important than a joint vocabulary.

</details>


### [84] [Nsanku: Evaluating Zero-Shot Translation Performance of LLMs for Ghanaian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04208)
*Stephen E. Moore,Mich-Seth Owusu,Akwasi Asare,Lawrence Adu Gyamfi,Paul Azunre,Joel Budu,Jonathan Asiamah,Elias Dzobo,Kelvin Newman,Edmund O. Benefo,Gerhardt Datsomor,Onesimus Addo Appiah,Ama Branoa Banful,Lucas Woedem Kpatah,Saani Mustapha Deishini,John Ayernor*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Nsanku，一个系统评估19种大语言模型在43种加纳语言与英语之间零样本机器翻译性能的基准。结果显示gemini-2.5-flash综合表现最佳（平均26.88），但所有模型均未达到高表现与高一致性兼具的Leaders象限，表明当前LLMs尚无法可靠地用于大规模加纳语言翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在资源丰富的语言上表现出色，但在低资源非洲语言上的性能尚未得到充分理解和系统评估。

Method: 从YouVersion Bible平台收集43种加纳语言的300句对作为评估数据，使用BLEU和chrF两种自动评估指标，辅以平均准确率和跨语言一致性维度，对19个开源和专有LLM进行零样本机器翻译评估。

Result: gemini-2.5-flash取得最高平均分26.88（BLEU:24.60, chrF:29.16），其次是claude-sonnet-4-5（24.87）和gpt-4.1（23.20）。开源模型中kimi-k2-instruct-0905领先（20.87）。Siwu语言平均分最高（25.73），Nkonya最低（11.65）。所有模型均未达到Leaders象限。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs在加纳语言翻译上尚不可靠，Nsanku提供了一个公开、可扩展的评估基础设施以推动非洲语言NLP研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive multilingual capabilities for well-resourced languages, yet their performance on low-resource African languages remains poorly understood and largely unevaluated. This paper presents Nsanku, a systematic benchmark that evaluates the zero-shot machine translation performance of 19 open-weight and proprietary LLMs across 43 Ghanaian languages paired with English. Evaluation sentences were sourced from the YouVersion Bible platform, providing 300 sentence pairs per language. Two complementary automatic metrics are employed: Bilingual Evaluation Understudy (BLEU) and Character n-gram F-Score (chrF), alongside an average accuracy score and a cross-language consistency dimension. Nsanku represents the most comprehensive LLM translation evaluation for Ghanaian languages conducted to date. Results show that gemini-2.5-flash achieves the highest overall average score of 26.88 (BLEU: 24.60, chrF: 29.16), followed by claude-sonnet-4-5 at 24.87 (BLEU: 22.46, chrF: 27.28) and gpt-4.1 at 23.20 (BLEU: 21.15, chrF: 25.24). Among open-weight models, kimi-k2-instruct-0905 leads at an average score of 20.87. A critical finding from the consistency analysis is that no model and no language reached the Leaders quadrant of high performance and high consistency simultaneously, indicating that current LLMs are not yet reliably usable for Ghanaian language translation at scale. Siwu achieved the highest per-language average score at 25.73 while Nkonya scored lowest at 11.65. Nsanku establishes a publicly available, community-extensible evaluation infrastructure for African language NLP research.

</details>


### [85] [Self-Prompting Small Language Models for Privacy-Sensitive Clinical Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04221)
*Yao-Shun Chuang,Tushti Mody,Uday Pratap Singh,Shirindokht Shiraz,Chun-Teh Lee,Ryan Brandon,Muhammad F Walji,Xiaoqian Jiang,Bunmi Tokede*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种本地可部署的框架，利用小语言模型自生成、验证、优化和评估实体特定的提示词，用于从高度非结构化的牙科进展记录中提取多种临床实体。通过多提示集成推理、QLoRA监督微调和直接偏好优化，Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct在DPO后达到微/宏F1分数0.864/0.837，表明自动提示优化与轻量级偏好后训练可支持本地小模型的临床信息提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙科进展记录的临床命名实体识别面临文档高度非结构化、领域专用性强且涉及隐私敏感的挑战，现有方法难以有效处理。

Method: 开发了本地可部署框架，使小语言模型能够自生成、验证、优化和评估实体特定提示；采用多提示集成推理评估候选模型，并使用QLoRA监督微调和直接偏好优化对选定模型进行进一步适配。

Result: Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct在基线中表现最佳；经DPO后，Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct分别达到微/宏F1分数0.864/0.837和0.806/0.797。

Conclusion: 自动提示优化结合轻量级偏好后训练能够支持使用本地部署的小语言模型进行可扩展的临床信息提取，且模型性能差异显著，需进行任务特定评估而非依赖通用基准。

Abstract: Clinical named entity recognition from dental progress notes is challenging because documentation is highly unstructured, domain-specific, and often privacy-sensitive. We developed a locally deployable framework that enables small language models to self-generate, verify, refine, and evaluate entity-specific prompts for extracting multiple clinical entities from dental notes. Using 1,200 annotated notes, we evaluated candidate open-weight models with multi-prompt ensemble inference and further adapted selected models using QLoRA-based supervised fine-tuning and direct preference optimization. Model performance varied substantially, highlighting the need for task-specific evaluation rather than reliance on generic benchmarks. Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct achieved the strongest baseline performance. After DPO, Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct achieved micro/macro F1 scores of 0.864/0.837 and 0.806/0.797, respectively. These findings suggest that automated prompt optimization combined with lightweight preference-based post-training can support scalable clinical information extraction using locally deployed small language models.

</details>


### [86] [Towards Self-Referential Analytic Assessment: A Profile-Based Approach to L2 Writing Evaluation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04298)
*Stefano Bannò,Kate Knill,Mark Gales*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习者内部优劣势识别的自参照评估框架，取代传统的排名相关指标来评估自动作文评分系统。实验使用ICNALE GRA数据集，通过双面Rasch模型校准评分者严厉度，比较了人类评分者和三个大语言模型在零样本设置下的分析性评分表现。结果显示，LLM在识别相对弱点（负面反馈）方面优于单个人类评分者，而人类评分者在识别相对优势（正面反馈）方面更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动作文评分研究依赖排名相关的相关性指标来验证分析性评估的有效性，但这些指标会掩盖分析维度之间的内在关联以及晕轮效应（整体印象渗入细粒度评分），导致高相关性可能掩盖系统的真实诊断行为。因此，需要一种新的评估框架来更准确地衡量系统的诊断能力。

Method: 提出自参照评估框架，聚焦于识别学习者内部的优劣势而非学习者之间的排名。使用ICNALE GRA数据集（由多达80名训练有素的评分者对第二语言写作进行整体和分析性标注），采用双面Rasch模型校准评分者严厉度，推导出十个分析维度和整体熟练度的公平平均分。在零样本设置下比较人类操作评分者和三个大语言模型的分析性评分表现。

Result: LLM在识别多个熟练度方面的相对弱点（负面反馈）方面优于单个人类评分者；人类评分者在识别相对优势（正面反馈）方面表现更强。

Conclusion: 排名基础的评价方法在分析性评估中存在局限性；基于学习者内部、面向能力剖面（profile-based）的方法对于评估和部署LLM在自动作文评分中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Automated essay scoring (AES) research often relies on rank-based correlation metrics to validate analytic assessment. However, such metrics obscure both intrinsic intercorrelations among analytic dimensions that arise from the structure of writing proficiency itself and halo effects, whereby holistic impressions bleed into fine-grained component scores. As a result, high correlations may mask a system's true diagnostic behaviour. In this study, we propose a novel self-referential assessment evaluation framework that focuses on identifying intra-learner strengths and weaknesses rather than assessing inter-learner rankings. We conduct experiments on the publicly available ICNALE GRA, a uniquely dense second-language writing dataset annotated holistically and analytically by up to 80 trained raters. To obtain reliable reference scores, we apply two-facet Rasch modelling to calibrate rater severity and derive fair average scores across ten analytic aspects and holistic proficiency. We compare the analytic scoring performance of human operational raters and three large language models (LLMs) in a zero-shot setting. Our results show that LLMs tend to outperform single human raters in identifying relative weaknesses (negative feedback) across several proficiency aspects, while human raters remain stronger at identifying relative strengths (positive feedback). Overall, our findings highlight the limitations of rank-based evaluation for analytic assessment and demonstrate the value of intra-learner, profile-based methods for assessing and deploying LLMs in AES.

</details>


### [87] [SWAN: Semantic Watermarking with Abstract Meaning Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04305)
*Ziping Ye,Gourab Dey,Christos Christodoulopoulos,Charith Peris,Anil Ramakrishna,Weitong Ruan,Aram Galstyan,Kai-Wei Chang,Rahul Gupta,Ninareh Mehrabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SWAN是一种基于AMR语义结构的水印框架，通过将签名嵌入句子语义表示而非token选择偏好，在保持检测性能的同时显著提升了对抗释义攻击的鲁棒性（AUC最高提升13.9个百分点），且无需训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本水印方法多通过调整token选择偏好嵌入签名，这种方式在文本被释义（paraphrase）后容易失效，缺乏对语义级别的鲁棒性保障。

Method: 利用AMR（抽象语义表示）将水印签名嵌入句子的语义结构中；通过提示LLM按照选定AMR模板生成句子实现水印注入；检测时使用现成AMR解析器提取语义结构，再通过单比例z检验进行统计验证。

Result: 在RealNews基准上，SWAN在未改动的水印文本上达到SOTA检测性能；面对释义攻击时，检测AUC比此前方法最高提升13.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 将水印锚定于AMR语义结构是一种简单、有效且基于提示的鲁棒文本溯源验证方法，为语义级水印研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce SWAN (Semantic Watermarking with Abstract Meaning Representation), a novel framework that embeds watermark signatures into the semantic structure of a sentence using Abstract Meaning Representation (AMR). In contrast to existing watermarking methods, which typically encode signatures by adjusting token selection preferences during text generation, SWAN embeds the signature directly in the sentence's semantic representation. As the signature is encoded at the semantic structure level, any paraphrase that preserves meaning automatically preserves the signature. SWAN is training-free: watermark injection is achieved by prompting an LLM to generate sentences guided by a selected AMR template while maintaining contextual coherence, and detection uses an off-the-shelf AMR parser followed by a simple one-proportion z-test. Empirical evaluation on the RealNews benchmark shows SWAN matches state-of-the-art detection performance on unaltered watermarked text, while significantly improving robustness against paraphrasing, increasing detection AUC by up to 13.9 percentage points compared to prior methods. These results demonstrate that SWAN's approach of anchoring watermarks in AMR semantic structures provides a simple, effective, and prompt-based method for robust text provenance verification under paraphrasing, opening new avenues for semantic-level watermarking research.

</details>


### [88] [NoisyCausal: A Benchmark for Evaluating Causal Reasoning Under Structured Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04313)
*Zhi Xu,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NoisyCausal基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在结构化噪声下的因果推理能力，并设计了一种模块化推理框架，通过将LLM与显式因果结构结合，显著提升了在噪声环境中的因果推理鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在因果推理中难以区分相关性与因果性，尤其在存在错误信息或无关干扰的噪声条件下表现不佳，现有基准测试缺乏对结构化噪声的系统性评估。

Method: 构建NoisyCausal基准，从真实因果图生成实例并用自然语言场景上下文化，注入无关干扰、值扰动、混杂和部分可观测等可控噪声；提出模块化推理框架，引导LLM提取变量、构建因果图，并基于该图重构结构化提示进行推理。

Result: 该方法在NoisyCausal上显著优于标准提示和推理基线，且在Cladder等外部基准上无需任务特定调优即展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 结合因果抽象与语言驱动推理对于实现LLM中忠实且鲁棒的因果理解至关重要，符号化因果结构有助于弥补纯统计推理的不足。

Abstract: Causal reasoning in natural language requires identifying relevant variables, understanding their interactions, and reasoning about effects and interventions, often under noisy or ambiguous conditions. While large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong general reasoning abilities, they struggle to disentangle correlation from causation, particularly when observations are partially incorrect or irrelevant information is present. In this work, we introduce NoisyCausal, a new benchmark designed to evaluate causal reasoning under structured noise. Each instance is generated from a ground-truth causal graph and contextualized with a natural language scenario by injecting controllable forms of noise, such as irrelevant distractors, value perturbations, confounding, and partial observability. Moreover, we propose a modular reasoning framework that combines LLMs with explicit causal structure to address these challenges. Our method prompts the LLM to extract variables, construct a causal graph from context, and then reformulates the reasoning task as a structured prompt grounded in this graph. Rather than relying on statistical patterns alone, the LLM is guided by symbolic structure, enabling more interpretable and robust inference. Experimental results show that our method significantly outperforms standard prompting and reasoning baselines on NoisyCausal. Furthermore, it generalizes well to external benchmarks such as Cladder without task-specific tuning. Our findings highlight the importance of combining causal abstractions with language-driven reasoning to achieve faithful and robust causal understanding in LLMs.

</details>


### [89] [Telegraph English: Semantic Prompt Compression via Structured Symbolic Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04426)
*Mikhail L. Arbuzov,Sisong Bei,Ziwei Dong,Dmitri Kalaev,Alexey A. Shvets*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Telegraph English (TE)，一种将自然语言重写为符号化形式化结构的提示压缩协议。与基于令牌删除的方法不同，TE执行完整的语义重写，将输入分解为原子事实行，用约40个逻辑和关系符号替换冗长短语，并根据文档信息密度自适应压缩比。在LongBench-v2的4,081个问答对上测试，约50%令牌压缩下，TE在GPT-4.1上保留了99.1%的关键事实准确率，在所有模型和任务上均优于LLMLingua-2，且在小模型上差距更大（精细任务上高达11个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示压缩方法（如LLMLingua-2）采用基于分类器的令牌删除策略，以固定比率删除低重要性令牌，缺乏语义理解与结构保留能力，导致信息丢失且压缩比不可自适应。亟需一种保留语义结构且自适应压缩的方法。

Method: Telegraph English (TE)协议包含三个核心设计：1) 将自然语言分解为原子事实行（每行为独立可寻址的事实）；2) 用约40种逻辑与关系符号替代冗长短语；3) 压缩比根据文档信息密度自适应。原子行结构使得压缩与语义分块成为同一操作，输出即语义索引。通过设计压缩提示（prompt）引导LLM执行重写。

Result: 在LongBench-v2的4,081个问答对上，以约50%令牌削减率，TE在GPT-4.1上达到99.1%关键事实准确率。在与LLMLingua-2的对比中，TE在所有模型和任务上均表现更优，小模型上差距更显著（精细任务达11个百分点）。

Conclusion: TE是一种有效的提示压缩方法，通过语义重写而非令牌删除实现更高保真度。其显式关系结构尤其有利于小容量模型，表明结构化的语义表示能够弥补模型容量的不足。已开源语法规范、压缩提示、基准数据和参考实现。

Abstract: We introduce Telegraph English (TE), a prompt-compression protocol that rewrites natural language into a symbol-rich, formally-structured dialect. Where token-deletion methods such as LLMLingua-2 train a classifier to delete low-importance tokens at a fixed ratio, TE performs a full semantic rewrite: it decomposes the input into atomic fact lines, substitutes verbose phrases with $\sim$40 logical and relational symbols, and lets the compression ratio adapt to each document's information density. A consequence of the line-structure rule is that compression and semantic chunking become the same operation -- each output line is an independently addressable fact, so the compressed representation is simultaneously a semantic index. We evaluate TE on 4{,}081 question-answer pairs from LongBench-v2 across five OpenAI models and two difficulty levels. At roughly 50\% token reduction, TE preserves 99.1\% accuracy on key facts with GPT-4.1 and outperforms LLMLingua-2 at matched compression ratios on every model and task tested. The gap widens on smaller models -- up to 11 percentage points on fine-detail tasks -- suggesting that explicit relational structure compensates for limited model capacity. We release the grammar specification, compression prompt, benchmark data, and reference implementation.

</details>


### [90] [DoGMaTiQ: Automated Generation of Question-and-Answer Nuggets for Report Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04458)
*Bryan Li,William Walden,Yu Hou,Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu,Dawn Lawrie,Jame Mayfield,Eugene Yang,Chris Callison-Burch,Laura Dietz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DoGMaTiQ是一个全自动流水线，用于生成基于QA的原子事实（nugget）集，从而实现对长文本、引用支撑报告的自动评估。该流水线包含三个阶段：文档驱动的nugget生成、释义聚类和基于质量标准的nugget子集选择。在跨语言TREC任务（NeuCLIR和RAGTIME）上的实验表明，其排名相关性可与人工评估相媲美，且对异常系统具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于nugget的评估方法需要人工为每个主题手动构建nugget集，这一过程劳动密集且难以扩展到新的信息需求，尤其在跨语言场景下更为突出。因此，需要一种自动化的nugget生成方法，以支持可扩展、高质量的检索增强生成（RAG）系统报告评估。

Method: 提出DoGMaTiQ流水线，包含三个阶段：(1) 文档驱动的nugget生成（document-grounded nugget generation），从源文档中提取QA对；(2) 释义聚类（paraphrase clustering），合并语义等价的nugget；(3) 基于质量标准的nugget子集选择（nugget subselection），筛选出高质量nugget。然后将生成的nugget集成到AutoArgue评估框架中，实现全自动评估。

Result: 在两个跨语言TREC共享任务（NeuCLIR和RAGTIME）上的实验表明，DoGMaTiQ生成的评估排名与人工参与的评估以及完全人工判断的排名具有强相关性。进一步的消融分析表明，强大的LLM nugget生成器是关键因素，并且DoGMaTiQ诱导的系统排名对异常系统具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DoGMaTiQ能够自动生成高质量的QA-based nugget集，有效解决了人工构建nugget的瓶颈问题，实现了全自动的长文本报告评估。研究代码和资源已公开，以促进该领域的后续研究。

Abstract: Evaluation of long-form, citation-backed reports has lately received significant attention due to the wide-scale adoption of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems. Core to many evaluation frameworks is the use of atomic facts, or nuggets, to assess a report's coverage of query-relevant information attested in the underlying collection. While nuggets have traditionally been represented as short statements, recent work has used question-answer (QA) representations, enabling fine-grained evaluations that decouple the information need (i.e. the question) from the potentially diverse content that satisfies it (i.e. its answers).
  A persistent challenge for nugget-based evaluation is the need to manually curate sets of nuggets for each topic in a test collection -- a laborious process that scales poorly to novel information needs. This challenge is acute in cross-lingual settings, where information is found in multilingual source documents. Accordingly, we introduce DoGMaTiQ, a pipeline for generating high-quality QA-based nugget sets in three stages: (1) document-grounded nugget generation, (2) paraphrase clustering, and (3) nugget subselection based on principled quality criteria. We integrate DoGMaTiQ nuggets with AutoArgue -- a recent nugget-based evaluation framework -- to enable fully automatic evaluation of generated reports. We conduct extensive experiments on two cross-lingual TREC shared tasks, NeuCLIR and RAGTIME, showing strong rank correlations with both human-in-the-loop and fully manual judgments. Finally, detailed analysis of our pipeline reveals that a strong LLM nugget generator is key, and that the system rankings induced by DoGMaTiQ are robust to outlier systems. We facilitate future research in report evaluation by publicly releasing our code and artifacts at https://github.com/manestay/dogmatiq.

</details>


### [91] [CAR: Query-Guided Confidence-Aware Reranking for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04495)
*Zhipeng Song,Yizhi Zhou,Xiangyu Kong,Jiulong Jiao,Xuezhou Ye,Chunqi Gao,Xueqing Shi,Yuhang Zhou,Heng Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CAR（置信度感知重排序）框架，利用生成器置信度变化作为文档有用性信号进行重排序，无需训练即可即插即用，在多个数据集和检索器上显著提升NDCG@5，且重排序增益与下游生成F1提升高度相关（Spearman ρ=0.964）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统重排序方法主要优化查询-文档相关性，而非生成有用性；相关文档仍可能引入噪声，而排名较低的文档可能更能降低生成器的不确定性。

Method: 基于生成器置信度变化进行重排序，置信度通过查询仅和查询-文档条件下多个采样答案的语义一致性估计；提升置信度的文档被提升，降低的被降级，不确定情况保留基线顺序；查询级门控避免对已经置信的查询进行不必要的干预。

Result: 在BEIR四个数据集上，CAR在稀疏和稠密检索器、LLM基础和监督重排序器、四个LLM骨干上一致提升NDCG@5；对YesNo重排序器平均提升25.4%；重排序增益与下游生成F1的Spearman ρ=0.964。

Conclusion: CAR是一种查询引导、无需训练、即插即用的重排序框架，通过置信度感知机制有效将重排序优化目标从相关性转向生成有用性，显著提升RAG系统的检索和生成性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) depends on document ranking to provide useful evidence for generation, but conventional reranking methods mainly optimize query-document relevance rather than generation usefulness. A relevant document may still introduce noise, while a lower-ranked document may better reduce the generator's uncertainty. We propose CAR (Confidence-Aware Reranking), a query-guided, training-free, and plug-and-play reranking framework that uses generator confidence change as a document usefulness signal. CAR estimates confidence through the semantic consistency of multiple sampled answers under query-only and query-document conditions.
