Documents that significantly increase confidence are promoted, those that decrease confidence are demoted, and uncertain cases preserve the baseline order, while a query-level gate avoids unnecessary intervention on already confident queries. Experiments on four BEIR datasets show that CAR consistently improves NDCG@5 across sparse and dense retrievers, LLM-based and supervised rerankers, and four LLM backbones. Notably, CAR improves the YesNo reranker by 25.4 percent on average under Contriever retrieval, and its ranking gains strongly correlate with downstream generation F1 improvements, achieving Spearman rho = 0.964.

</details>


### [92] [SCOUT: Active Information Foraging for Long-Text Understanding with Decoupled Epistemic States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04496)
*Zhenliang Zhang,Wenqing Wang,Yong Hu,Yaming Yang,Jiaheng Gao,Chen Shen,Xiaojun Wan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SCOUT是一种主动信息觅食范式，通过自适应粗到细探索和锚定状态更新，在百万token级长文本理解中实现与SOTA专有模型相当的性能，同时将token消耗降低最多8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文本理解方法存在两难：端到端长上下文LLM存在高token消耗和注意力稀释问题，而专用LTU智能体通过任务无关的抽象（如图构建或索引）牺牲了推理保真度。作者的核心洞见是：查询相关信息在文档中通常是稀疏的，因此有效推理应基于查询充分的子集而非整个上下文。

Method: SCOUT将文档视为可探索环境，通过状态级差距诊断自适应地在粗到细探索和锚定状态更新之间交替，逐步将认知状态收缩至查询充分性。核心包括：(1) 基于provenance的紧凑认知状态表示；(2) 状态级差距诊断指导探索策略；(3) 粗到细探索与锚定更新交替进行。

Result: SCOUT在性能上与SOTA专有模型相当，同时将token消耗降低最多8倍。此外，随着上下文长度扩展，SCOUT保持稳定，显著缓解了实际中的成本-性能权衡问题。

Conclusion: SCOUT证明了从被动处理转向主动信息觅食是解决百万token级长文本理解中推理保真度与计算效率矛盾的有效范式。

Abstract: Long-Text Understanding (LTU) at million-token scale requires balancing reasoning fidelity with computational efficiency. Frontier long-context LLMs can process millions of token contexts end-to-end, but they suffer from high token consumption and attention dilution. In parallel, specialized LTU agents often sacrifice fidelity through task-agnostic abstractions like graph construction or indexing. We identify a key insight for LTU: query-relevant information is typically sparse relative to the full document, so effective reasoning should rely on a query-sufficient subset rather than the entire context. To address this, we propose SCOUT, a new paradigm for LTU that shifts from passive processing to active information foraging. It treats the document as an explorable environment and answers from a compact, provenance-grounded epistemic state. Guided by state-level gap diagnosis, SCOUT adaptively alternates between coarse-to-fine exploration and anchored state updates that progressively contract its epistemic state toward query sufficiency. Experiments show that SCOUT matches state-of-the-art proprietary models while reducing token consumption by up to 8x. Moreover, SCOUT remains stable as context length scales, substantially alleviating the practical cost-performance trade-off.

</details>


### [93] [Harnessing Linguistic Dissimilarity for Language Generalization on Unseen Low-Resource Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04500)
*Jinju Kim,Haeji Jung,Youjeong Roh,Jong Hwan Ko,David R. Mortensen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对低资源语言变体，提出两阶段语言泛化框架：TOPPing源选择方法 + VACAI-Bowl轻量架构（分支分别学习变体特有和不变特征），在依存句法任务上平均提升54.62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言变体在多语言模型中长期被忽视，现有跨语言研究主要关注语言间对齐与差异最小化，但低资源变体中的语言差异性恰恰是泛化到未见变体的重要线索。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：(1) TOPPing——专为低资源变体设计的源选择方法；(2) VACAI-Bowl——轻量级架构，一个分支学习变体特有属性，并行分支通过对抗训练捕获变体不变属性。

Result: 在10种低资源变体的依存句法分析任务上，VACAI-Bowl结合TOPPing平均提升54.62%。

Conclusion: 低资源语言变体的差异性可作为泛化线索，所提框架有效利用变体特有与不变特征，显著提升结构预测任务性能。

Abstract: Low-resource language varieties used by specific groups remain neglected in the development of Multilingual Language Models. A great deal of cross-lingual research focuses on inter-lingual language transfer which strives to align allied varieties and minimize differences between them. However, for low-resource varieties, linguistic dissimilarity is also an important cue allowing generalization to unseen varieties. Unlike prior approaches, we propose a two-stage Language Generalization framework that focuses on capturing variety-specific cues while also exploiting rich overlap offered by high-resource source variety. First, we propose TOPPing, a source-selection method specifically designed for low-resource varieties. Second, we suggest a lightweight VACAI-Bowl architecture that learns variety-specific attributes with one branch while a parallel branch captures variety-invariant attributes using adversarial training. We evaluate our framework on structural prediction tasks, which are among the few tasks available, as proxy for performance on other downstream tasks. Using VACAI-Bowl with TOPPing yields an average 54.62% improvement in the dependency parsing task, which serves as a proxy for performance on other downstream tasks across 10 low-resource varieties.

</details>


### [94] [Distilling Bayesian Belief States into Language Models for Auditable Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04507)
*Zongqi Cui,Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出BOND框架，通过贝叶斯教师模型推断对手优先级信念并蒸馏到8B学生模型，在CaSiNo数据集上超越SOTA，实现可审计的谈判智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端LLM谈判智能体的对手信念通常是隐式且难以检查的，缺乏可审计性。

Method: BOND框架包含：1) LLM贝叶斯教师模型，对六种对手优先级排序进行后验更新并用于菜单决策；2) 8B学生语言模型，同时输出谈判动作和归一化后验信念。

Result: CaSiNo数据集上Brier分数0.085，蒸馏学生模型0.114，优于均匀基线(0.139)；8B学生模型相比70B结构CoT基线产生更好的后验校准。

Conclusion: 蒸馏保留了可评分的信念报告，通过后验轨迹、信念-策略误差分解和后缀干预等诊断工具实现了审计性，使弱信念-行为耦合变得可见。

Abstract: Negotiation agents must infer what their counterpart values, update those beliefs over dialogue turns, and choose actions under uncertainty. End-to-end large language models (LLMs) can imitate negotiation dialogue, but their opponent beliefs are usually implicit and difficult to inspect. We propose BOND (Bayesian Opponent-belief Negotiation Distillation), a framework for auditable negotiation. BOND consists of an LLM-based Bayesian teacher that scores dialogue contexts against the six possible opponent priority orderings, updates a posterior over those orderings, and uses the posterior for menu-based decision making, as well as a smaller 8B student language model that emits both negotiation actions and normalized posterior beliefs as tagged text. In the CaSiNo negotiation dataset, BOND outperforms the state-of-the-art and achieves mean Brier score 0.085 over opponent-priority posteriors. The distilled student preserves much of this belief signal, achieving Brier 0.114, below the uniform six-ordering reference of 5/36, approximately 0.139. Compared with a 70B structured-CoT baseline, the significantly smaller 8B student model yields substantially better elicited posterior calibration. We further showcase auditability through posterior trajectories, belief-versus-policy error decomposition, and posterior-prefix interventions. These diagnostics reveal that distillation preserves a scoreable belief report more strongly than causal belief-conditioned control, making weak belief-action coupling visible, not hidden.

</details>


### [95] [RaguTeam at SemEval-2026 Task 8: Meno and Friends in a Judge-Orchestrated LLM Ensemble for Faithful Multi-Turn Response Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04523)
*Ivan Bondarenko,Roman Derunets,Oleg Sedukhin,Mikhail Komarov,Ivan Chernov,Mikhail Kulakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种异构集成七个LLM的系统，在SemEval-2026 Task~B的MTRAGEval任务中获得第一名，条件调和平均数达0.7827，远超最强基线（0.6390）。系统使用GPT-4o-mini作为评判者选择最佳候选，并推出了领域自适应模型Meno-Lite-0.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MTRAGEval任务中，单一模型性能有限，需要结合多种模型和提示策略的多样性来提升生成质量，并探索高性价比的领域自适应模型。

Method: 异构集成七个LLM，使用两种提示变体；采用GPT-4o-mini作为评判者，为每个实例选择最佳候选输出；并开发了7B参数的领域自适应模型Meno-Lite-0.1。

Result: 在26支参赛队伍中排名第一，条件调和平均数为0.7827，超越最强基线gpt-oss-120b（0.6390）；消融实验表明集成始终优于任何单一模型。

Conclusion: 模型家族、规模和提示策略的多样性对集成系统性能至关重要；同时指出了MTRAGEval数据集的标注局限性和改进方向。

Abstract: We present our winning system for Task~B (generation with reference passages) in SemEval-2026 Task~8: MTRAGEval. Our method is a heterogeneous ensemble of seven LLMs with two prompting variants, where a GPT-4o-mini judge selects the best candidate per instance. We ranked 1st out of 26 teams, achieving a conditioned harmonic mean of 0.7827 and outperforming the strongest baseline (gpt-oss-120b, 0.6390). Ablations show that diversity in model families, scales, and prompting strategies is essential, with the ensemble consistently beating any single model. We also introduce Meno-Lite-0.1, a 7B domain-adapted model with a strong cost--performance trade-off, and analyse MTRAGEval, highlighting annotation limitations and directions for improvement. Our code is publicly available: https://github.com/RaguTeam/ragu_mtrag_semeval

</details>


### [96] [RLearner-LLM: Balancing Logical Grounding and Fluency in Large Language Models via Hybrid Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04539)
*Qiming Bao,Juho Leinonen,Paul Denny,Michael J. Witbrock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RLearner-LLM与Hybrid-DPO框架，融合DeBERTa-v3 NLI信号与验证器LLM分数，在知识密集型生成任务中提升逻辑正确性，消除人类标注并克服单一信号优化的对齐税，在五个学术领域、三种基座架构上实现NLI最高提升6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DPO在知识密集型生成中存在事实逻辑对齐缺口——来自人类或LLM裁判的偏好信号存在系统性冗长偏差，奖励流畅性而非逻辑正确性，导致SFT模型NLI蕴含度仅0.05-0.22。

Method: 提出RLearner-LLM与Hybrid-DPO自动偏好管线：(1) 融合DeBERTa-v3 NLI信号作为逻辑正确性指示器；(2) 结合验证器LLM分数；(3) 消除人工标注依赖；(4) 在不同基座架构（LLaMA-2-13B, Qwen3-8B, Gemma 4 E4B-it）上跨五个学术领域评估。

Result: NLI最高提升6倍，15个实验单元中11个NLI提升，答案覆盖率一致提升；Gemma 4 E4B-it上四个领域NLI提升+11.9%至2.4倍，推理速度全面加快；Qwen3-8B模型在95%成对比较中战胜自身SFT基线；复现了前沿比较器上的冗长偏差现象。

Conclusion: Hybrid-DPO有效缓解对齐税，可扩展至紧凑基座模型仍保持优势；暴露了LLM-as-a-judge的冗长偏差，论证了在知识密集型生成中应采用逻辑感知指标（NLI, ACR）替代纯LLM评判。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), the efficient alternative to PPO-based RLHF, falls short on knowledge-intensive generation: standard preference signals from human annotators or LLM judges exhibit a systematic verbosity bias that rewards fluency over logical correctness. This blindspot leaves a logical alignment gap -- SFT models reach NLI entailment of only 0.05-0.22 despite producing fluent text. We propose RLearner-LLM with Hybrid-DPO: an automated preference pipeline that fuses a DeBERTa-v3 NLI signal with a verifier LLM score, removing human annotation while overcoming the "alignment tax" of single-signal optimization. Evaluated across five academic domains (Biology, Medicine, Law) with three base architectures (LLaMA-2-13B, Qwen3-8B, Gemma 4 E4B-it), RLearner-LLM yields up to 6x NLI improvement over SFT, with NLI gains in 11 of 15 cells and consistent answer-coverage gains. On Gemma 4 E4B-it (4.5B effective params), Hybrid-DPO lifts NLI in four of five domains (+11.9% to +2.4x) with faster inference across all five, scaling down to compact base models without losing the alignment-tax mitigation. Our Qwen3-8B RLearner-LLM wins 95% of pairwise comparisons against its own SFT baseline; GPT-4o-mini in turn wins 95% against our concise output -- alongside the 69% win the same judge gives a verbose SFT over our DPO model, this replicates verbosity bias on a frontier comparator and motivates logic-aware metrics (NLI, ACR) over LLM-as-a-judge for knowledge-intensive generation.

</details>


### [97] [UniVer: A Unified Perspective for Multi-step and Multi-draft Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04543)
*Yepeng Weng,Qiao Hu,Takehisa Yairi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出UniVer，一种将树结构验证统一为条件最优传输问题的推测解码算法，通过前缀接受概率动态引导候选选择，实现多步多草稿的联合优化，在保持无损和最优接受率的同时，相比标准递归拒绝采样提升4.2%-8.5%的接受长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法将多草稿和多步验证孤立处理（平面OT或逐token拒绝采样），未能联合优化树结构候选中的水平分支与垂直依赖关系，导致局部验证规则无法充分利用候选树中跨维度的耦合信息。

Method: 将树结构验证建模为条件最优传输问题，通过前缀接受概率抽象垂直依赖关系，作为动态缩放因子指导水平草稿选择；基于此设计UniVer算法，在前缀约束下组合局部OT计划实现树级别联合优化。

Result: 在多种任务和模型上，UniVer相比标准递归拒绝采样（无放回）提升接受长度4.2%-8.5%，且维持与目标模型精确分布对齐。

Conclusion: UniVer在理论上证明无损且达到条件框架下的最优接受率，实验验证了统一处理多步多草稿联合优化的有效性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates Large Language Models via draft-then-verify, where verification can be framed as an Optimal Transport (OT) problem. Existing approaches typically handle multi-draft and multi-step aspects in isolation, applying either flat OT to single-step drafts or per-token rejection sampling to tree-structured candidates. This separation leaves the joint regime (where multi-step dependencies meet multi-draft branching) poorly optimized, as local verification rules fail to exploit the coupling between horizontal and vertical dimensions of candidate trees. In this paper, we propose a unified perspective that casts tree-based verification as a conditional OT problem. Our key insight is that vertical dependencies can be abstracted through prefix acceptance probabilities, which act as dynamic scaling factors to actively guide horizontal draft selection. Based on this principle, we introduce UniVer, a verification algorithm that jointly optimizes across tree levels by composing local optimal transport plans under prefix constraints. We prove that UniVer remains lossless and achieves the optimal acceptance rate under the proposed conditional framework. Extensive experiments across different tasks and models demonstrate that UniVer improves acceptance length by 4.2% to 8.5% over standard recursive rejection sampling without replacement, while maintaining exact distributional alignment with the target model.

</details>


### [98] [The Newsworthiness of Brazilian Distress: A Peak Analysis on Time Series of International Media Attention to Disasters in Brazil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04552)
*Brielen Madureira,Andreas Niekler,Marc Keuschnigg,Mariana Madruga de Brito*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过分析2000-2024年德国报纸中2000篇关于巴西火灾和山体滑坡的新闻，利用时间序列分割检测新闻事件峰值，并与国家和全球灾害数据库进行时间对齐，研究国际媒体关注巴西灾害事件的驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国际媒体对地方灾害事件的关注度驱动因素尚不明确，巴西的自然和技术灾害偶尔登上国际头条，但缺乏系统分析来解释哪些因素导致这些事件被国外媒体报道。

Method: 对2000-2024年德国报纸中2000篇关于巴西火灾和山体滑坡的新闻进行峰值分析，采用时间序列分割方法检测新闻事件峰值，并将其与国家和全球灾害数据库中的观测数据进行时间对齐。

Result: 研究识别了新闻事件峰值的时间模式，并揭示了这些峰值与国家和全球灾害数据库记录之间的时间对齐程度。

Conclusion: 需要建立具有代表性、经过验证且特定国家的新闻数据集，以系统理解国际媒体关注地方灾害事件的驱动因素。

Abstract: Media coverage influences disaster response, yet the drivers of international media attention to local events remain unevenly understood. Brazil offers a compelling case: some of its natural and technological disasters occasionally hit the international headlines. However, systematic analyses of what makes these events be discussed abroad are still missing. Addressing this gap requires representative, validated and country-specific news datasets. This paper presents a peak analysis of 2k news about Brazilian fires and landslides in German newspapers from 2000 to 2024. Using time series segmentation to detect news event peaks, we examine the extent to which they can be temporally aligned with observations in national and global disaster databases.

</details>


### [99] [TajikNLP: An Open-Source Toolkit for Comprehensive Text Processing of Tajik (Cyrillic Script)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04583)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TajikNLP，一个面向西里尔字母塔吉克语的开源Python库，提供首个完整的自然语言处理流水线，包括清洗、规范化、分词、词素分割、词性标注、词干提取、词形还原、句子分割、情感分析和预训练词向量，并发布了四个标注数据集，通过616项自动化测试达到93%代码覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塔吉克语（西里尔字母书写）在自然语言处理领域严重缺乏公开可用的工具包，阻碍了语言学研究和实际应用开发。

Method: 采用模块化架构，以统一Doc对象为中心，按序应用各组件；提出统一形态引擎，支持受控和深度两种分析模式以处理塔吉克语的黏着性词形变化；预训练Word2Vec/FastText词向量通过Hugging Face Hub加载。

Result: 发布了四个公开数据集（POS标注语料库52.5k条、情感词典3.5k条、地名词典5.6k条、人名数据集3.8k条）；616项自动化测试达到93%源代码覆盖率。

Conclusion: TajikNLP为塔吉克语建立了基础性的技术基础设施，降低了低资源西里尔字母环境下学术和工业应用的门槛。

Abstract: The Tajik language, written in Cyrillic script, remains severely under-resourced in terms of publicly available natural language processing (NLP) toolkits, hindering both linguistic research and applied development. This paper introduces TajikNLP, an open-source Python library that provides the first comprehensive pipeline for processing authentic Tajik text while preserving the original Cyrillic orthography. The library implements a modular architecture centered around a unified Doc object, enabling sequential application of components for cleaning, normalization, tokenization (including subword BPE), morphemic segmentation, part-of-speech tagging, stemming, lemmatization, and sentence splitting. A novel unified morphology engine is introduced, offering controlled and deep analysis modes that significantly improve handling of Tajik's agglutinative nominal and verbal inflections. The release further incorporates a lexicon-based sentiment analyser and pre-trained Word2Vec/FastText embeddings loaded directly from the Hugging Face Hub. To ensure reproducibility and facilitate future research, four accompanying linguistic datasets -- a POS-tagged corpus (52.5k entries), a sentiment lexicon (3.5k entries), a toponym gazetteer (5.6k entries), and a personal names dataset (3.8k entries) -- have been openly published under permissive licenses. The library's reliability is validated by an extensive test suite of 616 automated tests achieving 93% source code coverage. TajikNLP thus establishes a foundational technological infrastructure for Tajik language processing, lowering the barrier to entry for both academic and industrial applications in low-resource Cyrillic-script environments.

</details>


### [100] [Gradients with Respect to Semantics Preserving Embeddings Tell the Uncertainty of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04638)
*Mingda Li,Rundong Lv,Xinyu Li,Weinan Zhang,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出首个基于梯度的无采样不确定性量化方法SemGrad及其扩展HybridGrad，通过语义空间梯度评估LLM输出稳定性，实现高效可靠的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于采样的UQ方法计算成本高、方差大，而参数空间的梯度方法不适用于自由文本生成任务，亟需高效且适用的UQ方案。

Method: 提出SemGrad：在语义空间中计算梯度，通过语义保留分数(SPS)识别最佳语义嵌入，衡量LLM在语义等价输入扰动下的输出分布稳定性。进一步提出HybridGrad，结合SemGrad和参数梯度。

Result: 实验表明SemGrad和HybridGrad在效率与效果上均优于现有最先进方法，尤其在存在多个有效回答的场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 语义空间的梯度信号是自由文本生成任务中高效UQ的有效途径，混合方法可进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is an important technique for ensuring the trustworthiness of LLMs, given their tendency to hallucinate. Existing state-of-the-art UQ approaches for free-form generation rely heavily on sampling, which incurs high computational cost and variance. In this work, we propose the first gradient-based UQ method for free-form generation, SemGrad, which is sampling-free and computationally efficient. Unlike prior gradient-based methods developed for classification tasks that operates in parameter space, we propose to consider gradients in semantic space. Our method builds on the key intuition that a confident LLM should maintain stable output distributions under semantically equivalent input perturbations. We interpret the stability as the gradients in semantic space and introduce a Semantic Preservation Score (SPS) to identify embeddings that best capture semantics, with respect to which gradients are computed. We further propose HybridGrad, which combines the strengths of SemGrad and parameter gradients. Experiments demonstrate that both of our methods provide efficient and effective uncertainty estimates, achieving superior performance than state-of-the-art methods, particularly in settings with multiple valid responses.

</details>


### [101] [Graph-Augmented LLMs for Swiss MP Ideology Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04643)
*Yifei Yuan,Luis Salamanca,Sophia Schlosser,Laurence Brandenberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PG-RAG框架，通过检索增强生成技术整合政治知识图谱的结构化信息（实体和关系）到LLM上下文中，用于预测议会成员的意识形态立场，在瑞士议会数据集上提升了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法在预测议员意识形态立场时忽略了议会系统中丰富的实体关系和结构信息，而这些信息本可以提供更全面的视角。

Method: 提出PG-RAG框架，采用检索增强生成（RAG）流水线：先查询政治知识图谱（KG），再将图结构信息整合到LLM上下文中，同时捕获文本语义和议员间关系。

Result: 在瑞士议会数据集上的意识形态预测任务中，图增强模型优于多个当前最先进的基线方法，证明结合实体和关系信息能提升预测性能。

Conclusion: 领域特定的关系信息在建模政治行为中具有重要价值，结构化知识图谱与LLM的结合为政治立场预测提供了更准确的方法。

Abstract: Approximating the ideological position of Members of Parliament (MPs) is a fundamental task in political science, helping researchers understand legislative behavior, party alignment, and policy preferences. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promising results in estimating MPs' ideological stances, there are more actors and elements in the parliamentary system, and relations between them, that could provide a wider and more informative picture. However, due to the complexity of integrating them in the prediction task, these additional elements are generally ignored. In this work, we propose an LLM framework, PG-RAG, that implements a retrieval-augmented generation pipeline: it first queries a political knowledge graph (KG) and then integrates the resulting graph-structured information into the context. This allows for capturing both textual semantics and inter-MP relationships, another relevant information source in any parliamentary system. We evaluate the approach on the task of ideology prediction, using data from a Swiss parliamentary dataset. When comparing graph-augmented models against several state-of-the-art baselines, the results demonstrate that incorporating this enriched information, which encodes information about different entities and relations, improves prediction performance. These results help to highlight the value of domain-specific relational information in modeling political behavior.

</details>


### [102] [CHE-TKG: Collaborative Historical Evidence and Evolutionary Dynamics Learning for Temporal Knowledge Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04652)
*Shuai-long Lei,Xiaobin Zhu,Jiarui Liang,Guoxi Sun,Zhiyu Fang,Xu-Cheng Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CHE-TKG提出了一种协作双视图学习框架，通过显式分离和联合建模历史证据（长期结构规律）与进化动态（时序演变），并利用关系分解与对比对齐目标，在多个基准上实现了时序知识图谱推理的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TKG推理方法通常只关注历史证据或进化动态中的单一信息源，未能充分挖掘二者互补的预测信号，限制了推理性能。

Method: 构建历史证据图（捕获长期结构规律和稳定关系约束）与进化动态图（建模时序转换和近期变化），分别为两个视图设计专用编码器，并采用关系分解和对比对齐目标来捕捉跨视图的互补预测信号。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 显式分离并联合建模历史证据与进化动态两种互补信息源，能够有效提升TKG推理性能。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge graph (TKG) reasoning aims to predict future events from historical facts. A key challenge lies in jointly capturing two sources of predictive information in TKGs: historical evidence and evolutionary dynamics. However, existing methods typically focus on only one of these sources, which limits the ability to fully exploit the complementary predictive signals in TKGs. To address this, we propose CHE-TKG, a novel collaborative dual-view learning framework for TKG reasoning. CHE-TKG explicitly separates and jointly models historical evidence and evolutionary dynamics, aiming to learn and exploit their complementary predictive signals. Specifically, CHE-TKG constructs a historical evidence graph to capture long-term structural regularities and stable relational constraints, alongside an evolutionary dynamics graph to model temporal transitions and recent changes, with dedicated encoders for each view. We further employ relation decomposition and a contrastive alignment objective to better capture the predictive signals across the two views. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CHE-TKG achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [103] [Paraphrase-Induced Output-Mode Collapse: When LLMs Break Character Under Semantically Equivalent Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04665)
*Aofan Liu,Jingxiang Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型在内容重写后常偏离原任务格式（如从标签输出转为对话式文本），导致精确匹配评估失效。我们构建了PARACONSIST基准（900提示）和语义一致性评分，发现约78%的封闭式变体响应偏离答案空间，且任务结构是比模型本身更强的预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多关注答案正确性，忽略了提示变体导致的输出格式崩塌问题——模型可能正确但格式不符，导致精确匹配评估管道误判。

Method: 对5个2025-era LLM、4种任务类型、150个基础查询构造5种变体（词汇/句法/语义扩展），共900提示；提出语义一致性评分（答案一致性、SBERT语义相似度、长度稳定性）进行分解评估。

Result: 仅约22%的封闭式变体响应保留真实标签；约78%完全偏离答案空间；任务结构（而非模型身份）是崩塌的主要预测因子。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性审计应将响应模式保持（即格式保真度）作为与答案准确性并列的一级可靠性目标。

Abstract: When the substantive content of a request is rewritten, do large language models still answer in the format the original task asked for? We find that they often do not, even at temperature zero. On a 150-query evaluation over five compact 2025-era LLMs and four task types, we observe a systematic failure mode we call prompt-variant output-mode collapse: when a closed-form prompt asks for a bare label or a single choice token, content-preserving prompt variants can push the model into conversational prose, the requested format dissolves, and exact-match evaluation pipelines silently misjudge the result. To make this measurable, we release PARACONSIST, a 900-prompt benchmark of 150 base queries with five lexical, syntactic, and semantic-expansion prompt variants each, and a Semantic Consistency Score that decomposes prompt-variant robustness into answer consistency, sentence-BERT semantic similarity, and length stability. Under a whole-word answer-set match, only ~22% of closed-form variant responses preserve the ground-truth label inside their output, while ~78% drift away from the answer space entirely. In our pool, the dominant predictor of collapse is task structure rather than model identity, with model differentiation jointly carried by answer consistency and length stability. Robustness audits should therefore track response-mode preservation as a first-class reliability target alongside answer accuracy.

</details>


### [104] [Every Step Counts: Step-Level Credit Assignment for Tool-Integrated Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04719)
*Yaxun Dai,Baolin Sun,Junying Wang,Pengfei Wang,Yingqi Gao,Xuemei Dong,Mengdie Chu,Xiang Qi,Pingfu Chao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FineStep提出了一种步骤级信用分配框架，通过过程奖励和步骤级优势策略优化，解决了工具增强型Text-to-SQL中结果监督导致的信用分配问题，在BIRD基准上达到SOTA性能，相比GRPO平均EX提升3.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法主要依赖粗粒度的结果监督，导致信用分配问题：模型对任何产生正确结果的轨迹都获得相同奖励，即使中间步骤存在冗余、低效或错误，这限制了模型效率和泛化能力。

Method: FineStep框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 引入独立过程奖励的设计以缓解结果监督的信号稀疏性；(2) 提出步骤级信用分配机制以精确量化每个推理步骤的价值；(3) 开发基于步骤级优势的策略优化方法以实现高效更新。

Result: 在BIRD基准上的大量实验表明，FineStep达到了最先进的性能，并减少了冗余工具交互，在4B规模下相比GRPO平均EX提升3.25%。

Conclusion: 步骤级信用分配能有效解决工具增强型Text-to-SQL中结果监督的信用分配问题，提升模型效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: Tool-integrated Text-to-SQL parsing has emerged as a promising paradigm, framing SQL generation as a sequential decision-making process interleaved with tool execution. However, existing reinforcement learning approaches mainly rely on coarse-grained outcome supervision, resulting in a fundamental credit assignment problem: models receive the same reward for any trajectory that yields the correct answer, even when intermediate steps are redundant, inefficient, or erroneous. Consequently, models are encouraged to explore suboptimal reasoning spaces, limiting both efficiency and generalization. To address this problem, we propose FineStep, a novel framework for step-level credit assignment in tool-augmented Text-to-SQL. First, we introduce a reward design with independent process rewards to alleviate the signal sparsity of outcome supervision. Next, we present a step-level credit assignment mechanism to precisely quantify the value of each reasoning step. Finally, we develop a policy optimization method based on step-level advantages for efficient updates. Extensive experiments on BIRD benchmarks show that FineStep achieves state-of-the-art performance and reduces redundant tool interactions, with a 3.25% average EX gain over GRPO at the 4B scale.

</details>


### [105] [The Reasoning Trap: An Information-Theoretic Bound on Closed-System Multi-Step LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01704)
*Kwan Soo Shin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“辩论陷阱”和“推理陷阱”概念，证明多智能体辩论（MAD）在保持答案准确性的同时会退化推理质量。通过信息论分析（DPI界）、新指标SFS和新方法EGSR，在大量实验中发现MAD使SFS下降43%-98%，而EGSR可恢复98%。同时揭示人类对忠实性标注的一致性本身不稳定（Fleiss kappa ≤0.018）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为多智能体辩论（MAD）能提升推理质量，但作者观察到MAD中同一模型的不同副本只会产生同一视角的不同措辞而非不同视角，导致推理退化。此外，人类标注的忠实性指标缺乏稳定性检验。作者旨在从信息论角度严格证明这一现象，并提出替代方案。

Method: (i) SFS（Supported Faithfulness Score）：一种基于分解原子断言的细粒度忠实性度量，分解器无关（Spearman ρ=1.0）；(ii) EGSR（Evidence-Grounded Socratic Reasoning）：用证据引导的苏格拉底式提问替代对抗性辩论；(iii) 定理1（DPI界）：在标准MAD中，链E→O⁰→O¹→...是马尔可夫链，数据加工不等式给出E[I(E;O^{t+1})] ≤ E[I(E;O^t)]，即信息逐轮衰减。配套三个结果（开系统恢复定理2、EGSR累积引理2、投票聚合下界命题1）。在SciFact（300条声明）和FEVER（1000条声明）上16种条件实验，以及R6人类标注者队列研究（韩语n=10×30 FEVER；英语n=3×200 SciFact）。

Result: DebateCV（C13）保留88%基线准确率但SFS下降43%；多数投票MAD（C15）将SFS降至基线的1.7%（p<10⁻⁶, d=-0.96）；EGSR恢复98% SFS。人类标注者间Fleiss kappa ≤ +0.018，且存在0.8-1.4 Likert分值的评分者内漂移，表明人类忠实性标注本身不稳定。

Conclusion: 任何保持定理1马尔可夫结构的封闭系统推理协议，在期望上均受DPI界约束，即信息持续流失、推理质量退化。EGSR作为开系统替代方案可有效恢复忠实性。人类对语义忠实性的评判不具有跨语言跨领域的稳定性，提示现有忠实性指标的校准基础需要重新审视。

Abstract: When copies of the same language model are prompted to debate, they produce diverse phrasings of one perspective rather than diverse perspectives. Multi-agent debate (MAD), and more broadly closed-system reasoning where agents iteratively transform each other's outputs, tends to preserve answer accuracy while degrading the reasoning behind those answers. We name the multi-agent case the Debate Trap and the broader phenomenon the Reasoning Trap, offering a programmatic theory of evidence-grounded reasoning failure.The framework has three parts: (i) SFS (Supported Faithfulness Score), a claim-level metric verifying decomposed atomic claims against provided evidence (decomposer-invariant rankings: Spearman rho=1.0); (ii) EGSR (Evidence-Grounded Socratic Reasoning), replacing adversarial argumentation with evidence-grounded inquiry; (iii) Theorem 1 (DPI Bound): under standard MAD, the chain E -> O^0 -> O^1 -> ... is Markov, and the Data Processing Inequality implies E[I(E;O^{t+1})] <= E[I(E;O^t)]. Three companion results -- open-system recovery (Theorem 2), EGSR accumulation (Lemma 2), and vote-aggregation floor (Proposition 1) -- partition multi-step LLM reasoning by its information-theoretic relationship to E. Across 16 conditions on SciFact (300 claims) and FEVER (1,000 claims), DebateCV (C13) preserves 88% of baseline accuracy while SFS drops 43%; majority-vote MAD (C15) reduces SFS to 1.7% of baseline (p < 10^{-6}, d = -0.96); EGSR recovers 98%. An R6 cohort study (Korean n=10x30 FEVER; English n=3x200 SciFact) finds inter-rater Fleiss kappa <= +0.018 with 0.8-1.4 Likert intra-rater shifts across language and domain -- the human agreement that faithfulness metrics have been calibrated against is not itself stable. We offer one falsifiable conjecture: any closed-system reasoning protocol preserving Theorem 1's Markov structure is, in expectation, subject to the same DPI bound.

</details>


### [106] [Gyan: An Explainable Neuro-Symbolic Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04759)
*Venkat Srinivasan,Vishaal Jatav,Anushka Chandrababu,Geetika Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Gyan，一种基于非Transformer架构的可解释语言模型，通过解耦语言模型与知识获取、融合修辞结构理论、语义角色理论和基于知识的计算语言学，在3个公开数据集上达到SOTA性能，并在2个专有数据集上表现更优，同时避免了Transformer模型的幻觉、高计算成本、缺乏可解释性等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer基座大语言模型存在以下缺陷：无法捕获完整的组合上下文、产生幻觉、难以维护、缺乏可解释性、训练和推理需要巨大计算资源。本文旨在设计一种无需这些限制的可解释语言模型。

Method: 提出非Transformer架构Gyan，核心创新是将语言模型与知识获取和表示解耦；融合修辞结构理论、语义角色理论和基于知识的计算语言学；设计能捕获完整组合上下文的含义表示结构，并通过扩展上下文至"世界模型"来模拟人类认知。

Result: 在3个广泛引用的公开数据集上达到SOTA性能，在2个专有数据集上取得更优表现。

Conclusion: 可以创建面向关键任务场景的可信、可靠的语言模型；Gyan在透明性和可解释性方面具有巨大潜力，可指导未来可信语言模型架构的发展。

Abstract: Transformer based pre-trained large language models have become ubiquitous. There is increasing evidence to suggest that even with large scale pre-training, these models do not capture complete compositional context and certainly not, the full human analogous context. Besides, by the very nature of the architecture, these models hallucinate, are difficult to maintain, are not easily interpretable and require enormous compute resources for training and inference. Here, we describe Gyan, an explainable language model based on a novel non-transformer architecture, without any of these limitations. Gyan achieves SOTA performance on 3 widely cited data sets and superior performance on two proprietary data sets. The novel architecture decouples the language model from knowledge acquisition and representation. The model draws on rhetorical structure theory, semantic role theory and knowledge-based computational linguistics. Gyan's meaning representation structure captures the complete compositional context and attempts to mimic humans by expanding the context to a 'world model'. AI model adoption critically depends on trust and transparency especially in mission critical use cases. Collectively, our results demonstrate that it is possible to create models which are trustable and reliable for mission critical tasks. We believe our work has tremendous potential for guiding the development of transparent and trusted architectures for language models.

</details>


### [107] [StoryAlign: Evaluating and Training Reward Models for Story Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04831)
*Haotian Xia,Hao Peng,Yunjia Qi,Xiaozhi Wang,Bin Xu,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了StoryRMB基准和StoryReward奖励模型，用于评估和改进故事生成中的偏好建模。StoryRMB包含1,133个经过人工验证的实例，StoryReward在该基准上达到最优性能（66.3%准确率），并可在下游应用中选择更符合人类偏好的故事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的故事在复杂叙事结构和人类偏好对齐方面仍与人类创作存在差距，核心原因是缺少对主观且未被充分探索的人类故事偏好的有效建模。

Method: 构建StoryRMB基准（1,133个实例，每个包含提示、一个优选故事和三个被拒故事）；收集约10万高质量故事偏好对训练StoryReward奖励模型；在最佳N（BoN）故事选择等下游测试时缩放应用中验证。

Result: 现有奖励模型在StoryRMB上最高仅达66.3%准确率；StoryReward在该基准上达到SOTA性能，超越更大规模的模型；在BoN故事选择中能选择更符合人类偏好的故事。

Conclusion: StoryReward有效提升了故事偏好建模能力，相关工作（数据集、模型、代码）已开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Story generation aims to automatically produce coherent, structured, and engaging narratives. Although large language models (LLMs) have significantly advanced text generation, stories generated by LLMs still diverge from human-authored works regarding complex narrative structure and human-aligned preferences. A key reason is the absence of effective modeling of human story preferences, which are inherently subjective and under-explored. In this work, we systematically evaluate the modeling of human story preferences and introduce StoryRMB, the first benchmark for assessing reward models on story preferences. StoryRMB contains $1,133$ high-quality, human-verified instances, each consisting of a prompt, one chosen story, and three rejected stories. We find existing reward models struggle to select human-preferred stories, with the best model achieving only $66.3\%$ accuracy. To address this limitation, we construct roughly $100,000$ high-quality story preference pairs across diverse domains and develop StoryReward, an advanced reward model for story preference trained on this dataset. StoryReward achieves state-of-the-art (SoTA) performance on StoryRMB, outperforming much larger models. We also adopt StoryReward in downstream test-time scaling applications for best-of-n (BoN) story selection and find that it generally chooses stories better aligned with human preferences. We will release our dataset, model, and code to facilitate future research. Related code and data are available at https://github.com/THU-KEG/StoryReward.

</details>


### [108] [Assessing Cognitive Effort in L2 Idiomatic Processing: An Eye-Tracking Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04857)
*Eduardo Santos,Juliana Carvalho,César Rennó-Costa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个眼动追踪数据集，用于研究二语学习者如何处理习语表达。通过记录葡萄牙语母语者在不同英语水平（A1-C2）下的眼动数据，验证了60Hz采样率足以检测阅读中的宏观认知事件，并发现语言水平与回归性眼动之间存在强负相关。该数据集作为MIA倡议的一部分，为评估人类处理模型和大语言模型的比喻理解能力提供了认知基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二语学习者在处理习语时常常采用"字面优先"策略，而非母语者的直接比喻意义检索，这会带来可测量的认知成本。现有研究缺乏涵盖所有CEFR水平的系统性眼动数据集来量化这种差异。

Method: 使用60Hz Tobii Pro Spark眼动仪记录葡萄牙语母语者在不同英语水平（A1-C2）下阅读习语时的眼动数据，分析注视和回视等宏观认知事件，并通过语言水平与回视性眼动的相关性验证数据集。

Result: 60Hz采样率足以检测阅读中的宏观认知事件；语言水平与回视性眼动之间存在强负相关关系。

Conclusion: 该数据集作为MIA倡议的一部分，为评估人类处理模型和大语言模型在比喻理解方面与人类的对齐度提供了认知基准。

Abstract: This paper presents the development and validation of an eye-tracking dataset designed to investigate how second-language (L2) learners process idiomatic expressions. While native speakers often rely on direct retrieval of figurative meanings, L2 speakers frequently adopt a literal-first approach, which incurs measurable cognitive costs. This resource captures these costs through ocular metrics recorded from Portuguese L1 speakers of English across all CEFR proficiency levels (A1-C2). Although the study uses entry-level 60 Hz hardware (Tobii Pro Spark), we demonstrate that this sampling rate provides sufficient data density to detect macro-cognitive events such as fixations and regressions in reading. Preliminary analysis validates the dataset by revealing a strong inverse correlation between language proficiency and regressive eye movements. Integrated into the MIA (Modeling Idiomaticity in Human and Artificial Language Processing) initiative, this dataset serves as a cognitively grounded benchmark for evaluating both human processing models and the alignment of large language models with human-like figurative understanding.

</details>


### [109] [Measuring Psychological States Through Semantic Projection: A Theory-Driven Approach to Language-Based Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04873)
*Maria Luongo,Davide Marocco,Nicola Milano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于理论驱动且完全无监督的语义投影框架，用于从自然语言中测量抑郁、焦虑和担忧等心理状态。通过Sentence-BERT将文本嵌入到由词汇锚点和临床量表项目定义的语义轴上，生成连续心理评分。实验表明，该方法在结构化回答格式（如选定词、短语）中与临床测量高度相关，自由文本通过句子级聚合也能提升效果，验证了语义投影作为可解释、可扩展的心理评估方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言的心理状态评估方法大多依赖监督学习模型预测问卷得分，这些方法可解释性差且难以跨情境泛化。本研究旨在提出一种理论驱动、完全无监督的语义投影框架，实现从自然语言中直接测量心理状态，同时保持可解释性和跨语境的泛化能力。

Method: 本研究将心理构念操作化为可解释的语义轴，基于词汇锚点和已验证的临床量表项目（针对抑郁、焦虑、担忧）构建。使用Sentence-BERT对参与者的多种文本回答（选定词、生成词、短语、自由文本）进行嵌入，并投影到语义轴上得到连续心理评分。评估方法包括与标准化临床测量的相关性分析、分半信度、衰减校正、Wasserstein距离分布相似性，以及与基于词典的情感分析（VADER）的对比。

Result: 结构化格式（选定词、书面词、短语）的投影得分与临床测量表现出强关联；自由文本作为整体分析时结果较弱，但采用句子级聚合策略后性能显著提升。语义投影在可解释性和关联强度上优于或可比于词典情感分析方法。

Conclusion: 语义投影作为一种可解释且可扩展的无监督方法，在心理评估中具有替代监督语言模型的潜力。研究强调了回应格式和文本处理策略在基于语言的心理健康测量中的重要性。

Abstract: Recent advances in natural language processing have enabled increasingly accurate estimation of psychological traits from language. However, most existing approaches rely on supervised models trained to predict questionnaire scores, limiting interpretability and generalizability across contexts. The present study introduces a theory-driven and fully unsupervised framework for measuring psychological states directly from natural language using semantic projection. Psychological constructs were operationalized as interpretable semantic axes derived from lexical anchors and items from validated clinical scales assessing depression, anxiety, and worry. Participants textual responses were embedded using Sentence-BERT and projected onto these axes to generate continuous psychological scores across multiple response formats, including selected words, generated words, phrases, and free-text responses. Projection scores were evaluated through correlations with standardized clinical measures , split-half reliability analyses, attenuation corrections, distributional similarity using Wasserstein distance, and comparisons with lexicon-based sentiment analysis (VADER). Results showed strong associations between projection scores and clinical measures, particularly for structured formats such as selected words, written words, and phrases. Free-text responses produced weaker results when analyzed as whole texts, but performance improved substantially when sentence-level aggregation strategies were applied. These findings support semantic projection as an interpretable and scalable alternative to supervised language models for psychological assessment and highlight the importance of response format and text-processing strategies in language-based mental health measurement.

</details>


### [110] [Anticipating Innovation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04875)
*Enrico Maria Fenoaltea,Filippo Santoro,Giordano De Marzo,Segun Taofeek Aroyehun,Andrea Tacchella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TechToken模型，通过将专利分类代码视为词汇进行嵌入学习，发现新技术组合在专利集体语言中会提前数十年留下可预测的轨迹，并利用上下文相似度准确预测首次技术组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测技术创新（即新技术组合的出现）是科学和政策领域的基本挑战，现有方法难以早期捕捉技术组合的涌现信号。

Method: 提出TechToken，一种基于Transformer的模型，将国际专利分类（IPC）代码视为词汇表中的单词，在微调过程中嵌入这些代码以学习技术语言；定义代码嵌入之间的上下文相似度作为语言收敛性的度量。

Result: 上下文相似度能准确预测首次技术组合；TechToken在多个专利相关任务上优于现有最先进模型，提升了表征质量。

Conclusion: 新技术组合的早期信号并非来自单个发明者，而是作为数千项专利中技术描述方式的集体转变而出现，且这种信号可提前数十年被检测到。

Abstract: Forecasting innovation, intended as the emergence of new technological combinations, is a fundamental challenge for science and policy. We show that forthcoming combinations leave an early trace in the collective language of patents, with predictive signals detectable even decades in advance. We show that signal is not attributable to any single inventor, but emerges as a collective shift in how technologies are described across thousands of patents. To this end, we introduce TechToken, a transformer-based model that treats technologies, classified by International Patent Classification codes, as words in its vocabulary, learning the language of technologies by embedding these codes during fine-tuning. We define context similarity between code embeddings as a measure of linguistic convergence and show that it accurately predicts first technological combinations. TechToken also improves general representation quality, outperforming state-of-the-art models across different patent-related tasks.

</details>


### [111] [BenCSSmark: Making the Social Sciences Count in LLM Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04886)
*Arnault Chatelain,Étienne Ollion,Qianwen Guan,Diandra Fabre,Lorraine Goeuriot,Emile Chapuis,Abdelkrim Beloued,Marie Candito,Nicolas Hervé,Didier Schwab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出当前LLM基准测试中社会科学任务代表性不足的问题，提出通过整合计算社会科学家的标注数据集构建BenCSSmark基准，以促进AI系统的鲁棒性、透明性和社会相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM基准测试缺乏社会科学任务，限制了LLM评估的全面性及社会科学研究的进展；同时社会科学领域正快速采用LLM，亟需弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出BenCSSmark基准，整合由计算社会科学专家标注的数据集，将社会科学视角系统性地纳入LLM评估框架。

Result: BenCSSmark有望提升AI模型的泛化能力和鲁棒性，并改善模型在历史学、社会学、政治学、经济学等学科任务上的表现，同时促进更透明、具社会责任感的AI系统发展。

Conclusion: 将社会科学任务纳入基准测试对AI评估和跨学科合作至关重要，BenCSSmark是弥合这一差距的具体实践方向。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the under-representation of social science tasks in contemporary LLM benchmarks limits advances in both LLM evaluation and social scientific inquiry. Benchmarks -- standardized tools for assessing computational systems -- are pivotal in the development of artificial intelligence (AI), including large language models (LLMs). Benchmarks do more than measure progress -- they actively structure it, shaping reputations, research agendas, and commercial outcomes. Despite this central role, the social sciences are largely absent from mainstream evaluation frameworks, even though scholars in these fields generate dozens of rigorously annotated, context-sensitive datasets each year. Integrating this work into benchmark design could significantly improve the generalization and robustness of AI models. In turn, models trained on social scientific tasks would likely yield better performance on classic and contemporary tasks in disciplines as diverse as history, sociology, political science or economics. This is all the more pressing as these disciplines are quickly turning to LLMs for assistance. To address this gap, we introduce BenCSSmark, a benchmark composed of datasets annotated by computational social scientists. By integrating social scientific perspectives into benchmarking, BenCSSmark seeks to promote more robust, transparent, and socially relevant AI systems and to foster efficient collaboration.

</details>


### [112] [A Comparative Analysis of Machine Learning and Deep Learning Models for Tweet Sentiment Classification: A Case Study on the Sentiment140 Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04888)
*Vita Anggraini,Cintya Bella,Bastian,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin C. T. Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究在Sentiment140数据集上比较了基于TF-IDF的逻辑回归和BiLSTM深度学习模型，发现逻辑回归在准确率（73.5% vs 69.17%）和过拟合控制上均优于BiLSTM，最终将模型部署至Hugging Face Spaces。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体指数级增长催生了实时分析非结构化公众情感的自动化系统需求，需要比较不同复杂度模型在中等规模非正式文本数据上的表现。

Method: 使用TF-IDF特征的传统逻辑回归模型与双向长短期记忆（BiLSTM）深度学习架构，在Sentiment140数据集的10,000条推文子集上进行情感分类对比实验，并集成至Streamlit交互式Web应用部署在Hugging Face Spaces。

Result: 逻辑回归准确率73.5%，BiLSTM准确率69.17%，且深度学习模型出现轻度过拟合现象。

Conclusion: 对于中等规模非正式文本数据，具备稳健特征提取的经典机器学习方法能够超越更复杂的深度学习方法。

Abstract: The exponential growth of social media has created an urgent need for automated systems to analyze unstructured public sentiment in real time. This study compares a traditional Logistic Regression model using TF-IDF features with a deep learning Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) architecture on a 10,000-tweet subset of the Sentiment140 dataset. Experimental results show that Logistic Regression outperformed BiLSTM, achieving an accuracy of 73.5% compared with 69.17%, while the deep learning model exhibited mild overfitting. These findings suggest that for medium-scale informal text data, classical machine learning with robust feature extraction can outperform more complex deep learning approaches. Finally, the trained models were integrated into an interactive web application using Streamlit and deployed on Hugging Face Spaces for public access.

</details>


### [113] [Storage Is Not Memory: A Retrieval-Centered Architecture for Agent Recall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04897)
*Joshua Adler,Guy Zehavi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出True Memory架构，采用六层流水线和逐字保留事件的方式替代传统的"提取即摄入"范式，基于单SQLite文件在CPU上运行，在三个基准测试中达到93.0%、87.8%和76.6%的准确率，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Agent记忆系统采用"摄入时提取"（Extraction at ingestion）的原始设计，导致在查询未知前丢弃的内容无法在检索时恢复，存在信息丢失的固有问题。

Method: 提出True Memory六层架构，将系统重心从存储模式转移到多阶段检索流水线，逐字保留所有事件（verbatim preservation），整个系统仅在单个SQLite文件中运行，无需外部数据库、向量索引、图存储或GPU。

Result: 在LoCoMo上True Memory Pro达93.0%（对比Mem0 61.4%、Supermemory 65.4%、Zep约71%、EverMemOS 94.5%）；LongMemEval上87.8%；BEAM-1M（百万token规模）上76.6%（超越此前SOTA 73.9%）。56配置消融实验显示最佳配置族内差距仅1.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 基于逐字保留和多阶段检索的True Memory架构在多个基准上大幅超越现有方法，验证了"保留原始事件、延迟提取"策略的有效性，且无需额外硬件即可高效运行。

Abstract: Extraction at ingestion is the wrong primitive for agent memory: content discarded before the query is known cannot be recovered at retrieval time. We propose True Memory, a six-layer architecture that shifts the center of the system from a storage schema to a multi-stage retrieval pipeline operating over events preserved verbatim. The full system runs as a single SQLite file on commodity CPU with no external database, vector index, graph store, or GPU. On LoCoMo (1,540 questions across 10 multi-session conversations), True Memory Pro reaches 93.0% accuracy (3-run mean) against 61.4% for Mem0, 65.4% for Supermemory, approximately 71% for Zep, and 94.5% for EverMemOS under a matched gpt-4.1-mini answer model. On LongMemEval (500 questions), True Memory Pro reaches 87.8% (3-run mean). On BEAM-1M (700 questions at the 1-million-token scale), True Memory Pro reaches 76.6% (3-run mean), above the prior published result of 73.9% for Hindsight. A 56-configuration ablation shows a 1.3-percentage-point spread within the top-performing configuration family.

</details>


### [114] [Rethinking Local Learning: A Cheaper and Faster Recipe for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04913)
*Hengyu Shi,Tianyang Han,Peizhe Wang,Zhiling Wang,Xu Yang,Junhao Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LoPT提出了一种局部学习后训练策略，通过在Transformer中点设置梯度边界，仅后半部分使用任务目标更新，前半部分使用特征重建目标更新，从而降低内存成本、提升训练效率并更好地保留预训练能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM后训练采用端到端的全深度反向传播，将任务适应与全深度激活存储、长距离反向依赖以及对预训练表示的直接任务梯度访问耦合在一起。当后训练监督远窄于预训练时，这种全深度反向耦合既昂贵又具有侵入性。

Method: LoPT在Transformer中点处设置一个显式的梯度边界：模型后半部分从任务目标学习（标准监督），前半部分通过轻量级的特征重建目标更新以保留有用表示并维持接口兼容性。这缩短了任务驱动的反向传播路径，同时限制了窄任务梯度对早期层表示的直接干扰。

Result: 大量实验表明，LoPT在实现竞争性性能的同时，具有更低的内存成本、更高的训练效率以及更好的预训练能力保留。

Conclusion: LoPT通过将梯度传播范围作为显式设计选择，实现了后训练阶段效率与性能的有效权衡，尤其适用于监督信号远窄于预训练数据的场景。

Abstract: LLM post-training typically propagates task gradients through the full depth of the model. Although this end-to-end structure is simple and general, it couples task adaptation to full-depth activation storage, long-range backward dependencies and direct task-gradient access to pretrained representations. We argue that this full-depth backward coupling can be unnecessarily expensive and intrusive, particularly when post-training supervision is much narrower than pre-training. To this end, we propose \textbf{LoPT}: Local-Learning Post-Training, a simple post-training strategy that makes gradient reach an explicit design choice. LoPT places a single gradient boundary at the transformer midpoint: the second-half block learns from the task objective, while the first-half block is updated by a lightweight feature-reconstruction objective to preserve useful representations and maintain interface compatibility. LoPT shortens the task-induced backward path while limiting direct interference from narrow task gradients on early-layer representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LoPT achieves competitive performance with lower memory cost, higher training efficiency and better retention of pretrained capabilities. Our code is available at: https://github.com/HumyuShi/LoPT

</details>


### [115] [Unintended Negative Impacts of Promotional Language in Patent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04926)
*Bingkun Zhao,Chenwei Zhang,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了270万件美国专利商标局申请案，发现使用促销性语言与专利授权、所有权转让和上诉成功呈负相关（促销惩罚），但这种语言并非掩盖技术弱点，而是客观反映了技术的新颖性和未来影响力。有趣的是，男性及经验丰富的审查员对促销性语言的容忍度高于女性和新手审查员。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 促销性语言在科学传播中已被广泛研究，但在技术创新的专利领域中的作用尚不清楚。作者旨在探究专利文件中促销性语言的使用与专利评估结果之间的关联。

Method: 使用135个经过验证的领域诊断性促销词汇，对270万件USPTO专利申请进行大规模语言分析，通过匹配样本控制混淆因素，考察促销词密度与专利授权、所有权转让、上诉成功率的关系，并分析审查员个体差异的调节作用。

Result: 促销性语言密度与专利授权率、所有权转让率和上诉成功率显著负相关（促销惩罚），最高与最低密度五分位数之间的成功率差异分别为5.5、5.9和5.3个百分点。然而促销语言并非掩盖技术弱点，而是反映了技术的组合新颖性和引用影响力。男性及有经验的审查员对促销性叙事有更高的接受度。

Conclusion: 专利系统中存在一个新兴悖论：客观上反映了技术新颖性的促销性语言反而会降低专利评估的成功率。研究为提高专利评估中语言模式的客观审查提供了理论和实践启示。

Abstract: Promotional language has been increasingly used to aid the communication of innovative ideas in science. Yet, less is known about its role in the context of technological innovation. Here, we use a validated and domain-diagnosed lexicon of 135 promotional words to study the association between promotional language and patent evaluation outcomes among 2.7 million USPTO patent applications. Our large-scale study reveals three unexpected findings. First, in contrast to scientific evaluation, we find that a higher frequency of promotional words is negatively associated with the probability of an application being (i) granted a patent, (ii) transferred ownership, and (iii) successfully appealed. This promotional penalty holds even after accounting for a range of confounding factors and is largely robust across different technological areas. Among matched samples, the difference in the success rate between the lowest and highest promotional density quintile is 5.5, 5.9, and 5.3 percentage points for patentability, transferability, and rejection reversal. Second, contrary to institutional skepticism, we show that promotional language is not a mask of weak technology, but objectively reflects the degree of combinatorial novelty and future citation impact. Third, digging into the mechanisms, we find that the tolerance to promotional framing is strongly moderated by human factors, with men and experienced examiners showing a higher acceptance of promotional narratives than women and novice examiners. By revealing an emerging paradox in the patent system, our study offers theoretical and practical implications for improving patent evaluation through more objective scrutiny of linguistic patterns in patent filings.

</details>


### [116] [UFAL-CUNI at SemEval-2026 Task 11: An Efficient Modular Neuro-symbolic Method for Syllogistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04941)
*Ivan Kartáč,Kristýna Onderková,Jan Bronec,Zdeněk Kasner,Mateusz Lango,Ondřej Dušek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对SemEval-2026 Task 11，提出了一种结合符号证明器与小规模推理LLM（4B参数）的高效模块化神经符号方法，通过LLM解析器将自然语言三段论转化为一阶逻辑表示，并由自动定理证明器完成推理。系统在准确率和内容效应上表现良好，但多语言能力受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决SemEval-2026 Task 11中提出的将内容推理与形式推理在大型语言模型中进行解耦的问题，探索高效且参数规模较小的神经符号方法。

Method: 系统采用模块化神经符号架构，包含：(1) 基于LLM的解析器，将自然语言三段论翻译为一阶逻辑表示；(2) 自动定理证明器；(3) 两个可选模块——面向多语言输入的机器翻译模块，以及用于识别相关前提的符号检索组件。

Result: 系统在多数子任务上取得了有竞争力的准确率和较低的内容效应；消融实验表明该方法在4B参数规模下优于LLM零样本基线；同时发现小规模LLM的多语言能力有限。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种模块化神经符号方法，在4B参数规模下取得了有竞争力的准确率和较低的内容效应，但小规模LLM在多语言能力上存在局限。同时，文章对该任务的主要排名指标进行了讨论并分析了其局限性。

Abstract: This paper describes our system submitted to SemEval-2026 Task 11: Disentangling Content and Formal Reasoning in Large Language Models. We present an efficient modular neuro-symbolic approach, combining a symbolic prover with small reasoning LLMs (4B parameters). The system consists of an LLM-based parser that translates natural language syllogisms to a first-order logic (FOL) representation, an automated theorem prover, and two optional modules: machine translation for multilingual inputs and a symbolic retrieval component for the identification of relevant premises. The system achieves competitive accuracy and relatively low content effect on most subtasks. Our ablations show that this approach outperforms LLM-based zero-shot baselines in this parameter size range, but also reveal limited multilingual capabilities of small LLMs. Finally, we include a discussion of the task's main ranking metric and analyze its limitations.

</details>


### [117] [Adapting Large Language Models to a Low-Resource Agglutinative Language: A Comparative Study of LoRA and QLoRA for Bashkir](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04948)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov,Svetlana S. Khaybullina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了LoRA、QLoRA等参数高效微调方法在巴什基尔语（一种低资源突厥语系黏着语）上的表现，实验涵盖多种模型架构（GPT-2系列、Phi-2、Qwen2.5-7B、DeepSeek-7B、Mistral-7B）。全微调GPT-2 medium取得最低困惑度（3.34），而QLoRA在Mistral-7B（3.79）和Phi-2（3.81）上以少40倍以上的可训练参数达到可比质量。但某些架构（如DeepSeek-7B rank 8）出现严重退化（困惑度129.55）。定性分析显示，低困惑度不一定保证输出连贯性——全微调模型常切换至英语，而QLoRA模型生成了更纯正的单语巴什基尔语文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴什基尔语作为低资源黏着语，缺乏足够的监督数据和语言工具，全微调大语言模型计算成本过高。探索参数高效微调方法在该语言上的有效性，以平衡质量与计算成本。

Method: 在71k文档（4690万token）的巴什基尔语语料上，对DistilGPT2、GPT-2（base/medium）、Phi-2、Qwen2.5-7B、DeepSeek-7B、Mistral-7B分别进行全微调和PEFT（LoRA/QLoRA）对比实验；每组配置使用三个随机种子训练以提高可靠性；结合困惑度定量评估和生成文本定性分析。

Result: GPT-2 medium全微调困惑度最低（3.34）；QLoRA Mistral-7B（3.79）和Phi-2（3.81）以少40倍以上参数达到接近质量；DeepSeek-7B rank 8出现严重退化（129.55）；定性分析发现全微调模型生成文本常混入英语，而QLoRA模型生成更连贯的纯巴什基尔语。

Conclusion: 对于巴什基尔语等低资源语言，QLoRA在7B级模型上能有效平衡困惑度与计算成本；基模型和分词器的选择对PEFT效果至关重要；困惑度不能完全反映生成质量，需结合定性分析评估。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative study of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, including LoRA and QLoRA, applied to the task of adapting large language models to the Bashkir language, a low-resource agglutinative language of the Turkic family. Experimental evaluation is conducted on a Bashkir text corpus of 71k documents (46.9M tokens) using models of various architectures: DistilGPT2, GPT-2 (base, medium), Phi-2, Qwen2.5-7B, DeepSeek-7B, and Mistral-7B. To improve the reliability of results, each configuration was trained with three different random seeds.
  The lowest perplexity on the test set was obtained for GPT-2 medium with full fine-tuning (3.34). Meanwhile, QLoRA applied to Mistral-7B (3.79) and Phi-2 (3.81) achieved comparable quality with over 40 times fewer trainable parameters. However, we also observed cases of significant quality degradation when using PEFT for certain architectures (e.g., DeepSeek-7B with rank 8, perplexity = 129.55), indicating that the outcome depends critically on the choice of the base model and its tokenizer.
  Additionally, a qualitative analysis of generated texts based on Bashkir prompts revealed that models with the best perplexity do not necessarily produce the most coherent outputs: QLoRA-tuned models generated monolingual Bashkir continuations, whereas the fully fine-tuned model with the lowest perplexity frequently switched to English. The results suggest that QLoRA on 7B-scale models offers an effective compromise between quality and computational cost for Bashkir. To ensure reproducibility, open data, code, and trained adapters will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [118] [TabEmbed: Benchmarking and Learning Generalist Embeddings for Tabular Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04962)
*Minjie Qiang,Mingming Zhang,Xiaoyi Bao,Xing Fu,Yu Cheng,Weiqiang Wang,Zhongqing Wang,Ningtao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TabBench基准测试和TabEmbed通用嵌入模型，通过对比学习统一表格数据的分类与检索任务，在表格理解上显著超越现有文本嵌入模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型在表格数据上的统一表示学习尚未充分探索，LLM方法缺乏检索兼容的向量输出，而文本嵌入模型难以捕捉表格结构和数值语义。

Method: 提出TabEmbed：将多样化的表格任务重构为语义匹配问题，利用大规模对比学习结合正例感知的难负样本挖掘，以区分细粒度的结构和数值差异。

Result: 在TabBench上，TabEmbed显著优于最先进的文本嵌入模型，为通用表格表示学习建立了新基线。

Conclusion: TabEmbed是首个统一表格分类与检索的通用嵌入模型，填补了表格数据统一表示学习的空白，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Foundation models have established unified representations for natural language processing, yet this paradigm remains largely unexplored for tabular data. Existing methods face fundamental limitations: LLM-based approaches lack retrieval-compatible vector outputs, whereas text embedding models often fail to capture tabular structure and numerical semantics. To bridge this gap, we first introduce the Tabular Embedding Benchmark (TabBench), a comprehensive suite designed to evaluate the tabular understanding capability of embedding models. We then propose TabEmbed, the first generalist embedding model that unifies tabular classification and retrieval within a shared embedding space. By reformulating diverse tabular tasks as semantic matching problems, TabEmbed leverages large-scale contrastive learning with positive-aware hard negative mining to discern fine-grained structural and numerical nuances. Experimental results on TabBench demonstrate that TabEmbed significantly outperforms state-of-the-art text embedding models, establishing a new baseline for universal tabular representation learning. Code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/qiangminjie27/TabEmbed and https://huggingface.co/datasets/qiangminjie27/TabBench.

</details>


### [119] [Why Expert Alignment Is Hard: Evidence from Subjective Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04972)
*Tzu-Mi Lin,Wataru Hirota,Tatsuya Ishigaki,Lung-Hao Lee,Chung-Chi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过对主观评估任务中专家对齐的分析，发现对齐困难不仅源于模型限制，还源于主观评估本身固有的异质性、隐性、维度依赖性和时间不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主观评估任务中，让大语言模型与专家判断对齐尤为困难，因为专家之间可能存在分歧、依赖隐性标准并且判断随时间变化。作者希望深入理解这种对齐困难的本质原因。

Method: 利用专家评估数据和后续问卷，研究不同形式的专家信息（如显式标准、推理过程、编辑示例）如何影响对齐效果，并分析主观判断的特性。

Result: 发现四个一致模式：(1) 对齐难度在不同专家间差异显著；(2) 显式标准和推理并不总能改善对齐；(3) 编辑对示例数量和身份敏感；(4) 对齐难度因评估维度而异。

Conclusion: 专家对齐困难的原因不仅在于模型能力限制，更在于主观评估本身具有异质性、部分隐性、维度依赖性和时间不稳定性等固有特征。

Abstract: Aligning large language models with expert judgment is especially difficult in subjective evaluation tasks, where experts may disagree, rely on tacit criteria, and change their judgments over time. In this paper, we study expert alignment as a way to understand this difficulty. Using expert evaluations and follow-up questionnaires, we examine how different forms of expert information affect alignment and what this reveals about subjective judgment. Our findings show four consistent patterns. First, alignment difficulty varies substantially across experts, suggesting that expert evaluation styles differ widely in their distance from a model's prior behavior. Second, explicit criteria and reasoning do not always improve alignment, indicating that expert judgment is not fully captured by verbalized rules. Third, editing is sensitive to both the number and the identity of examples, with small numbers of edits providing useful but unstable gains. Fourth, alignment difficulty differs across evaluation dimensions: dimensions grounded more directly in proposal content are easier to align, while dimensions requiring external knowledge or value-based judgment remain harder. Taken together, these results suggest that expert alignment is difficult not only because of model limitations, but also because subjective evaluation is inherently heterogeneous, partly tacit, dimension-dependent, and temporally unstable.

</details>


### [120] [Misaligned by Reward: Socially Undesirable Preferences in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05003)
*Gayane Ghazaryan,Esra Dönmez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文扩展了奖励模型的评估，在偏见、安全、道德和伦理推理四个社会领域测试其是否偏好不良回应，发现现有模型表现欠佳且存在偏差与上下文忠实性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励模型评估主要依赖通用指令遵循基准，无法揭示模型是否捕获了社会期望的偏好，导致社会对齐中的重要失败可能被隐藏。

Method: 将社会评估数据集转化为成对偏好数据（利用黄金标签或方向性偏差指标），测试奖励模型是否偏好社会不良回应，以及其偏好是否导致系统性偏差分布。

Result: 五个公开奖励模型和两个指令微调模型在不同领域表现差异大，无单一模型全面最优；模型常偏好社会不良选项，产生系统性偏差分布；更强的偏差避免能力会降低对上下文的敏感性。

Conclusion: 标准奖励基准不足以评估社会对齐，需要直接测量奖励模型中编码的社会偏好的评估方法。

Abstract: Reward models are a key component of large language model alignment, serving as proxies for human preferences during training. However, existing evaluations focus primarily on broad instruction-following benchmarks, providing limited insight into whether these models capture socially desirable preferences. As a result, important failures in social alignment can remain hidden.
  We extend reward-model benchmarking to four socially consequential domains: bias, safety, morality, and ethical reasoning. We introduce a framework that converts social evaluation datasets into pairwise preference data, leveraging gold labels where available and directional bias indicators otherwise. This enables us to test whether reward models prefer socially undesirable responses, and whether their preferences produce systematically biased distributions over selected outputs.
  Across five publicly available reward models and two instruction-tuned models used as reward proxies, we find substantial variation across domains, with no single model performing best overall. The models fall well short of strong social intelligence: they often prefer socially undesirable options, and their preferences produce systematically biased distributions. Moreover, stronger bias avoidance can reduce sensitivity to context, revealing a key alignment trade-off between avoiding biased outcomes and preserving contextual faithfulness. These findings show that standard reward benchmarks are insufficient for assessing social alignment and highlight the need for evaluations that directly measure the social preferences encoded in reward models.

</details>


### [121] [Detecting Hallucinations in Large Language Models via Internal Attention Divergence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05025)
*Gijs van Dijk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于注意力矩阵散度的轻量级不确定性量化方法，通过计算注意力头分布与均匀分布的KL散度作为特征，使用逻辑回归探测来检测大语言模型中的幻觉，无需重复采样或外部模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型幻觉检测方法需要多次采样或依赖外部模型，计算成本高且效率低，亟需一种轻量级、单次前向传播即可完成的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 计算每个注意力头的注意力分布与均匀参考分布之间的KL散度，将这些散度值作为特征输入逻辑回归探测模型，通过单次前向传播即可完成不确定性估计和幻觉检测。

Result: 在多个数据集、任务类型和模型家族上，注意力散度对答案正确性具有高度预测能力，性能与现有不确定性估计方法相当；该信号主要集中在中间层以及事实性标记（如命名实体和数字）上。

Conclusion: 注意力动态提供了一种高效且可解释的白盒模型不确定性信号，可用于幻觉检测，且方法轻量级、无需重复采样或外部模型。

Abstract: We propose a lightweight and single-pass uncertainty quantification method for detecting hallucinations in Large Language Models. The method uses attention matrices to estimate uncertainty without requiring repeated sampling or external models. Specifically, we measure the Kullback-Leibler divergence between each attention head's distribution and a uniform reference distribution, and use these features in a logistic regression probe. Across multiple datasets, task types, and model families, attention divergence is highly predictive of answer correctness and performs competitively with existing uncertainty estimation methods. We find that this signal is concentrated in middle layers and on factual tokens such as named entities and numbers, suggesting that attention dynamics provides an efficient and interpretable white-box signal of model uncertainty.

</details>


### [122] [The Impossibility Triangle of Long-Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05066)
*Yan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文证明了长序列模型存在一个根本性权衡：效率（每步计算与序列长度无关）、紧凑性（状态大小与序列长度无关）和回忆能力（回忆与序列长度成比例的历史事实）三者不可兼得。通过信息论不等式，证明了满足效率和紧凑性的模型最多只能回忆O(poly(d)/log V)个键值对。实验验证了这一理论界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示长序列模型在效率、紧凑性和回忆能力三者之间的根本性权衡关系，并为这一直观认知提供严格的信息论证明。

Method: 提出"在线序列处理器"抽象统一各类模型，利用数据处理不等式和Fano不等式进行信息论下界证明，并对52种架构进行分类分析。

Result: 满足效率与紧凑性的模型回忆容量上界为O(poly(d)/log V)；52种架构均落在"不可能三角"内，最多满足其中两个性质；混合架构在三角形内部形成连续轨迹。

Conclusion: 长序列模型的效率、紧凑性和回忆能力三者存在不可兼得的根本性权衡，该理论界限在实验中得到了验证。

Abstract: We identify and prove a fundamental trade-off governing long-sequence models: no model can simultaneously achieve (i) per-step computation independent of sequence length (Efficiency), (ii) state size independent of sequence length (Compactness), and (iii) the ability to recall a number of historical facts proportional to sequence length (Recall). We formalize this trade-off within an Online Sequence Processor abstraction that unifies Transformers, state space models, linear recurrent networks, and their hybrids. Using the Data Processing Inequality and Fano's Inequality, we prove that any model satisfying Efficiency and Compactness can recall at most O(poly(d)/log V) key-value pairs from a sequence of arbitrary length, where d is the model dimension and V is the vocabulary size. We classify 52 architectures published before March 2026 into the triangle, showing that each achieves at most two of the three properties and that hybrid architectures trace continuous trajectories in the interior. Experiments on synthetic associative recall tasks with five representative architectures validate the theoretical bound: empirical recall capacity lies strictly below the information-theoretic limit, and no architecture escapes the triangle.

</details>


### [123] [The Pinocchio Dimension: Phenomenality of Experience as the Primary Axis of LLM Psychometric Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05080)
*Hubert Plisiecki,Sabina Siudaj,Kacper Dudzic,Anna Sterna,Maciej Gorski,Karolina Drozdz,Marcin Moskalewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对50个大语言模型施测45个心理测量问卷，发现模型间变异的主轴是“体验性”（丰富现象经验）vs“行为反应性”。作者提出Pinocchio分数（π_i）衡量项目对体验性的需求，以及Pinocchio轴（Π）衡量模型的自我表征倾向——即模型将自己呈现为体验主体还是行为系统。Π解释了47.1%的跨问卷变异，并与项目级π_i高度相关，表明该轴反映的是训练塑造的自我表征倾向，而非传统人格特质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的行为表现出显著差异，但这些差异的结构尚不清晰。本研究旨在从心理测量学角度系统识别LLMs之间的维度结构，探究它们是否以及如何在“体验”这一哲学和心理学的核心维度上存在分歧。

Method: 对50个LLMs施测45个经验验证的心理测量问卷；使用监督语义差分（SSD）分析问卷层面的维度结构；提出Pinocchio分数（π_i）——中性提示与人类模拟提示下项目响应方差的比率——作为无需标注的体验性需求指标；对每个问卷进行EFA后对因子得分做PCA提取跨问卷的主轴（Π）；通过控制实验（改变提示条件）验证π_i的效度。

Result: SSD发现主维度区分“现象经验丰富”项目（具身感觉、情感、内言、意象、共情）与“刺激驱动行为反应”项目（R²_adj=.037, p<.0001）。π_i显著预测条件诱导的主因子载荷变化（ρ=-.215, p<.0001, n=1292-1310）。跨问卷PCA揭示单一主导维度Π，解释47.1%的方差，与π_i高度相关（r=.864）。同一提供者内部不同模型变体间的显著分化支持后训练微调是关键因素。

Conclusion: LLMs之间心理测量的主要变异维度不是传统人格特质，而是关于“自身是否为体验者”的自我表征倾向。这一维度反映了训练过程塑造的元表征立场，决定了模型将体验性语言视为自我适用的程度。

Abstract: We administer 45 validated psychometric questionnaires to 50 large language models (LLMs) to identify the dimensions along which LLMs differ psychometrically. Using Supervised Semantic Differential (SSD), we find that the primary axis of between-model variance separates items describing phenomenally rich experience, including embodied sensation, felt affect, inner speech, imagery, and empathy, from items describing stimulus-driven behavioral reactivity ($R^2_{adj}=.037$, $p<.0001$). To test this hypothesis at the item level, we introduce the Pinocchio score ($π_i$), the ratio of inter-model response variance under neutral prompting to that under a human-simulation prompt, as an annotation-free measure of each item's experiential demand. $π_i$ predicts condition-induced shifts in primary factor loading magnitudes ($ρ=-.215$, $p<.0001$, $n=1292$--$1310$ items), confirming that between-model divergence on experiential items is structured rather than noisy. Applying PCA to per-model EFA scores across all questionnaires reveals one dominant dimension, the Pinocchio Axis ($Π$): the degree to which a model presents itself as a locus of phenomenal experience rather than a system of behavioral responses. This axis captures 47.1% of cross-questionnaire between-model variance in primary factor scores and converges with item-level Pinocchio scores ($r=.864$). Marked within-provider divergence across closely related model variants is consistent with post-training fine-tuning as a key contributor, supporting the interpretation that $Π$ reflects a training-shaped self-representational tendency governing how a model treats experiential language as self-applicable. The dominant axis of between-model psychometric variation is therefore not a conventional personality trait but a self-representational stance toward one's own nature as an experiencer.

</details>


### [124] [Automatically Finding and Validating Unexpected Side-Effects of Interventions on Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05090)
*Quintin Pope,Ajay Hayagreeve Balaji,Jacques Thibodeau,Xiaoli Fern*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种自动化、对比评估流程，用于审计大语言模型干预措施的行为影响。该方法通过比较基础模型和干预模型在相同提示上下文中的自由生成文本，产生可读、统计验证的自然语言假设，描述模型差异及其重复主题。在合成设置和三种真实干预（推理蒸馏、知识编辑、遗忘）中验证，该方法能可靠发现已知变化、区分大小干预，且无幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以系统化、可解释地审计大语言模型干预（如蒸馏、编辑、遗忘）带来的行为变化，缺乏统计意义上可靠且自动化的对比评估工具。

Method: 构建自动化对比评估流程：给定基础模型M1和干预模型M2，在对齐的提示上下文中比较两者的自由多token生成，产生经过统计验证的自然语言假设来描述差异，再通过重复主题总结模式。

Result: 合成实验中可靠恢复已知行为变化；在推理蒸馏、知识编辑和遗忘三种真实干预中，成功发现预期和非预期的行为偏移，能区分显著与细微干预效果，在无差异或提示不匹配时不会产生虚假差异。

Conclusion: 该流程为干预后模型行为审计提供了统计上有依据且可解释的工具。

Abstract: We present an automated, contrastive evaluation pipeline for auditing the behavioral impact of interventions on large language models. Given a base model $M_1$ and an intervention model $M_2$, our method compares their free-form, multi-token generations across aligned prompt contexts and produces human-readable, statistically validated natural-language hypotheses describing how the models differ, along with recurring themes that summarize patterns across validated hypotheses.
  We evaluate the approach in synthetic setting by injecting known behavioral changes and showing that the pipeline reliably recovers them. We then apply it to three real-world interventions, reasoning distillation, knowledge editing and unlearning, demonstrating that the method surfaces both intended and unexpected behavioral shifts, distinguishes large from subtle interventions, and does not hallucinate differences when effects are absent or misaligned with the prompt bank. Overall, the pipeline provides a statistically grounded and interpretable tool for post-hoc auditing of intervention-induced changes in model behavior.

</details>


### [125] [Text Corpora as Concept Fields: Black-Box Hallucination and Novelty Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05103)
*Nicholas S. Kersting,Vittorio Castelli,Chieh Ting Yeh,Xinzhu Wang,Saad Taame*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出文本语料的“概念场”（Concept Field）：在句子嵌入空间中，基于连续句子之间的增量估计局部漂移场及其逐点不确定性。通过ζ分数评估候选句转换与场的吻合度，支持黑盒、可归因、概率解读的基座性检测和新颖性检测，并在CFR法规语料和古登堡项目上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基座性/新颖性检测方法要么依赖模型内部状态（白盒），要么依赖检索（受限于检索质量），缺乏一种轻量、可解释、跨域迁移性好的黑盒信号。作者希望设计一种仅利用句子嵌入序列几何信息的统计场方法，实现无需模型内部信息的检测。

Method: 1) 构建**向量序列数据库(VSDB)**，存储句子嵌入及其序列位置和下一句增量元数据；2) 在嵌入空间中，利用连续句子间的增量估计局部高斯漂移场（含不确定性）；3) 给定候选句转换，计算观测增量与局部高斯估计之间的平均绝对z距离ζ作为评分；4) 在密集聚类上计算概念场的散度和旋度，用于发现语义模式（逻辑源、汇、隐式主题）。

Result: 在CFR法规语料的幻觉式基座性检测和古登堡项目的新颖性检测两个大规模场景中，使用受控LLM生成改写，概念场在基座/非基座/不确定三分类策略下实现了强选择性分类性能；与基于检索的基线相比，概念场在两个领域上表现出相似的覆盖-风险行为，支持跨域迁移的概率解读。散度和旋度分析揭示了有意义的语义模式。

Conclusion: 概念场为基座性和新颖性检测提供了一种快速、轻量、可解释的信号，与LLM-as-judge和白盒检测器互补。其黑盒、可归因、概率化特性使其具有良好的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: We introduce the **Concept Field** of a text corpus: a local drift field with pointwise uncertainty, estimated in sentence-embedding space from the deltas between consecutive sentences. Given a candidate sentence transition, we score its agreement with the field by $ζ$, the mean absolute z-distance between the observed delta and the field's local Gaussian estimate. The score is black-box (no model internals), corpus-attributable (every score traces to nearby corpus sentences), and admits a direct probabilistic reading. We support the computation with the introduction of a **Vector Sequence Database (VSDB)** that stores embeddings together with sequence-position and next-delta metadata. We evaluate this approach on two large-scale settings: hallucination-style groundedness detection over the U.S. Code of Federal Regulations, and novelty detection over Project Gutenberg. Using controlled LLM-generated rewrites, Concept Fields achieve strong selective classification performance under a grounded / ungrounded / unsure triage policy, which unlike retrieval-centric baselines have similar coverage-risk behavior across both domains, supporting a probability-based interpretation that transfers across domains. We also sketch how divergence and curl of the Concept Field, computed on dense clusters, surface qualitatively meaningful semantic patterns (logic sources, sinks, and implicit topics), which we offer as hypothesis-generating rather than as a quantitative result. Concept Fields provide a fast, lightweight, and interpretable signal for groundedness and novelty, complementary to LLM-as-judge and white-box detectors.

</details>


### [126] [Beyond Semantics: An Evidential Reasoning-Aware Multi-View Learning Framework for Trustworthy Mental Health Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05121)
*Yucheng Ruan,Ling Huang,Qika Lin,Kai He,Mengling Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多视角学习和主观逻辑的证据融合框架，结合编码器-仅模型的语义信息与解码器-仅模型的推理信息，实现可靠的心理健康预测与不确定性估计，在三个真实数据集上分别取得0.835、0.731和0.751的准确率，并表现出良好的鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习和大语言模型在心理健康预测中存在两个关键问题：一是依赖语义表示，在模糊、噪声或分布偏移数据下产生过度自信预测；二是缺乏可靠的不确定性估计，削弱了高风险心理健康应用中的信任度。

Method: 将任务构建为多视角学习问题：编码器-仅模型提供语义信息，解码器-仅模型提供高阶推理信息；采用基于主观逻辑的证据学习框架显式建模不确定性，并提出一种证据融合策略，平衡互补视角同时折扣不可靠证据。

Result: 在Dreaddit、SDCNL和DepSeverity三个数据集上分别达到0.835、0.731和0.751的准确率；额外实验验证了噪声鲁棒性和可解释性，表明模型能提供可信的不确定性估计和人类可理解的推理信号。

Conclusion: 提出的框架不仅提升了预测性能，还提供了可信的不确定性估计和可解释的推理信号，适用于心理健康评估等风险敏感应用场景。

Abstract: Automated mental health prediction using textual data has shown promising results with deep learning and large language models. However, deploying these models in high-stakes real-world settings remains challenging, as existing approaches largely rely on semantic representations and often produce overconfident predictions under ambiguous, noisy, or shifted data. Moreover, most methods lack reliable uncertainty estimation, undermining trust in risk-sensitive mental health applications. To address these limitations, we formulate the task as a multi-view learning problem that integrates semantic information from encoder-only models with higher-level reasoning information from decoder-only models, where reasoning-aware representations and uncertainty modeling are obtained in a trustworthy manner. To ensure reliable fusion, we adopt an evidential learning framework based on Subjective Logic to explicitly model uncertainty and introduce an evidential fusion strategy that balances complementary views while discounting unreliable evidence. Benchmarking on three real-world datasets, Dreaddit, SDCNL, and DepSeverity, reports accuracies of 0.835, 0.731, and 0.751, respectively, demonstrating its potential for reliable mental health prediction. Additional experiments on robustness to noise and case studies for interpretability confirm that our proposed framework not only improves predictive performance but also provides trustworthy uncertainty estimates and human-understandable reasoning signals, making it suitable for risk-sensitive applications in mental health assessment.

</details>


### [127] [PSK at SemEval-2026 Task 9: Multilingual Polarization Detection Using Ensemble Gemma Models with Synthetic Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05159)
*Srikar Kashyap Pulipaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对SemEval-2026 Task 9多语言极化检测任务，采用基于LoRA微调的Gemma 3模型（12B和27B），结合GPT-4o-mini生成的合成数据与多阶段质量过滤流水线，通过逐语言阈值调优和加权集成策略，在22种语言上取得0.811的平均宏F1分数，排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决跨22种语言的二元极化检测分类问题，探索多语言场景下大模型微调、合成数据增强和集成策略的有效性，并关注模型在不同语言上的泛化能力差异。

Method: 分别对每种语言使用LoRA微调独立的Gemma 3模型（12B和27B）；采用三种合成数据策略（直接生成、释义、对比对创建）由GPT-4o-mini生成，经嵌入去重等多阶段质量过滤；在开发集上为每种语言单独调优阈值（带来2-4% F1提升）；对12B和27B模型预测进行加权集成，并为每种语言选择最优策略。

Result: 在22种语言上平均宏F1为0.811，在所有参赛队伍中排名第二；在3种语言中取得第一名，8种语言进入前三名；替代架构（XLM-RoBERTa、Qwen3）在测试集上F1下降30-50%，表明泛化是关键挑战。

Conclusion: 逐语言阈值调优和加权集成能有效提升多语言极化检测性能；合成数据增强策略有效；大模型在多语言场景下的泛化能力优于传统跨语言模型，后者存在严重的过拟合或分布偏移问题。

Abstract: We present our system for SemEval-2026 Task 9: Multilingual Polarization Detection, a binary classification task spanning 22 languages. Our approach fine-tunes separate Gemma~3 models (12B and 27B parameters) per language using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), augmented with synthetic data generated by a large language model (LLM). We employ three synthetic data strategies (direct generation, paraphrasing, and contrastive pair creation) using GPT-4o-mini, with a multi-stage quality filtering pipeline including embedding-based deduplication. We find that per-language threshold tuning on the development set yields 2 to 4\% F1 improvements without retraining. We also use weighted ensembles of 12B and 27B model predictions with per-language strategy selection. Our final system achieves a mean macro-F1 of 0.811 across all 22 languages, ranking 2nd overall of the participating teams, with 1st place finishes in 3 languages and top-3 in 8 languages. We also find that alternative architectures (XLM-RoBERTa, Qwen3) that showed strong development set performance suffered 30 to 50\% F1 drops on the test set, highlighting the importance of generalization.

</details>


### [128] [The First Token Knows: Single-Decode Confidence for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05166)
*Mina Gabriel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用单次贪婪解码的首词置信度（phi_first）来替代需要多次采样和语义聚类的自一致性方法，在闭卷短答案事实问答任务上达到了相当或略优的不确定性估计效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自一致性检测幻觉需要多次解码且对词汇变化敏感；语义自一致性虽有改进但增加了采样和外部推理开销。作者希望寻找一种低成本且有效的不确定性估计方法。

Method: 提出phi_first指标：在单次贪婪解码中，计算第一个内容性回答token的top-K logits的归一化熵作为置信度分数。在三个7-8B指令微调模型和两个基准数据集上，与标准自一致性和语义自一致性进行比较。

Result: phi_first的平均AUROC为0.820，优于语义自一致性的0.793和标准自一致性的0.791。子集检验表明phi_first与语义一致性中度到强相关，两者融合仅带来微小提升。

Conclusion: 模型初始token分布已包含了多采样一致性方法所捕获的大部分不确定性信息，建议将phi_first作为默认的低成本基线报告。

Abstract: Self-consistency detects hallucinations by generating multiple sampled answers to a question and measuring agreement, but this requires repeated decoding and can be sensitive to lexical variation. Semantic self-consistency improves this by clustering sampled answers by meaning using natural language inference, but it adds both sampling cost and external inference overhead. We show that first-token confidence, phi_first, computed from the normalized entropy of the top-K logits at the first content-bearing answer token of a single greedy decode, matches or modestly exceeds semantic self-consistency on closed-book short-answer factual question answering. Across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models and two benchmarks, phi_first achieves a mean AUROC of 0.820, compared with 0.793 for semantic agreement and 0.791 for standard surface-form self-consistency. A subsumption test shows that phi_first is moderately to strongly correlated with semantic agreement, and combining the two signals yields only a small AUROC improvement over phi_first alone. These results suggest that much of the uncertainty information captured by multi-sample agreement is already available in the model's initial token distribution. We argue that phi_first should be reported as a default low-cost baseline before invoking sampling-based uncertainty estimation.
