</details>


### [129] [Implicit Representations of Grammaticality in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05197)
*Yingshan Susan Wang,Linlu Qiu,Zhaofeng Wu,Roger P. Levy,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过线性探针分析发现，预训练语言模型在内部表征中隐式习得了语法性（grammaticality）与字符串概率（string probability）的区分，探针在语法判断任务上优于基于概率的方法，但在语义合理性任务上表现较差，且跨语言泛化能力良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练语言模型是否在内部表征中隐式地习得了与字符串概率不同的语法性区分能力，即模型是否"知道"语法性独立于统计概率。

Method: 训练线性探针（linear probe）在自然语料库中通过扰动构建的语法/非语法句数据集上，评估其内部表征是否编码语法性；在语法性判断基准、语义合理性基准上进行测试，并开展跨语言泛化实验和概率相关性分析。

Result: 语法性探针在人类标注的语法性判断基准上优于基于LM概率的判断方法；在语义合理性基准上表现不如字符串概率；英语训练的探针在多种语言上展现出显著的跨语言泛化能力；探针分数与字符串概率仅微弱相关。

Conclusion: 预训练语言模型的隐层表征中确实在一定程度上习得了与字符串概率不同的隐式语法性区分，模型内部编码了语法性的独立表征。

Abstract: Grammaticality and likelihood are distinct notions in human language. Pretrained language models (LMs), which are probabilistic models of language fitted to maximize corpus likelihood, generate grammatically well-formed text and discriminate well between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences in tightly controlled minimal pairs. However, their string probabilities do not sharply discriminate between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences overall. But do LMs implicitly acquire a grammaticality distinction distinct from string probability? We explore this question through studying internal representations of LMs, by training a linear probe on a dataset of grammatical and (synthetic) ungrammatical sentences obtained by applying perturbations to a naturalistic text corpus. We find that this simple grammaticality probe generalizes to human-curated grammaticality judgment benchmarks and outperforms LM probability-based grammaticality judgments. When applied to semantic plausibility benchmarks, in which both members of a minimal pair are grammatical and differ in only plausibility, the probe however performs worse than string probability. The English-trained probe also exhibits nontrivial cross-lingual generalization, outperforming string probabilities on grammaticality benchmarks in numerous other languages. Additionally, probe scores correlate only weakly with string probabilities. These results collectively suggest that LMs acquire to some extent an implicit grammaticality distinction within their hidden layers.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [From Language to Logic: A Theoretical Architecture for VLM-Grounded Safe Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04327)
*Kristy Sakano,Kalonji Harrington,Mumu Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种将人类提供的安全规则和操作员偏好集成到自主机器人户外导航中的架构，通过自然语言规则转译为信号时序逻辑（STL）规范，结合视觉-语言模型实现零样本场景理解，并利用形式化满足指标进行运行时监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化户外环境中，自主机器人导航需要同时满足高层安全规则和操作员的语义偏好，现有方法缺乏将自然语言指令与形式化规范有效结合的手段。

Method: 1) 将自然语言规则翻译为STL规范；2) 持久性环境规则和地形偏好映射到2D成本图；3) 时变需求通过STL规范进行运行时监控；4) 使用视觉-语言模型（VLM）进行零样本场景理解，连接人类指令与语义特征和环境约束。

Result: 构建了一个导航模型，能够通过嵌入到环境属性中的形式化满足指标和运行时监控，同时满足STL编码的规范集合和操作员的软偏好。

Conclusion: 该架构为户外非结构化环境中的自主导航提供了一种将高层语义规则与形式化验证相结合的可行方案。

Abstract: We propose an architecture for integrating high-level, human-provided safety rules and operator-aligned semantic preferences into autonomous robot navigation in unstructured outdoor environments. In our approach, natural-language rules are translated into Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications that guide planning and navigation during runtime. Persistent, environment-centric rules and terrain preferences are grounded into a 2D cost map, while temporally dynamic requirements are expressed as STL specifications to be monitored during runtime. We hypothesize the use of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for zero-shot scene understanding, enabling mapping between human instructions, semantic features, and environmental constraints. Within this framework, we construct an illustrative navigation model that is designed to satisfy a set of STL-encoded specifications and soft operator preferences through formal satisfaction metrics embedded into environmental properties and runtime monitoring.

</details>


### [131] [Conditional Flow-VAE for Safety-Critical Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04366)
*Zimu Gong,Brian Zhaoning Zhang,Chris Zhang,Kelvin Wong,Raquel Urtasun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件潜在流匹配的方法，用于可扩展且真实地生成自动驾驶安全关键场景，通过分布匹配将标称场景转化为安全关键场景，并融合仿真与真实数据以提升多样性和真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的安全关键场景在真实数据中极为稀少，手动设计的测试场景缺乏可扩展性，而对抗性优化方法常产生不真实的行为，因此需要一种既能规模化生成又具备真实感的安全关键场景生成方法。

Method: 提出条件潜在流匹配（Conditional Latent Flow Matching）方法，通过分布匹配将标称场景转换为安全关键场景，并利用仿真与真实世界数据的结合来驱动多样化、数据驱动的场景生成。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够更一致且真实地生成新颖的安全关键场景，在场景生成的多样性和真实感方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为自动驾驶系统训练与基准测试的有效工具，为安全关键场景的规模化生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Safety-critical scenarios are essential for the development of autonomous vehicles (AVs) but are rare in real-world driving data. While simulation offers a way to generate such scenarios, manually designed test cases lack scalability, and adversarial optimization often produces unrealistic behaviors. In this work, we introduce a conditional latent flow matching approach for scalable and realistic safety-critical scenario generation. Our method uses distribution matching to transform nominal scenes into safety-critical rollouts. Furthermore, we demonstrate that incorporating both simulation and real-world data enables our framework to efficiently generate diverse, data-driven scenarios. Experimental results highlight that our approach is able to more consistently and realistically generate novel safety-critical scenarios, making it a valuable tool for training and benchmarking AV systems.

</details>


### [132] [Autonomous Laparoscope Control through Unified Mechanics-Based Representation of Multimodal Intraoperative Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04408)
*Xiaojian Li,Jin Fang,Yudong Shi,Xilin Xiao,Kai Yan,Kang Min,Ling Li,Hua Tang,Hangjie Mo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于统一力学建模的多模态信息腹腔镜持镜机器人控制方法，通过将位置、力/力矩、图像等术中信号统一映射为等效 wrench，并采用任务优先级投影合成控制命令，实现了RCM约束保持、柔顺拖拽和器械自主跟踪等多任务操作，经仿真和活体猪实验验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术中多模态信号（位置、力/力矩、图像）物理意义和单位差异显著，难以建立统一表示并生成可直接用于腹腔镜控制的命令。

Method: 1) 设计位置、力/力矩、图像等多术中源的映射策略，统一为操作空间中的等效 wrench 表示；2) 采用任务优先级方案，将 wrench 分别注入任务空间和零空间，通过任务优先级投影合成腹腔镜控制命令。

Result: 在手术模型和活体猪实验中，该方法支持柔顺腹腔镜操作、自主器械跟踪等多任务操作，同时保持RCM约束并减少持续的戳卡部位载荷。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了多模态信息在单一框架内的一致表示与协调融合，有效支持了持镜机器人的多任务控制。

Abstract: Laparoscope-holding robots can provide surgeons with a stable laparoscopic field of view (FOV) and reduce the burden on human assistants. To maintain an ideal intraoperative FOV, the robot must continuously adjust the laparoscope pose according to intraoperative information. However, intraoperative multimodal signals, such as position, force/torque, and images, differ markedly in physical meaning and units, making it difficult to build a unified representation and to generate control commands that can be used directly for laparoscope control. To address this issue, we propose a laparoscope-holding robot control method based on unified mechanics modeling of multimodal information. First, we design mapping strategies for multiple intraoperative sources, including position, force/torque, and images, and unify them into an equivalent-wrench representation in the operational space. Then, using a task-priority scheme, we inject the wrenches into the task space and the null space, respectively, and synthesize laparoscope control commands via task-priority projection, thereby achieving consistent representation and coordinated fusion of multimodal information within a single framework. Finally, taking the intraoperative remote center of motion (RCM) position, force/torque sensor readings, and laparoscopic images as examples, we construct an RCM-constraint wrench to enforce the RCM geometric constraint and reduce the contact force at the trocar site, a laparoscope-manipulation wrench to enable compliant dragging, and an instrument-tracking wrench to achieve autonomous visual tracking of the instruments. Experiments on a surgical phantom and in vivo porcine trials demonstrate that the proposed method supports multi-task operation, including compliant laparoscope manipulation and autonomous instrument tracking, while maintaining the RCM constraint and reducing sustained trocar-site loading.

</details>


### [133] [Tightly-Coupled Estimation and Guidance for Robust Low-Thrust Rendezvous via Adaptive Homotopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04481)
*Batu Candan,Simone Servadio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种导航置信度自适应调节同伦参数的估计-制导紧耦合架构，通过MTF协方差膨胀和自适应同伦控制器，将最小燃料交会制导在严重测量退化下的终端脱靶量从数百米降至亚米级，仅以适度控制增量代价实现鲁棒闭环运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小燃料低推力交会制导产生的bang-bang控制结构对估计误差、传感器异常和求解器正则化高度敏感，导致非合作近距离操作中激进的闭环执行变得脆弱，亟需一种能够根据导航置信度自适应调节控制律的方法。

Method: 在Clohessy-Wiltshire相对运动框架下，采用线性卡尔曼滤波器结合多调谐因子(MTF)协方差膨胀机制进行状态估计；构建归一化新息与MTF活动的复合分数，在线映射至同伦参数，使控制器在置信度下降时平滑过渡到保守控制，感知改善时恢复燃油高效的bang-bang控制。

Result: 固定bang-bang制导在严重测量退化下脆性显著，纯KF和MTF-KF固定同伦参数控制器均产生数百米量级终端脱靶量；所提MTF自适应同伦控制器将终端脱靶量降低约两个数量级（从数百米降至亚米级），仅需适中的控制增量代价。

Conclusion: 自适应同伦是主要的鲁棒性机制，MTF提供额外的精度和效率改善；滚动时域实现具有快速可靠的求解时间，支持该方法在在线实际应用中的可行性。

Abstract: Minimum-fuel low-thrust rendezvous guidance yields bang-bang control structures highly sensitive to estimation errors, sensor anomalies, and solver regularization, making aggressive closed-loop execution brittle for uncooperative proximity operations. This paper proposes a tightly-coupled estimation and guidance architecture where navigation confidence directly modulates the homotopy parameter of a receding-horizon indirect optimal control solver. Relative motion is modeled in the Clohessy-Wiltshire frame. The translational state is estimated via a linear Kalman filter augmented by a Multiple Tuning Factors (MTF) covariance inflation mechanism that suppresses suspicious innovation directions. A composite score from the normalized innovation and MTF activity is mapped online to the homotopy parameter, allowing the controller to relax toward a smoother, conservative regime when confidence degrades, and recover fuel-efficient bang-bang control as sensing improves. Numerical results under severe measurement degradation show fixed bang-bang guidance remains brittle; both plain-KF and MTF-KF fixed-epsilon controllers yield large terminal miss distances. Conversely, the proposed MTF-adaptive homotopy controller reduces terminal miss by roughly two orders of magnitude, from hundreds of meters to sub-meter levels, requiring only a moderate increase in control effort versus the open-loop fuel-optimal benchmark. A comparison indicates adaptive homotopy is the dominant robustness mechanism, while MTF provides additional accuracy and efficiency improvements. The receding-horizon implementation exhibits consistently fast and reliable solution times, supporting the practical online viability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [134] [HDFlow: Hierarchical Diffusion-Flow Planning for Long-horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04525)
*Nandiraju Gireesh,Yuanliang Ju,Chaoyi Xu,Weiheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HDFlow，一种结合扩散模型（高层战略子目标规划）与整流流模型（低层平滑轨迹生成）的分层规划框架，在长时域稀疏奖励任务（家具组装、运动操控）中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式规划器缺乏层次分解的理论框架，且迭代去噪过程导致实时计算开销大。

Method: 高层扩散规划器在潜空间生成策略性子目标序列，利用扩散模型的强探索能力；低层整流流规划器基于ODE生成平滑密集轨迹，兼顾速度与效率。

Result: 在四项家具组装任务（仿真+真实）上显著优于SOTA，并在长时域运动与操控基准上验证了泛化性。

Conclusion: HDFlow通过分层范式融合两种生成模型的互补优势，有效解决了计算效率与层次分解问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative models have shown promise in generating behavior plans for long-horizon, sparse reward tasks. While these approaches have achieved promising results, they often lack a principled framework for hierarchical decomposition and struggle with the computational demands of real-time execution, due to their iterative denoising process. In this work, we introduce Hierarchical Diffusion-Flow (HDFlow), a novel hierarchical planning framework that optimally leverages the strengths of diffusion and rectified flow models to overcome the limitations of single-paradigm generative planners. HDFlow employs a high-level diffusion planner to generate sequences of strategic subgoals in a learned latent space, capitalizing on diffusion's powerful exploratory capabilities. These subgoals then guide a low-level rectified flow planner that generates smooth and dense trajectories, exploiting the speed and efficiency of ordinary differential equation (ODE)-based trajectory generation. We evaluate HDFlow on four challenging furniture assembly tasks in both simulation and real-world, where it significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Furthermore, we also showcase our method's generalizability on two long-horizon benchmarks comprising diverse locomotion and manipulation tasks. Project website: https://hdflow-page.github.io/

</details>


### [135] [Practical validation of synthetic pre-crash scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04564)
*Jian Wu,Ulrich Sander,Carol Flannagan,Jonas Bärgman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对自动驾驶系统安全评估中合成预碰撞场景的实用等价性评估问题，提出了一种基于贝叶斯ROPE框架的改进方法，通过引入分箱统计量来量化数据集之间的分布差异，并通过自动紧急制动系统的案例研究验证了框架的有效性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对合成预碰撞场景与真实场景之间实用等价性的稳健评估方法，传统显著性检验仅关注差异检测而不适用于等价性判断，存在明显研究空白。

Method: 在前期贝叶斯ROPE等价性检验框架基础上，引入基于分箱（binning-based）的方法来定义合适的统计量和等价性准则，提出了两种分箱统计量以衡量数据集中具有实际意义的分布差异。

Result: 案例研究（针对自动紧急制动系统安全评估的两个合成后碰撞前场景数据集）表明，该框架能够提供信息丰富的实用等价性量化评估，并能提供数据集发散方向的诊断性见解。

Conclusion: 该框架具有通用性和可扩展性，可适用于更广泛的验证场景，为不同合成数据应用中的实用等价性评估提供了可解释且有原则的基础。

Abstract: The representativeness of synthetic pre-crash scenarios is crucial for assessing the safety impact of Driving Automation Systems through virtual simulations. However, a gap remains in the robust evaluation of synthetic pre-crash scenarios' practical equivalence to their real-world counterparts; that is, whether they are similar enough for the intended assessment purpose. Conventional significance testing is inadequate, as it focuses on detecting differences rather than establishing practical equivalence. This study addresses the research gap by extending our previous work on a Bayesian Region of Practical Equivalence (ROPE)-based equivalence testing framework by introducing a binning-based approach to define appropriate statistics and equivalence criteria. Two binning-based statistics are proposed to measure practically meaningful distributional differences between datasets in the context of safety impact assessment. The framework's applicability is demonstrated through a case study, which tests the practical equivalence of two synthetic rear-end pre-crash datasets with a previously developed reference dataset in the context of the safety impact assessment of an Automatic Emergency Braking system. The results show that the framework provides informative quantitative assessments of practical equivalence as well as diagnostic insights into the divergence of datasets. Although the demonstration focuses on rear-end pre-crash scenarios, the framework is generic and extensible to broader validation contexts, providing an interpretable and principled basis for practical equivalence assessment across diverse synthetic data applications.

</details>


### [136] [Right Model, Right Time: Real-Time Cascaded-Fidelity MPC for Bipedal Walking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04607)
*Franek Stark,Felix Wiebe,Shubham Vyas,Dennis Mronga,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多阶段全身模型预测控制方法，在近端采用详细全身模型、远端采用简化单刚体模型，通过SQP求解非线性最优控制问题，在MuJoCo仿真中验证了HyPer-2双足机器人的行走控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低双足行走控制中全身模型预测控制的计算复杂度，同时保持预测能力，避免依赖预选足迹位置。

Method: 结合近端详细全身模型与远端简化单刚体模型的多阶段MPC方法，通过acados中的SQP求解非线性最优控制问题，使用预定义接触时序和目标行走速度优化关节力矩。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真中，该方法成功控制18自由度双足机器人HyPer-2实现行走。

Conclusion: 多阶段模型组合策略能有效平衡计算效率与控制性能，为双足机器人实时全身控制提供可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a multi-phase whole-body model predictive control approach for bipedal walking, combining a detailed whole-body model in the near horizon with a simplified single-rigid-body model in the later prediction steps. This reduces computational complexity while retaining prediction capabilities. The resulting nonlinear optimal control problem is solved using sequential quadratic programming (SQP) in acados. Using a prior specified contact schedule and a target walking speed, the controller optimizes joint torques without depending on prior selected foot step locations. The controller is validated in MuJoCo simulation on the 18-DoF bipedal robot HyPer-2

</details>


### [137] [Active Contact Sensing for Robust Robot-to-Human Object Handover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04610)
*Linfeng Li,Lin Shao,David Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种主动感知方法，通过信息采集动作和人体施加力来区分牢固抓握与偶然触碰，实现机器人到人的鲁棒物体交接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的被动感知方法难以在多样化物体和人类行为中泛化，无法有效区分牢固抓握与偶然触碰这两种不同的接触状态。

Method: 采用主动感知方法，机器人执行信息采集动作并感知人体施加的力，通过贝叶斯线性模型（将机器人运动到人体施加力的分段线性映射的分布）来建模接触状态，实现牢固抓握检测和主动信息采集。

Result: 在包含12名参与者和30种不同刚性物体的实验中，达到了97.5%的成功率，比两个常见基线方法高30%以上。

Conclusion: 主动感知方法通过信息性扰动显著提升了机器人-人物体交接的鲁棒性，能够有效区分牢固抓握与偶然触碰，且在不同物体和人类行为中具有良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Robot-to-human object handover is an essential skill for robot assistants, from serving drinks at home to passing surgical tools in the operating room. We expect robots to perform handover robustly -- to release the object only after a firm human grasp while ignoring incidental touches. Existing passive-sensing methods struggle to generalize across diverse objects and human behaviors, as they lack informative perturbations to disambiguate different contact conditions, such as firm grasp versus incidental touch. We propose an active sensing approach for robust handovers: the robot applies information-gathering motions and senses the resulting human-applied forces to infer the contact state. A firm grasp produces forces in multiple directions, while an accidental touch does not. To capture this distinction, we model the contact state with a Bayesian linear model: a distribution over piecewise-linear mappings from robot motions to human-applied forces. This model enables firm grasp detection and active information gathering. In experiments with 12 participants and 30 diverse rigid objects, our method achieved a 97.5% success rate -- over 30% higher than two common baselines.

</details>


### [138] [From Reach to Insert: Tactile-Augmented Precision Assembly under Sub-Millimeter Tolerances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04649)
*Xinpan Meng,Siyao Huang,JingPu Yang,Muyuan Ma,Zhenghua Ma,Lijun Han,Gao Yuan,Houcheng Li,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合模仿学习和强化学习的触觉增强两阶段方法，用于高精度装配中的紧公差插入任务。第一阶段通过IL学习带位置泛化的到达策略，第二阶段通过RL执行插入并实现接触交互中的故障恢复。通过触觉组采样和触觉评论家设计，在五种孔几何形状和三种间隙设置下，该方法在0.05mm最严苛间隙下实现67%成功率，同时将最大交互力降低60%、扭矩降低44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度装配中紧公差插入任务对位姿误差极其敏感，微小的误差便会导致卡死或过大的交互力，使得获取鲁棒且安全的插入策略非常困难。

Method: 提出触觉增强两阶段方法：第一阶段使用模仿学习（IL）学习带位置泛化的到达策略，将peg抓取并带到目标区域附近；第二阶段使用强化学习（RL）执行插入并实现接触交互中的故障恢复。引入触觉组采样增加训练中关键接触段的覆盖率，并设计触觉评论家更准确评估策略值。

Result: 在五种孔几何形状和三种间隙设置下进行系统实验，0.05mm最严苛间隙下达到67%成功率，最大交互力降低60%，扭矩降低44%。

Conclusion: 该方法在多种几何形状和间隙设置下显著提升插入性能，同时保持低接触力，验证了其在精度装配中的有效性和安全性。

Abstract: High-precision assembly frequently involves tight-tolerance insertions, where even slight pose errors can cause jamming or excessive interaction forces, making robust and safe insertion policies difficult to obtain. This paper proposes a tactile-augmented two-stage method that combines Imitation Learning (IL) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) for precision insertion tasks. In the first stage, IL learns a reaching policy with position generalization that grasps the peg and brings it to the vicinity of the target region. In the second stage, RL executes the insertion and enables recovery from failures during contact-rich interactions. To better exploit tactile feedback, we introduce tactile group sampling to increase coverage of critical contact segments during training, and design a tactile critic to more accurately evaluate policy values, improving insertion performance while maintaining low contact forces. We conduct systematic experiments across five hole geometries and three clearance settings. Results show that our method substantially improves insertion performance across all settings; under the most challenging 0.05\,mm clearance, it achieves a 67\% success rate while keeping contact forces low, reducing the maximum interaction force by 60\% and torque by 44\%, thereby validating both effectiveness and safety for precision assembly.

</details>


### [139] [AI-Aided Advancements in Autonomous Underwater Vehicle Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04672)
*Guy Damari,Zeev Yampolsky,Nadav Cohen,Arup Kumar Sahoo,Jeryes Danial,Felipe O. Silva,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI辅助的AUV定位技术，重点介绍了集成惯性导航系统、多普勒测速仪和相机的先进传感器融合架构，以及AI驱动的学习方法在增强惯性航位推算和自适应融合算法方面的最新进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁波在水中快速衰减导致卫星无线电信号不可用，且海洋环境动态不可预测，给AUV导航带来巨大挑战，因此需要高精度定位方法。

Method: 回顾了AI辅助AUV定位方法，包括先进传感器融合架构（集成惯性导航系统、多普勒测速仪、相机）以及AI驱动的学习方法来增强惯性航位推算和自适应融合算法。

Result: 综述了AI辅助定位技术的最新里程碑，为高精度自主水下导航提供了综合路线图。

Conclusion: AI驱动的学习方法为克服传统模型滤波的局限性提供了变革性方案，是实现自主水下任务高精度导航的关键路径。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) have become indispensable for deep-sea exploration, spanning critical scientific research and commercial applications. The rapid attenuation of electromagnetic waves renders satellite radio signals unavailable, while the dynamic unpredictability of the marine environment presents formidable navigation challenges. This chapter explores recent advancements in AI-aided AUV positioning, specifically focusing on advanced sensor fusion architectures that integrate inertial navigation systems with Doppler velocity logs and cameras. Beyond traditional model-based filtering, we examine the transformative emergence of AI-driven learning approaches in enhancing inertial dead-reckoning tasks and adaptive fusion algorithms. By addressing these recent milestones, this chapter provides a comprehensive roadmap for achieving the high-precision navigation essential for autonomous underwater missions.

</details>


### [140] [From Pixels to Tokens: A Systematic Study of Latent Action Supervision for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04678)
*Yihan Lin,Haoyang Li,Yang Li,Haitao Shen,Yihan Zhao,Chao Shao,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中潜在动作监督的两种范式：基于图像的潜在动作（正则化轨迹）和基于动作的潜在动作（统一目标空间）。在统一VLA基线框架下，比较了四种集成策略，发现图像型潜在动作有利于长程推理和场景泛化，动作型则擅长复杂运动协调，且直接用离散潜在动作令牌监督VLM效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型使用潜在动作作为异构数据集的中间表征，但缺乏对不同潜在动作监督方法的系统性比较和统一研究。

Method: 从两个视角（基于图像的潜在动作和基于动作的潜在动作）对潜在动作监督进行结构化研究，在统一VLA基线框架下实例化并比较了四种代表性集成策略。

Result: 发现了公式-任务对应关系：图像型潜在动作利于长程推理和场景级泛化，动作型潜在动作擅长复杂运动协调；直接用离散潜在动作令牌监督VLM效果最优；潜在动作监督在混合数据训练中具有优势。

Conclusion: 潜在动作监督为VLA训练提供了有前景的方向，为异构数据处理和模型训练提供指导性建议。

Abstract: Latent actions serve as an intermediate representation that enables consistent modeling of vision-language-action (VLA) models across heterogeneous datasets. However, approaches to supervising VLAs with latent actions are fragmented and lack a systematic comparison. This work structures the study of latent action supervision from two perspectives: (i) regularizing the trajectory via image-based latent actions, and (ii) unifying the target space with action-based latent actions. Under a unified VLA baseline, we instantiate and compare four representative integration strategies. Our results reveal a formulation-task correspondence: image-based latent actions benefit long-horizon reasoning and scene-level generalization, whereas action-based latent actions excel at complex motor coordination. Furthermore, we find that directly supervising the VLM with discrete latent action tokens yields the most effective performance. Finally, our experiments offer initial insights into the benefits of latent action supervision in mixed-data, suggesting a promising direction for VLA training. Code is available at https://github.com/RUCKBReasoning/From_Pixels_to_Tokens.

</details>


### [141] [3D Printing of Passively Actuated Self-Folding Robots with Integrated Functional Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04757)
*Gaolin Ge,Qifeng Yang,Haoran Lu,Tingyu Cheng,Martin Nisser,Yiyue Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种弹性驱动的自折叠方法，通过3D打印导电PLA平板、弹性带和钩子结构，实现从平面到三维几何的折叠，并集成电子元件、电容触觉、霍尔传感器和振动电机，实现低成本、无外部刺激的模块化机器人制造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自折叠机器人制造方法复杂、成本高，或依赖外部刺激（如热、光），不利于集成传感和驱动，且难以实现可扩展的模块化设计。

Method: 在3D打印的导电PLA平板上设计钩子结构，通过弹性带储存能量驱动折叠；推导了平衡折叠角度的闭合模型（铰链刚度与弹性带力矩平衡）；平板基底用作电容触觉电极，集成霍尔传感器和ERM电机实现对接检测和振动驱动。

Result: 实验验证了折叠模型的有效性，建立了铰链厚度、弹性带尺寸、钩间距与目标角度的设计图谱；实现了多种多面体模块，展示了自折叠立方体、可部署夹爪和腱驱动手指三种应用。

Conclusion: 该方法低成本、无需外部刺激，将驱动和传感集成于同一基底，为可扩展的模块化机器人集体提供了新途径。

Abstract: We introduce an elastic-driven self-folding approach that fabricates robots directly from flat 3D-printed conductive PLA nets. Elastic bands routed through printed hooks store energy that folds the sheet into programmed 3D geometries, while the flat state allows accurate placement of electronics and magnets before deployment. The same substrate doubles as electrodes for capacitive touch and supports a reusable platform I/O palette with Hall sensors and eccentric rotating mass (ERM) motors for docking detection and vibration actuation. We also derive a closed-form folding model that balances hinge stiffness with elastic band moment to predict equilibrium fold angles; experiments validate the model and yield a design map linking hinge thickness, band size, and hook spacing to target angles. Using this workflow we realize multiple polyhedral modules and demonstrate three applications: a cube that highlights the potential of self-folding for scalable modular robot collectives, a deployable gripper, and a tendon-driven finger. The method is low cost, stimulus-free, and integrates actuation and sensing.

</details>


### [142] [Dr-PoGO: Direct Radar Pose-Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04806)
*Cedric Le Gentil,Weican Li,Leonardo Brizi,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dr-PoGO是一种利用2D旋转雷达进行SLAM的方法，通过直接配准技术实现里程计和回环检测，在多种真实自动驾驶环境下300公里数据上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达能在恶劣天气（如灰尘、雪、雨）中工作，但现有雷达SLAM方法多依赖点云或特征提取，作者希望利用直接配准技术提升鲁棒性。

Method: 采用直接配准技术进行里程计（DRO）；使用RaPlace算法提供回环候选；提出粗到细配准方法，先用视觉特征和描述子获得初始位姿估计，再进行直接变换精化；最后通过位姿图优化优化全局轨迹。

Result: 在超过300公里的多种真实自动驾驶环境数据中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: Dr-PoGO在恶劣天气条件下具有鲁棒性，且代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper introduces Dr-PoGO, a method for Simultaneous Localization And Mapping (SLAM) using a 2D spinning radar. Unlike cameras or lidars that require line-of-sight, millimetre-wave radars can `see' through dust, falling snow, rain, etc. Accordingly, it is a great modality for robust perception regardless of the weather conditions. While most existing radar-based SLAM methods rely on the extraction of point clouds or features to perform ego-motion estimation, Dr-PoGO leverages direct registration techniques for odometry (DRO) and loop-closure registration. An off-the-shelf radar-focused place recognition algorithm, RaPlace, provides loop-closure candidates. As RaPlace does not provide relative transformations, Dr-PoGO introduces a coarse-to-fine registration that uses visual features and descriptors to obtain an initial guess for the direct transformation refinement. The global trajectory is optimized in a pose-graph optimization. Dr-PoGO demonstrates state-of-the-art performance over 300km of data in various real-world automotive environments. Our implementation is publicly available: https://github.com/utiasASRL/dr_pogo.

</details>


### [143] [Optimal Uncertainty-Aware Calibration for the AX=YB Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04809)
*Yanjia Chen,Xiangfei Li,Huan Zhao,Yiyuan Hong,Guanxiao Xia,Jiexin Zhang,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于李代数的迭代优化框架用于手眼标定，通过结构约束保持、同步参数更新、动态不确定性度量和高效初始解生成，在高不确定条件下将估计精度提升至少67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手眼标定方法在过载、大工作空间等实际工业机器人场景中，数据存在不确定性且难以精确建模，导致标定精度显著下降。

Method: 1）基于李代数的迭代优化算法，严格保持标定参数的结构约束并实现同步更新；2）引入不确定性度量指标评估数据源间的相对不确定性，动态优化迭代过程；3）设计高效初始解生成方法以提升收敛稳定性和精度。

Result: 在合成数据集的高不确定条件下，相比现有方法估计精度提升至少67%；数值仿真和实际实验均验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架避免了显式不确定性建模的困难，通过动态不确定性度量实现了近似全局最优的手眼标定，在工业场景中具有显著优势。

Abstract: This article proposes a general optimization framework for solving hand-eye calibration problem. Unlike traditional methods, an iterative algorithm based on Lie algebra that achieves approximately global optimal solutions is developed. During the optimization process, the method strictly preserves the structural constraints of the calibration parameters and enables synchronized updates between calibration parameters. Recognizing that data used in real-word hand-eye calibration often contain uncertainty, especially in over-loading and large workspace industrial robot scenarios, which can significantly degrade accuracy, and accurately modeling such uncertainty is inherently difficult, this article avoids explicit uncertainty modeling. Instead, an uncertainty metric to evaluate the relative uncertainty between data sources is introduced and used to dynamically refine the iterative process. To further enhance convergence efficiency, an effective initial solution generation method that improves overall stability and accuracy is designed. Numerical simulations and real-world experiments validate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, and in synthetic datasets, the proposed approach improves the estimation accuracy by at least 67\% under high-uncertainty conditions compared with the existing methods.

</details>


### [144] [Modular Reinforcement Learning For Cooperative Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04939)
*Erel Shtossel,Gal A. Kaminka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种模块化分解表示方法，将空间交互状态的每个特征交由独立学习过程处理并聚合结果，以解决多机器人强化学习中因交互状态组合爆炸导致的机器人内存挑战，并在模拟觅食任务中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人强化学习中，每个机器人在独立学习时需要表示大量可能的交互状态（组合爆炸问题），这对机器人有限的内存能力构成挑战。现有方法要求机器人维护组合数量的交互状态表示，存储开销过大。

Method: 采用模块化（分解式）表示方法：将空间交互状态的每个特征拆分开来，每个特征由独立的学习过程处理，然后将各学习结果进行聚合，从而避免维护完整的联合状态空间。

Result: 在模拟机器人蜂群觅食任务的多个实验中验证了该方法的效果。

Conclusion: 模块化分解表示能有效解决多机器人强化学习中的交互状态组合爆炸问题，使计算资源有限的机器人也能学习有效的协作交互策略。

Abstract: A cooperative robot swarm is a collective of computationally-limited robots that share a common goal. Each robot can only interact with a small subset of its peers, without knowing how this affects the collective utility. Recent advances in distributed multi-agent reinforcement learning have demonstrated that it is possible for robots to learn how to interact effectively with others, in a manner that is aligned with the common goal, despite each robot learning independently of others. However, this requires each robot to represent a potentially combinatorial number of interaction states, challenging the memory capabilities of the robots. This paper proposes an alternative approach for representing spatial interaction states for multi-robot reinforcement learning in swarms. A modular (decomposed) representation is used, where each feature of the state is handled by a separate learning procedure, and the results aggregated. We demonstrate the efficacy of the approach in numerous experiments with simulated robot swarms carrying out foraging.

</details>


### [145] [Reduced-order Neural Modeling with Differentiable Simulation for High-Detail Tactile Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05053)
*Yuhu Guo,Zhikai Shen,Jiasheng Qu,Chenghao Qian,Yuming Huang,Bin Chen,Guoxing Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种降阶神经仿真框架，将粗粒度MPM动力学与隐式神经解码器结合，从紧凑潜变量中重建亚粒子级触觉细节，相比TacIPC实现65%以上加速和40%内存节省，同时保持更高几何保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率弹性体变形仿真计算成本极高，有限元方法(FEM)需要昂贵的网格重构，物质点法(MPM)则面临粒子-内存权衡问题，亟需一种高效且高保真的触觉仿真方法。

Method: 提出降阶神经仿真框架：耦合粗粒度MPM动力学与隐式神经解码器，从紧凑潜变量重建亚粒子级触觉细节；通过高-低分辨率配对仿真学习连续变形流形；实现物理一致且可微的推理。

Result: 相比TacIPC：仿真速度提升65%以上，内存使用降低40%，几何保真度更优；触觉渲染和3D表面重建精度提升25%，能生成逼真深度图和表面网格，推理速度更快。

Conclusion: 提出的降阶神经模型实现了高细节、物理一致的触觉仿真，在显著提升效率的同时保持了物理合理性，适用于机器人交互与优化任务。

Abstract: Tactile perception is key to dexterous manipulation, yet simulating high-resolution elastomer deformation remains computationally prohibitive. Finite element methods (FEM) deliver high fidelity but demand costly remeshing, while Material Point Methods (MPM) suffer from heavy particle-memory tradeoffs. We propose a {reduced-order neural simulation framework} that couples coarse-grained MPM dynamics with an implicit neural decoder to reconstruct sub-particle tactile details from compact latent states. The framework learns a continuous deformation manifold from paired high- and low-resolution simulations, enabling physically consistent, differentiable inference. Compared to the TacIPC, our method achieves over 65\% faster simulation and {40\% lower memory usage}, while maintaining better geometric fidelity. In tactile rendering and 3D surface reconstruction, our methods further improve accuracy by 25\% and produce realistic depth images and surface mesh within a faster inference speed. These results demonstrate that the proposed reduced-order neural model enables high-detail, physically grounded tactile simulation with substantial efficiency gains for robotic interaction and optimization.

</details>


### [146] [Driver-WM: A Driver-Centric Traffic-Conditioned Latent World Model for In-Cabin Dynamics Rollout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05092)
*Haozhuang Chi,Daosheng Qiu,Hao Su,Haochen Liu,Zirui Li,Haoruo Zhang,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Driver-WM是一个以驾驶员为中心的潜在世界模型，能够在因果条件于外部交通上下文的情况下，滚动预测舱内动态，统一了物理运动学预测与行为及情感语义识别，采用双流架构和门控因果注入机制实现外部到内部的状态耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型仅关注外部环境预测，而舱内智能仅停留在识别层面，缺乏对驾驶员动态的多步滚动预测能力，无法满足L2/L3级共享控制过渡中的人机协同需求。

Method: 在冻结的视觉-语言特征构建的紧凑潜空间中，采用双流架构分别编码外部交通和内部驾驶员状态，通过门控因果注入机制（可学习向量门）调控外部上下文扰动并严格保持时间因果性，实现外部到内部的因果条件化滚动预测。

Result: 在多任务辅助驾驶基准上，Driver-WM在反应性高动态操作的长期几何预测上表现鲁棒，且提升了驾驶员与交通状态的语义对齐质量；外部到内部的显式条件化支持可控的测试时干预以分析机制响应。

Conclusion: 以驾驶员为中心的因果潜在世界模型能够有效统一物理运动学预测与行为情感语义识别，为L2/L3级自动驾驶中的人机共享控制过渡提供了可干预、可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Safe L2/L3 driving automation requires anticipating human-in-the-loop reactions during shared-control transitions. While most driving world models forecast the external environment, in-cabin intelligence remains strictly recognition-oriented and lacks multi-step rollout capabilities for driver dynamics. We introduce Driver-WM, a driver-centric latent world model that rolls out in-cabin dynamics causally conditioned on out-cabin traffic context. This formulation unifies physical kinematics forecasting with auxiliary behavioral and emotional semantic recognition. Operating in a compact latent space constructed from frozen vision-language features, Driver-WM adopts a dual-stream architecture to separately encode external traffic and internal driver states. These streams are directionally coupled via a gated causal injection mechanism, which uses a learned vector gate to modulate external contextual perturbations while strictly enforcing temporal causality. Evaluations on a multi-task assistive driving benchmark demonstrate that Driver-WM yields robust long-horizon geometric forecasting for reactive high-motion maneuvers and improves semantic alignment for both driver and traffic states. Finally, the explicit external-to-internal conditioning allows for controlled test-time interventions to systematically analyze mechanism responses.

</details>


### [147] [LineRides: Line-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Bicycle Robot Stunts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05110)
*Seungeun Rho,Shamel Fahmi,Jeonghwan Kim,Arianna Ilvonen,Sehoon Ha,Gabriel Nelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LineRides框架，通过用户提供的空间引导线和稀疏关键朝向，无需示范或显式时序即可让自行车机器人学习多种特技行为，并在Ultra Mobility Vehicle上实现了五种不同特技的成功执行与切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中为敏捷机器人动作设计奖励函数仍然困难，基于演示的方法需要参考运动数据，而新颖平台或极端特技场景往往缺乏这些数据。

Method: 提出LineRides框架，包括：(1) 使用跟踪裕度处理物理不可行的引导线；(2) 通过沿引导线的行进距离衡量进度以解决时序模糊性；(3) 通过位置和序列式关键朝向消除运动细节歧义。

Result: 在Ultra Mobility Vehicle (UMV)上训练的策略支持正常驾驶与特技执行之间的无缝切换，实现了五种不同特技：MiniHop、LargeHop、ThreePointTurn、Backflip和DriftTurn。

Conclusion: LineRides能够在不依赖示范或显式时序的情况下，使自定义自行车机器人学习多种可命令的特技行为，为敏捷机器人动作学习提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Designing reward functions for agile robotic maneuvers in reinforcement learning remains difficult, and demonstration-based approaches often require reference motions that are unavailable for novel platforms or extreme stunts. We present LineRides, a line-guided learning framework that enables a custom bicycle robot to acquire diverse, commandable stunt behaviors from a user-provided spatial guideline and sparse key-orientations, without demonstrations or explicit timing. LineRides handles physically infeasible guidelines using a tracking margin that permits controlled deviation, resolves temporal ambiguity by measuring progress via traveled distance along the guideline, and disambiguates motion details through position- and sequence-based key-orientations. We evaluate LineRides on the Ultra Mobility Vehicle (UMV) and show that the policy trained with our methods supports seamless transitions between normal driving and stunt execution, enabling five distinct stunts on command: MiniHop, LargeHop, ThreePointTurn, Backflip, and DriftTurn.

</details>


### [148] [ConsisVLA-4D: Advancing Spatiotemporal Consistency in Efficient 3D-Perception and 4D-Reasoning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05126)
*Wei Li,Jizhihui Liu,Li Yixing,Junwen Tong,Rui Shao,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ConsisVLA-4D是一个通过CV-Aligner(跨视角语义一致性)、CO-Fuser(跨对象空间几何一致性)和CS-Thinker(跨场景时空一致性)三个模块增强3D感知和4D推理时空一致性的统一高效框架，在LIBERO基准和真实平台上相比OpenVLA分别提升21.6%/41.5%性能并加速2.3倍/2.4倍推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在时空感知与推理上存在局限：1)空间表征依赖额外传感器带来计算开销；2)视觉推理仅限未来帧预测，缺乏与指令对齐的场景一致性，导致时空一致性不足。

Method: 提出ConsisVLA-4D框架，包含三个核心模块：1)CV-Aligner通过过滤指令相关区域并对齐多视角物体身份实现跨视角语义一致性；2)CO-Fuser通过紧凑潜在表征消除跨视角物体间空间关系歧义，保证跨对象空间几何一致性；3)CS-Thinker从CV-Aligner的物体语义token学习局部动态知识，从CO-Fuser的几何token学习全局深度，实现跨场景时空一致性。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上性能提升21.6%，推理速度提升2.3倍；在真实平台上性能提升41.5%，推理速度提升2.4倍。

Conclusion: ConsisVLA-4D通过高效时空一致性设计，显著提升了VLA模型在3D感知和4D推理任务中的性能与推理效率。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models primarily focus on mapping 2D observations to actions, but exhibit notable limitations in spatiotemporal perception and reasoning: 1) spatial representations often rely on additional sensors, introducing substantial computational overhead; 2) visual reasoning is typically limited to future-frame prediction, lacking alignment with the instruction-grounded scene and thus compromising spatiotemporal consistency. To address these challenges, we propose ConsisVLA-4D, a unified and efficient framework that enhances spatiotemporal consistency in 3D perception and 4D reasoning. Specifically, we design: 1) CV-Aligner, which ensures cross-view object semantic consistency by filtering instruction-relevant regions and aligning object identities across multiple viewpoints; 2) CO-Fuser, which guarantees cross-object spatial geometric consistency by eliminating spatial relation ambiguities between objects across views using compact latent representations. Building upon these, we introduce 3) CS-Thinker to achieve cross-scene spatiotemporal consistency as actions unfold. It learns implicit knowledge of local dynamics from object-semantic tokens of CV-Aligner and global depth from geometric tokens of CO-Fuser, thereby enhancing efficient visual reasoning under scene variations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, benefiting from its efficient spatiotemporal consistency design, ConsisVLA-4D achieves 21.6% and 41.5% performance improvements, along with 2.3-fold and 2.4-fold inference speedups compared to OpenVLA on the LIBERO benchmark and real-world platforms, respectively.ConsisVLA-4D is open-sourced and publicly available at

</details>


### [149] [When Life Gives You BC, Make Q-functions: Extracting Q-values from Behavior Cloning for On-Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05172)
*Lakshita Dodeja,Ondrej Biza,Shivam Vats,Stephen Hart,Stefanie Tellex,Robin Walters,Karl Schmeckpeper,Thomas Weng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Q2RL是一种结合行为克隆与强化学习的离线到在线学习算法，通过Q值估计和Q值门控机制，在少量在线交互后实现从BC到RL策略的平滑过渡，在机器人操作任务中达到100%成功率且比原始BC策略提升3.75倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆缺乏演示数据收集后的在线自我改进机制，现有离线到在线学习方法因离线数据与在线学习之间的分布不匹配，导致策略覆盖之前学到的良好动作。

Method: Q2RL包含两部分：(1) Q-Estimation：通过少量环境交互步骤从BC策略中提取Q函数；(2) Q-Gating：基于BC策略和RL策略动作各自的Q值进行门控切换，为RL策略训练收集样本。

Result: 在D4RL和robomimic基准的多种操作任务中，Q2RL在成功率和收敛速度上均优于SOTA离线到在线学习基线；在机器人上实时学习时，1-2小时内成功率可达100%，最高提升3.75倍。

Conclusion: Q2RL是一种高效的离线到在线学习算法，能够有效解决分布不匹配问题，且足够高效以应用于真实机器人上的强化学习训练。

Abstract: Behavior Cloning (BC) has emerged as a highly effective paradigm for robot learning. However, BC lacks a self-guided mechanism for online improvement after demonstrations have been collected. Existing offline-to-online learning methods often cause policies to replace previously learned good actions due to a distribution mismatch between offline data and online learning. In this work, we propose Q2RL, Q-Estimation and Q-Gating from BC for Reinforcement Learning, an algorithm for efficient offline-to-online learning. Our method consists of two parts: (1) Q-Estimation extracts a Q-function from a BC policy using a few interaction steps with the environment, followed by online RL with (2) Q-Gating, which switches between BC and RL policy actions based on their respective Q-values to collect samples for RL policy training. Across manipulation tasks from D4RL and robomimic benchmarks, Q2RL outperforms SOTA offline-to-online learning baselines on success rate and time to convergence. Q2RL is efficient enough to be applied in an on-robot RL setting, learning robust policies for contact-rich and high precision manipulation tasks such as pipe assembly and kitting, in 1-2 hours of online interaction, achieving success rates of up to 100% and up to 3.75x improvement against the original BC policy. Code and video are available at https://pages.rai-inst.com/q2rl_website/

</details>


### [150] [A Closed-Form Dual-Barrier CBF Safety Filter for Holonomic Robots on Incrementally Built Occupancy Grid Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05182)
*Himanshu Paudel,Basanta Joshi,Dhirendra Raj Madai,Alina Bartaula,Biman Rimal,Sanjay Neupane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双屏障控制屏障函数（CBF）的安全滤波器，用于在增量构建的占据栅格地图中实现全向机器人的实时安全速度控制。该滤波器同时避免已地图化障碍物并限制进入未探索区域，通过占据栅格符号距离场推导出闭式解，仅需每周期求解一个小型线性系统。在资源受限平台（如Raspberry Pi）上开销极低，并采用自适应增益调度在信息丰富区域放松前沿约束、在良好地图化区域收紧约束，以平衡探索效率与安全性。该方法可作为最小侵入性修正与任意标称控制器（包括基于学习的方法）组合使用。在PX4控制的四旋翼飞行器上的硬件飞行实验展示了零碰撞结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在未知环境中探索时，未地图化区域引入不可约减的不确定性，进入这些区域存在碰撞风险，尤其是使用前向传感器时。现有方法未能同时处理已地图化障碍物规避和未探索区域限制的问题，且需要高效的计算方案以适应资源受限平台。

Method: 提出双屏障CBF安全滤波器，从占据栅格符号距离场解析推导两个约束条件（避碰已地图化障碍物、限制进入未探索区域），形成闭式安全滤波器。采用自适应增益调度机制，根据信息丰富度动态调整前沿约束的松紧程度。方法在速度空间中以最小侵入性修正方式运行，可与任意标称控制器组合。

Result: 在资源受限的Raspberry Pi平台上实现了低计算开销，与SLAM和规划算法共存。在PX4控制的四旋翼飞行器上的多次室内硬件飞行实验中实现了零碰撞。

Conclusion: 所提出的双屏障CBF安全滤波器能够有效平衡未知环境探索中的安全性与效率，计算开销低，适用于资源受限平台，且与多种标称控制器兼容，硬件实验验证了其实际可行性。

Abstract: We present a dual-barrier control barrier function (CBF) safety filter for real-time, safety-critical velocity control of holonomic robots operating in incrementally built occupancy grid maps. As a robot explores an unknown environment, unmapped regions introduce irreducible uncertainty, since obstacle geometry beyond the explored frontier is unknown, making entry into such regions a source of collision risk, especially with front-facing sensors. To address this, we enforce two constraints: avoidance of mapped obstacles and restriction from unexplored regions. Both constraints are derived analytically from the occupancy grid's signed distance field, yielding a closed-form safety filter that requires only a small linear system solve per cycle. On resource-constrained platforms such as the Raspberry Pi, where SLAM and planning already consume significant compute, the low overhead of the proposed filter preserves resources. An adaptive gain schedule relaxes the frontier constraint in information-rich regions and tightens it in well-mapped areas, improving exploration efficiency while maintaining safety. The filter operates in velocity space as a minimally invasive correction and composes with arbitrary nominal controllers, including learning-based methods. Hardware flight experiments on a PX4-controlled quadrotor demonstrate zero collisions across multiple indoor runs.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [LCM: Lossless Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04050)
*Clint Ehrlich,Theodore Blackman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Lossless Context Management (LCM)，一种用于LLM记忆的确定性架构，通过递归上下文压缩和递归任务分解两种机制，在长上下文任务中显著优于Claude Code，并在OOLONG评估中所有上下文长度（32K至1M token）上取得更高分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在处理长上下文任务时存在记忆丢失、上下文窗口限制以及递归方法缺乏终止保证等问题，亟需一种既能保留完整历史状态又能保证任务可终止的确定性内存管理架构。

Method: LCM将符号递归分解为两种引擎管理的确定性机制：(1) 递归上下文压缩——构建层次化摘要DAG，在压缩历史消息的同时保留指向原始内容的无损指针；(2) 递归任务分解——使用引擎管理的并行原语（如LLM-Map）替代模型自编写的循环，类似于编程语言中从GOTO到结构化控制流的演进。

Result: 基于Opus 4.6构建的LCM增强型编码智能体Volt，在OOLONG长上下文评估中全面超越Claude Code，在32K到1M token的每个上下文长度区间均取得更高得分。

Conclusion: LCM既验证了递归语言模型范式的有效性，又通过牺牲最大灵活性换取了终止保证、短任务零开销连续性以及所有历史状态的无损可检索性，证明了递归上下文操作可超越传统LLM乃至具有原生文件系统访问能力的顶级编码智能体。

Abstract: We introduce Lossless Context Management (LCM), a deterministic architecture for LLM memory that outperforms Claude Code on long-context tasks. When benchmarked using Opus 4.6, our LCM-augmented coding agent, Volt, achieves higher scores than Claude Code on the OOLONG long-context eval, including at every context length between 32K and 1M tokens.
  LCM may be considered both a vindication and extension of the recursive paradigm pioneered by Recursive Language Models (RLMs). Our results demonstrate that recursive context manipulation can outperform not just conventional LLMs, but frontier coding agents with native file-system access.
  LCM departs from RLM by decomposing symbolic recursion into two deterministic, engine-managed mechanisms: recursive context compression, in which a hierarchical summary DAG automatically compacts older messages while retaining lossless pointers to every original; and recursive task partitioning, in which engine-managed parallel primitives like LLM-Map replace model-written loops. This trade-off, analogous to the move from GOTO to structured control flow in program-ming language design, sacrifices maximal flexibility for termination guarantees, zero-cost continuity on short tasks, and lossless retrievability of all prior state.

</details>


### [152] [Regularized Centered Emphatic Temporal Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04100)
*Xingguo Chen,Chaohui Wu,Jinguo Ye,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang,Guang Yang,Tianyu Liang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出正则化强调时序差分学习（RETD），通过在强调TD学习中引入仅作用于辅助中心化递归的正则化项，解决了离策略TD学习中稳定性、投影几何与方差控制的权衡问题，避免了朴素中心化强调方法导致的耦合矩阵正定性破坏，并在线性离策略预测任务上验证了其鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略时序差分学习存在稳定性、投影几何与方差控制三者间的结构性权衡。强调TD（ETD）通过后续强调改善了离策略投影几何，但后续迹的高方差问题突出。中心化虽能自然移除TD误差中的漂移项，但将其直接用于强调框架会引入辅助耦合，破坏ETD关键矩阵的正定性。

Method: 提出正则化强调时序差分学习（RETD），保留后续迹的同时仅对辅助中心化递归施加正则化，等价于将耦合关键矩阵的右下角块从1提升至1+c。推导了RETD核心矩阵，并在保守充分正则化条件下证明了收敛性。

Result: 在线性离策略预测诊断任务上，RETD避免了朴素中心化强调学习的不稳定性，保持了有利的强调几何特性，且正则化参数c在诊断测试中表现出鲁棒的中间区间。

Conclusion: RETD通过定向正则化有效解耦了中心化与强调机制之间的不良耦合，在保持ETD有利投影几何的同时控制了方差，为离策略TD学习中的三难困境提供了可行的解决路径。

Abstract: Off-policy temporal-difference (TD) learning with function approximation faces a structural tradeoff among stability, projection geometry, and variance control. Emphatic TD (ETD) improves the off-policy projection geometry through follow-on emphasis, but the follow-on trace can have high variance. We revisit this tradeoff through Bellman-error centering. Although centering naturally removes a common drift term from TD errors, we show that a naive centered emphatic extension introduces an auxiliary coupling that can destroy the positive-definiteness of the ETD key matrix. We propose \emph{Regularized Emphatic Temporal-Difference Learning} (RETD), which preserves the follow-on trace and regularizes only the auxiliary centering recursion, corresponding to lifting the lower-right block of the coupled key matrix from \(1\) to \(1+c\). We derive the RETD core matrix, prove convergence under a conservative sufficient regularization condition, and evaluate the method on diagnostic linear off-policy prediction tasks. The experiments show that RETD avoids the instability of naive centered emphatic learning, preserves favorable emphatic geometry, and exhibits a robust intermediate regime for the regularization parameter \(c\) across the diagnostics.

</details>


### [153] [ANDRE: An Attention-based Neuro-symbolic Differentiable Rule Extractor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04193)
*Iman Sharifi,Peng Wei,Saber Fallah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ANDRE，一种基于注意力机制的神经符号可微规则提取器，通过可微的注意力驱动逻辑算子（取代传统模糊算子）在连续规则空间中学习一阶逻辑程序，在概率谓词和噪声监督下实现了优于现有可微ILP方法的规则提取质量与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号与神经符号ILP方法难以扩展至含噪声和概率设定的场景：经典ILP依赖离散组合搜索，对不确定性脆弱；可微ILP方法依赖预定义规则模板或不精确的模糊算子，在概率谓词推理时存在梯度消失或逻辑结构近似差的问题。

Method: ANDRE通过基于注意力机制的可微合取与析取算子（近似逻辑min-max语义）替代规则模板和逻辑算子，支持在连续规则空间中优化；可柔性地选择、否定或排除规则中的谓词，在保持符号结构的同时实现全微分训练。

Result: 在经典ILP基准、大规模知识库以及含概率谓词和噪声监督的合成数据集上，ANDRE取得竞争性甚至更优的预测性能，能在不确定性下可靠恢复正确符号规则；对中等程度标签噪声具有鲁棒性，在规则提取质量和稳定性上显著超越现有可微ILP方法。

Conclusion: ANDRE通过注意力机制驱动的可微逻辑算子有效弥合了符号ILP与可微学习之间的差距，为含噪声概率环境下的可解释规则学习提供了稳定且准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Inductive Logic Programming (ILP) aims to learn interpretable first-order rules from data, but existing symbolic and neuro-symbolic approaches struggle to scale to noisy and probabilistic settings. Classical ILP relies on discrete combinatorial rule search and is brittle under uncertainty, while differentiable ILP methods typically depend on predefined rule templates or inaccurate fuzzy operators that suffer from vanishing gradients or poor approximation of logical structure when reasoning over probabilistic predicate valuations. This paper proposes an Attention-based Neuro-symbolic Differentiable Rule Extractor (ANDRE), a novel ILP framework that learns first-order logic programs by optimizing over a continuous rule space with attention-based logical operators. ANDRE replaces both rule templates and logical operators with fully differentiable, attention-driven conjunction and disjunction operators that approximate logical min-max semantics, enabling accurate, stable, and interpretable reasoning over probabilistic data. By softly selecting, negating, or excluding predicates within each rule, ANDRE supports flexible rule induction while preserving symbolic structure. Extensive experiments on classical ILP benchmarks, large-scale knowledge bases, and synthetic datasets with probabilistic predicates and noisy supervision demonstrate that ANDRE achieves competitive or superior predictive performance while reliably recovering correct symbolic rules under uncertainty. In particular, ANDRE remains robust to moderate label noise, substantially outperforming existing differentiable ILP methods in both rule extraction quality and stability.

</details>


### [154] [Pro$^2$Assist: Continuous Step-Aware Proactive Assistance with Multimodal Egocentric Perception for Long-Horizon Procedural Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04227)
*Lilin Xu,Bufang Yang,Siyang Jiang,Kaiwei Liu,Kaiyuan Hou,Yuang Fan,Hongkai Chen,Zhenyu Yan,Xiaofan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Pro²Assist，一种基于多模态大模型的步骤感知主动助手，利用AR眼镜数据持续跟踪用户在多步骤程序性任务中的进度，通过感知用户状态和推理需求，实时提供主动式辅助，在动作理解准确率上超越基线21%，主动辅助时机准确率提升至2.29倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人助手主要提供被动式响应（用户查询触发）或针对短期孤立事件的有限主动辅助，无法在长程程序性任务中持续追踪用户进度并提供及时帮助，因此需要一种能感知细粒度任务进度并主动推理用户需求的系统。

Method: Pro²Assist利用AR眼镜的多模态数据进行基于运动的感知，从多尺度时间动态和任务特定专家知识中提取面向步骤的程序性上下文，基于感官输入和程序性上下文进行持续推理以推断用户需求，并在AR眼镜上显示及时辅助信息。

Result: 在公开数据集和真实AR眼镜数据集上，Pro²Assist的程序性动作理解准确率比最优基线高21%以上，主动辅助时机准确率达到基线的2.29倍；用户研究中90%的参与者认为Pro²Assist有用。

Conclusion: Pro²Assist在长程程序性任务的主动辅助方面显著优于现有方法，证明了其在真实世界程序性辅助任务中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Procedural tasks with multiple ordered steps are ubiquitous in daily life. Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have enabled personal assistants that support daily activities. However, existing systems primarily provide reactive guidance triggered by user queries, or limited proactive assistance for isolated short-term events rather than long-horizon procedural tasks. In this work, we introduce Pro$^2$Assist, a step-aware proactive assistant that continuously tracks fine-grained task progress and reasons over the user's evolving state to provide timely assistance throughout tasks. Pro$^2$Assist leverages multimodal data from augmented reality (AR) glasses to achieve motion-based perception. It then extracts step-oriented procedural context from multi-scale temporal dynamics and task-specific expert knowledge. Based on both sensory input and procedural context, Pro$^2$Assist performs continuous reasoning to infer user needs and display timely assistance on AR glasses. We evaluate Pro$^2$Assist using a dataset curated from public sources and a real-world dataset collected on our testbed with AR glasses. Extensive evaluations show that Pro$^2$Assist outperforms the best-performing baselines by over 21% in procedural action understanding accuracy, and it achieves up to 2.29x the proactive timing accuracy of baselines. A user study with 20 participants further shows that 90% find Pro$^2$Assist useful, indicating its effectiveness for real-world procedural assistance.

</details>


### [155] [Temporal Reasoning Is Not the Bottleneck: A Probabilistic Inconsistency Framework for Neuro-Symbolic QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04243)
*Tran Quang Liem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"大语言模型在复杂时间推理任务中表现脆弱是因为自回归逻辑推理能力不足"的主流观点，证明真正的瓶颈在于非结构化文本到事件表示的转换。作者提出了一个基于概率不一致信号(PIS)的神经符号问答框架，将语义提取与符号推理严格解耦。实验表明，当提供正确的结构表示时，系统在时间算术基准上达到完美1.0准确率(4000/4000)；在噪声注入的QA设置中，框架保持75.1%准确率，并支持确定性故障定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战主流观点——即LLM在时间推理上的失败源于自回归逻辑演绎能力不足，揭示真正的瓶颈在于非结构化文本到事件表示的转换问题。

Method: 提出一个神经符号问答框架，包含：(1) 将非结构化文本显式提升为事件图和区间约束，实现语义提取与符号推理引擎的解耦；(2) 概率不一致信号(PIS)，统一符号置信区间与基于证据深度学习从LLM隐藏状态提取的认知神经不确定性，用于检测结构断裂。

Result: 在提供正确结构表示时，系统在时间算术基准上达到完美1.0准确率(4000/4000)，零假阳性/假阴性。在噪声注入的QA设置中，框架保持75.1%准确率，支持确定性、步骤级的故障定位。

Conclusion: 时间QA问题应从算法推理挑战重新定义为结构对齐问题，为可靠的神经符号AI提供了可验证的发展路径。

Abstract: Despite significant advances, large language models (LLMs) continue to exhibit brittle performance on complex temporal reasoning tasks. This failure mode is widely attributed to inherent deficits in autoregressive logical deduction. In this paper, we challenge this prevailing narrative, demonstrating that temporal reasoning is not the fundamental bottleneck; rather, the locus of failure lies in unstructured text-to-event representation. We introduce a novel neuro-symbolic question-answering framework governed by a Probabilistic Inconsistency Signal (PIS) that explicitly isolates perceptual errors from reasoning failures. By lifting unstructured text into explicit event graphs and interval constraints, our architecture strictly decouples semantic extraction from a symbolic reasoning engine. To robustly detect structural breaks, the PIS elegantly unifies symbolic credal intervals with epistemic neural uncertainty extracted via Evidential Deep Learning on LLM hidden states. Empirical evaluations reveal a striking paradigm shift: when provided with correct structural representations, our system's explicit proof traces achieve perfect 1.0 accuracy (4000/4000) and strictly zero false positives/negatives on temporal arithmetic benchmarks. On broader, noise-injected QA settings, the framework maintains a competitive 75.1\% accuracy while enabling deterministic, step-level failure localization. Ultimately, by isolating the representation bottleneck from the reasoning substrate, this work reframes temporal QA from an algorithmic reasoning challenge to a structural alignment problem, charting a verifiable path forward for reliable neuro-symbolic AI.

</details>


### [156] [Parallel Prefix Verification for Speculative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04263)
*Yuncheng Yao,Yuxuan Xia,Shengjie Wang,Danyang Zhuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PARSE是一种并行前缀推测引擎，通过语义级并行前缀验证加速LLM推理，无需逐token或逐段顺序验证，可在单次前向传播中识别最大有效前缀，实现1.25×至4.3×的吞吐增益，与EAGLE-3组合可达4.5×，且精度损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法受限于token级等价验证，导致接受长度短、加速比有限；而语义级或段级验证虽能增加接受粒度，但依赖顺序验证，引入显著开销。亟需一种既能实现语义级验证粒度、又避免顺序验证开销的高效推理加速方法。

Method: 提出PARSE框架，核心创新是并行前缀验证机制：从草稿模型获取完整草稿后，目标模型通过自定义注意力掩码在单次前向传播中并行评估多个前缀的正确性，直接识别最大有效前缀，无需逐段顺序验证。

Result: 在多种模型和基准测试上，PARSE相对目标模型实现1.25×至4.3×的吞吐增益；与EAGLE-3组合时可达到1.6×至4.5×的增益，且精度退化可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 并行前缀验证是一种有效且通用的LLM推理加速方法，与token级推测解码正交并可组合使用，为提升推测解码的效率提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce PARSE (PArallel pRefix Speculative Engine), a speculative generation framework that accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by parallelizing prefix verification on a semantic level. Existing speculative decoding methods are fundamentally limited by token-level equivalence: the target model must verify each token, leading to short acceptance lengths and modest speedups. Moving to semantic or segment-level verification can substantially increase acceptance granularity, but prior approaches rely on sequential verification, introducing significant overhead and limiting practical gains. PARSE introduces parallel prefix verification, enabling semantic-level verification without sequential checks. Given a full draft from a draft model, the target model evaluates correctness across multiple prefixes in a single forward pass using a custom attention mask, directly identifying the maximal valid prefix. This eliminates sequential segment verification, and makes verification compute-efficient. PARSE is orthogonal to token-level speculative decoding and can be composed with it for additional gains. Across models and benchmarks, PARSE delivers $1.25\times$ to $4.3\times$ throughput gain over the target model, and $1.6\times$ to $4.5\times$ when composed with EAGLE-3, all with negligible accuracy degradation. This demonstrates parallel prefix verification as an effective, general approach to accelerating LLM inference.

</details>


### [157] [The Scaling Properties of Implicit Deductive Reasoning in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04330)
*Enrico Vompa,Tanel Tammet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了深度受限Transformer在Horn子句隐式演绎推理中的缩放特性。通过系统性去相关性证明与伪特征，并强制执行算法对齐，发现在足够深且具有双向前缀掩码的模型中，隐式推理在各种图拓扑和问题宽度上接近显式CoT性能，但CoT在深度外推上仍然必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究深度受限Transformer在隐式演绎推理任务上的缩放特性，以及隐式推理能否在无需显式链式思维（CoT）的情况下达到接近CoT的性能水平。

Method: 系统性地去相关证明与伪特征之间的关联，强制执行算法对齐，使用双向前缀掩码的深度Transformer模型，在Horn子句推理任务上进行实验，比较隐式推理与显式CoT在不同图拓扑和问题宽度下的表现。

Result: 在足够深的双向前缀掩码Transformer中，隐式推理在多种图拓扑和问题宽度上接近显式CoT的性能，但在深度外推任务上CoT仍然必要。

Conclusion: 深度和算法对齐是隐式推理接近CoT性能的关键因素，但隐式推理在深度外推场景下仍存在局限，CoT在需要外推到更深推理链的任务中不可替代。

Abstract: We investigate the scaling properties of implicit deductive reasoning over Horn clauses in depth-bounded Transformers. By systematically decorrelating provability from spurious features and enforcing algorithmic alignment, we find that in sufficiently deep models with a bidirectional prefix mask, implicit reasoning approaches explicit CoT performance across graph topologies and problem widths, though CoT remains necessary for depth extrapolation.

</details>


### [158] [When Context Hurts: The Crossover Effect of Knowledge Transfer on Multi-Agent Design Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04361)
*Saranyan Vigraham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现多智能体软件设计中，增加上下文信息并非总是有益，存在交叉效应：同一类文档在某些任务中能提升探索性能（最高20倍），在另一些任务中反而降低性能（最多降低46%）。通过基线探索率（无上下文时）可预测影响方向（Pearson r=-0.82, p<0.001），建议采用有条件的上下文注入策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战多智能体编排中"更多上下文更好"的普遍假设，探究上下文注入对不同任务的差异化影响及背后机制。

Method: 在10个任务、7种上下文注入条件、超过2700次运行中测试多智能体软件设计，通过操纵提示中的收敛压力探究机制，区分自然收敛（由训练数据先验驱动）和诱导收敛（由显式指令驱动）两种模式。

Result: 发现上下文注入存在交叉效应：同一类文档在不同任务中效果相反（提升最高20倍 vs. 降低46%）；存在基线探索率与影响方向的强负相关（r=-0.82）；自然收敛对文档干扰敏感，诱导收敛则不敏感。

Conclusion: 上下文注入应是有条件的而非普适的：一次无上下文的试运行即可作为廉价诊断工具，预测知识文档对特定任务是有益还是有害。

Abstract: The prevailing assumption in agent orchestration is that more context is better. We test this on multi-agent software design across 10 tasks, 7 context-injection conditions, and over 2,700 runs, and find a crossover effect: the same artifact type improves design exploration on some tasks (up to 20$\times$ tradeoff coverage) and actively degrades it on others (up to 46% reduction). On several tasks, an irrelevant document performs as well as or better than every relevant artifact. The direction is predicted by a single measurable variable--baseline exploration without context--with Pearson $r = -0.82$ ($p < 0.001$). Probing the mechanism by manipulating convergence pressure through prompt design reveals two distinct regimes: convergence driven by training data priors (natural) responds to artifact disruption, while convergence driven by explicit instructions (induced) does not. The implication is that context injection should be conditional, not universal: one no-context trial is a cheap diagnostic that predicts whether knowledge artifacts will help or hurt a given task.

</details>


### [159] [Deployment-Relevant Alignment Cannot Be Inferred from Model-Level Evaluation Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04454)
*Varad Vishwarupe,Nigel Shadbolt,Marina Jirotka,Ivan Flechais*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了对齐评估不能仅靠模型级评估，而应区分模型级、响应级、交互级和部署级四个层级。通过对11个对齐基准的结构化审计和跨模型压力测试，发现现有基准普遍缺乏用户侧验证支持和过程可操控性，且脚手架有效性具有模型依赖性。提出系统级评估议程：对齐画像、固定脚手架协议和报告模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习中的对齐评估主要停留在模型级评估，但部署相关的对齐无法从模型级评估中可靠推断，需要在更细粒度的层级上收集证据。

Method: 1) 对11个对齐基准进行结构化审计，扩展至16个基准语料库，采用八维度评分体系进行双编码（Cohen's kappa=0.87）；2) 盲法跨模型压力测试，使用180份交互记录，涵盖3个前沿模型和4种脚手架。

Result: 所有基准均缺乏用户侧验证支持，过程可操控性几乎完全缺失；少数交互式基准覆盖分散；相同的验证脚手架在不同模型上效果差异显著（一个模型达到上限，另一个模型无变化）。

Conclusion: 部署相关的对齐评估需要系统级方法，包括多维度对齐画像、固定脚手架协议用于可比交互评估，以及明确评估证据与部署声明之间推断距离的报告模板。

Abstract: Alignment evaluation in machine learning has largely become evaluation of models. Influential benchmarks score model outputs under fixed inputs, such as truthfulness, instruction following, or pairwise preference, and these scores are often used to support claims about deployed alignment. This paper argues that deployment-relevant alignment cannot be inferred from model-level evaluation alone. Alignment claims should instead be indexed to the level at which evidence is collected: model-level, response-level, interaction-level, or deployment-level. Two studies support this position. First, a structured audit of eleven alignment benchmarks, extended to a sixteen-benchmark corpus, dual-coded against an eight-dimension rubric with Cohen's kappa = 0.87, finds that user-facing verification support is absent across every benchmark examined, while process steerability is nearly absent. The few interactional benchmarks identified, including tau-bench, CURATe, Rifts, and Common Ground, remain fragmented in coverage, and benchmark construction rather than data source determines what is measured. Second, a blinded cross-model stress test using 180 transcripts across three frontier models and four scaffolds finds that the same verification scaffold raises one model's verification support to ceiling while leaving another categorically unchanged. This shows that scaffold efficacy is model-dependent and that the gap identified by the audit cannot be closed at the model level alone. We propose a system-level evaluation agenda: alignment profiles instead of single scores, fixed-scaffolding protocols for comparable interactional evaluation, and reporting templates that make the inferential distance between evaluation evidence and deployment claims explicit.

</details>


### [160] [How Does Thinking Mode Change LLM Moral Judgments? A Controlled Instant-vs-Thinking Comparison Across Five Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04488)
*Sai Sourabh Madur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在五个前沿推理大语言模型中，启用推理模式与即时模式在100个道德判断场景上的二元判断一致性很高（Krippendorff's alpha = 0.78 vs 0.79），但在模型争议场景中推理模式缩小了跨模型分歧（平均配对一致率从5.4/10提升至6.7/10），降低了三个模型的判断不一致性，且推理更频繁地改变模型的自我标签伦理框架而非二元裁决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估在相同模型检查点下，启用推理模式是否会改变模型的道德判断，以理解推理过程对道德决策的影响。

Method: 使用100个道德判断场景和五个前沿推理大语言模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT 5.5、Gemini 3 Flash、DeepSeek V3.1、Qwen3.5 397B），比较即时模式与思考模式下的二元判断一致性、跨模型分歧程度、人口统计判断不一致性及自我标签伦理框架变化。

Result: 即时与思考模式在全部场景中的聚合判断一致性较高（alpha=0.78 vs 0.79）；在21个模型争议场景中，即时模式一致性接近随机（alpha=0.08），推理模式将平均配对一致率从5.4/10提升至6.7/10；三个模型的人口统计判断不一致性降低；推理更频繁地改变自我标签伦理框架而非二元裁决。

Conclusion: 推理模式在整体上不显著改变模型的道德判断一致性，但在争议场景中能缩小跨模型分歧并减少部分模型的人口统计判断不一致性；推理对自我标签伦理框架的影响大于对具体判断的影响。

Abstract: We evaluate whether enabling provider-exposed reasoning mode changes moral judgments within the same model checkpoint. Across 100 moral-judgment scenarios and five frontier reasoning-trained LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT 5.5, Gemini 3 Flash, DeepSeek V3.1, and Qwen3.5 397B), aggregate binary-verdict agreement remains high and statistically indistinguishable between instant and thinking modes (Krippendorff's alpha = 0.78 vs. 0.79). However, disagreement is concentrated in 21 model-disputed scenarios, where instant-mode agreement is near chance (alpha = 0.08). On these scenarios, reasoning directionally narrows cross-model disagreement, increasing mean pairwise agreement from 5.4 to 6.7 out of 10. Reasoning also reduces demographic-judgment inconsistency in three of five models and does not increase it for any model. Across all five model families, reasoning changes self-labeled ethical frameworks more often than binary verdicts.

</details>


### [161] [From Parameter Dynamics to Risk Scoring : Quantifying Sample-Level Safety Degradation in LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04572)
*Xiao Wang,Yifei Zhang,YongKang Liu,Xiaocui Yang,Zihan Wang,Shi Feng,Daling Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现良性微调导致LLM参数朝危险方向累积漂移，从而破坏安全对齐，并提出SQSD方法从样本级别量化安全退化风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通过对比微调前后的参数和隐藏状态来解释安全对齐的脆弱性，但忽视了微调过程中的动态演化过程。

Method: 提出SQSD方法，通过计算每个训练样本诱导的参数更新在安全方向与危险方向上的投影差异，为样本分配连续风险分数，量化其对安全退化的影响。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上的实验表明，SQSD能有效量化样本级别的微调风险，并在模型架构、参数规模和参数高效方法上表现出强迁移性。

Conclusion: 良性微调导致参数向危险方向累积漂移是安全退化的关键机制，SQSD可有效识别高风险样本，为安全微调提供指导。

Abstract: Safety alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) is extremely fragile, as fine-tuning on a small number of benign samples can erase safety behaviors learned from millions of preference examples. Existing studies attempt to explain this phenomenon by comparing parameters and hidden states before and after fine-tuning, but overlook their dynamic evolution during fine-tuning. In this paper, we uncover a critical mechanism underlying safety degradation by analyzing parameter dynamics, where benign fine-tuning causes parameters to cumulatively drift toward danger-aligned directions, progressively undermining the model's safety. This finding suggests that samples contributing more to this drift has greater fine-tuning risks. Based on this insight, we propose a method of Sample-Level Quantification of Safety Degradation (SQSD), which quantifies the influence of each training sample on safety degradation. Specifically, SQSD computes continuous risk scores to samples by measuring their induced parameter updates' projection difference between danger and safety directions. Extensive experiments across multiple models and datasets demonstrate that SQSD effectively quantifies sample-level fine-tuning risks and exhibits strong transferability across model architectures, parameter scales, and parameter-efficient methods.

</details>


### [162] [SensingAgents: A Multi-Agent Collaborative Framework for Robust IMU Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04608)
*Naiyu Zheng,Tianlong Yu,Haochen Yin,Xiaoyi Fan,Xiping Hu,Zhimeng Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SensingAgents，一种基于大语言模型的多智能体系统，通过角色分工（分析智能体、辩论智能体、决策智能体）实现鲁棒的IMU人体活动识别，在零样本场景下达到79.5%准确率，远超现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的HAR模型过度依赖标注数据、存在位置特异性歧义且缺乏透明推理能力，亟需一种不依赖标注、能处理多传感器冲突且具备可解释性的方法。

Method: SensingAgents包含三类智能体：①分析智能体群（Analyst Agents）负责各位置（手臂、手腕、腰带、口袋）的传感器数据分析；②辩护智能体对（Advocate Agents）通过动态与静态辩证辩论解决传感器冲突；③决策智能体（Decision Agent）在传感器漂移或失效时确保可靠性。

Result: 在Shoaib数据集上零样本设置下准确率达79.5%，比现有单/多智能体LLM模型高29%，比深度学习基线高9.4%，尤其在多传感器数据冲突或噪声场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作推理能够显著提升泛在感知系统的鲁棒性与可解释性，为无监督HAR提供了新范式。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) sensors is a cornerstone of mobile health, smart environments, and human-computer interaction. However, current deep learning-based HAR models often struggle with heavy reliance on labeled data, position-specific ambiguity, and a lack of transparent reasoning. Inspired by the advanced agents framework, which emulates a collaborative agent using Large Language Models (LLMs), we propose SensingAgents, a novel multi-agent system for robust IMU activity recognition. SensingAgents organizes LLM-powered agents into specialized roles: a group of Analyst Agents for position-specific sensor analysis (arm, wrist, belt, pocket), a pair of Advocate Agents that resolves sensor conflicts through dynamic and static dialectical debates, and a Decision Agent that ensures reliability under sensor drift or failure. Evaluation on the Shoaib dataset demonstrates that SensingAgents significantly outperforms state-of-the-art single-agent and multi-agent LLM models, achieving an accuracy of 79.5% in a zero setting--29% higher than existing agent models and 9.4% higher than deep learning baselines--particularly in complex scenarios where multi-sensor data is conflicting or noisy. Our work highlights the potential of multi-agent collaborative reasoning for advancing the robustness and interpretability of ubiquitous sensing systems.

</details>


### [163] [Reward-Decomposed Reinforcement Learning for Immersive Video Role-Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04733)
*Miao Wang,Yuling Shi,Yijiang Li,Yeheng Chen,Xiaodong Gu,Bin Li,Bo Gao,Yaduan Ruan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EBM-RL框架，通过解耦的GRPO强化学习将观察（感知）、推理（思考）和回答（话语）分离，使文本型角色扮演模型能够感知视觉场景氛围和动态张力，在视频驱动的角色扮演对话中显著超越纯文本基线和大规模视觉语言模型，并展现出强零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本型角色扮演模型能模仿角色风格，但无法感知场景氛围和演变的紧张感，而这对于VR游戏、交互叙事等沉浸式应用至关重要。

Method: 提出EBM-RL（Eye-Brain-Mouth Reinforcement Learning），一种解耦的GRPO框架，强制模型按[感知]→[思考]→[回答]顺序处理；集成四种互补奖励：CLIP场景-文本对齐奖励、感知-认知奖励、答案准确性奖励、格式奖励。

Result: 在沉浸式角色扮演基准上显著优于纯文本基线和更大规模的视觉语言模型；在视觉氛围一致性和角色真实性上同时提升；无需微调即可在域外VideoQA基准上展现强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将视觉感知显式解耦为感知-推理-表达三个阶段并结合结构化奖励，能有效增强角色扮演模型的环境感知和情境适应能力，并具有通用性和泛化潜力。

Abstract: Text-based role-playing models can imitate character styles, yet they often fail to reflect a scene's atmosphere and evolving tension, both essential for immersive applications such as Virtual Reality (VR) games and interactive narratives. We study video-grounded role-playing dialogue and introduce EBM-RL (Eye-Brain-Mouth Reinforcement Learning), a decoupled GRPO-based framework that explicitly separates observation ([perception]), reasoning ([think]), and utterance ([answer]). This structure promotes human-like sensory grounding by compelling the model to first attend to visual cues, then form internal interpretations, and finally generate context-appropriate dialogue.
  EBM-RL integrates four complementary rewards: (i) CLIP-based scene-text alignment to improve ambiance and emotion; (ii) a Perceptual-Cognitive reward that encourages [perception] and [think] processes that increase the likelihood of the reference response; (iii) answer accuracy to ensure faithfulness; and (iv) a dense format reward to enforce the desired structured output.
  Extensive experiments demonstrate that EBM-RL substantially outperforms text-only role-playing baselines and larger-scale vision-language models on our immersive role-playing benchmark, delivering simultaneous gains in visual-atmosphere consistency and character authenticity. Beyond the role-playing domain, EBM-RL also exhibits strong zero-shot generalization: without any additional fine-tuning, it consistently improves performance on out-of-domain VideoQA benchmarks. We additionally release an open-source dataset for video-grounded role-playing dialogue.

</details>


### [164] [AgentTrust: Runtime Safety Evaluation and Interception for AI Agent Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04785)
*Chenglin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentTrust 是一个运行时安全层，在 AI 智能体执行工具调用前进行拦截并返回结构化判决（允许、警告、阻止、审查），结合 shell 反混淆、SafeFix 建议、RiskChain 多步攻击链检测和 LLM-as-Judge 机制。在 300 场景内部基准上达到 95.0% 判决准确率，在 630 对抗场景上达到 96.7% 判决准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 AI 智能体通过工具调用执行真实世界副作用（文件操作、shell 命令、HTTP 请求、数据库查询等），单个不安全操作可能导致不可逆危害。现有防御方案（事后基准测试、静态护栏、基础设施沙箱）均不完整，无法在运行时有效拦截安全威胁。

Method: AgentTrust 提出运行时安全层架构，在智能体工具调用执行前进行拦截，包含四个核心组件：1) shell 反混淆归一化器；2) SafeFix 安全替代建议生成；3) RiskChain 多步攻击链检测；4) 缓存感知的 LLM-as-Judge 用于模糊输入判断。最终返回结构化判决（allow/warn/block/review）。

Result: 在内部 300 场景基准（六类风险）上，生产规则集达到 95.0% 判决准确率和 73.7% 风险级别准确率，端到端延迟在低毫秒级。在 630 独立构建的真实世界对抗场景上（使用修补规则集，非零样本），达到 96.7% 判决准确率，其中 shell 混淆载荷检测约 93%。

Conclusion: AgentTrust 作为 AGPL-3.0 开源项目发布，提供 MCP（模型上下文协议）服务器以兼容 MCP 智能体，为 AI 智能体工具调用提供了有效的运行时安全防护方案。

Abstract: Modern AI agents execute real-world side effects through tool calls such as file operations, shell commands, HTTP requests, and database queries. A single unsafe action, including accidental deletion, credential exposure, or data exfiltration, can cause irreversible harm. Existing defenses are incomplete: post-hoc benchmarks measure behavior after execution, static guardrails miss obfuscation and multi-step context, and infrastructure sandboxes constrain where code runs without understanding what an action means.
  We present AgentTrust, a runtime safety layer that intercepts agent tool calls before execution and returns a structured verdict: allow, warn, block, or review. AgentTrust combines a shell deobfuscation normalizer, SafeFix suggestions for safer alternatives, RiskChain detection for multi-step attack chains, and a cache-aware LLM-as-Judge for ambiguous inputs.
  We release a 300-scenario benchmark across six risk categories and an additional 630 independently constructed real-world adversarial scenarios. On the internal benchmark, the production-only ruleset achieves 95.0% verdict accuracy and 73.7% risk-level accuracy at low-millisecond end-to-end latency. On the 630-scenario benchmark, evaluated under a patched ruleset and not claimed as zero-shot, AgentTrust achieves 96.7% verdict accuracy, including about 93% on shell-obfuscated payloads. AgentTrust is released under the AGPL-3.0 license and provides a Model Context Protocol server for MCP-compatible agents.

</details>


### [165] [DecodingTrust-Agent Platform (DTap): A Controllable and Interactive Red-Teaming Platform for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04808)
*Zhaorun Chen,Xun Liu,Haibo Tong,Chengquan Guo,Yuzhou Nie,Jiawei Zhang,Mintong Kang,Chejian Xu,Qichang Liu,Xiaogeng Liu,Tianneng Shi,Chaowei Xiao,Sanmi Koyejo,Percy Liang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DecodingTrust-Agent Platform (DTap)——首个可控、可交互的AI智能体红队测试平台，覆盖14个真实领域和50多个模拟环境（如Google Workspace、Paypal、Slack）。同时提出了DTap-Red自主红队测试智能体和DTap-Bench大规模红队测试数据集，通过系统性评估揭示了主流AI智能体的安全漏洞模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在长期、高风险的动态环境中执行复杂工作流，面临严重的安全与隐私威胁。现有研究缺乏可控、可复现的大规模风险评估环境，难以系统性评估智能体的安全性。

Method: 构建DTap平台（14个真实领域、50+模拟环境），提出DTap-Red自主红队攻击智能体（探索多种注入向量：提示、工具、技能、环境、组合攻击），并构建DTap-Bench基准数据集（含可验证的评判器）。

Result: 基于多种主干模型的主流AI智能体评估揭示了系统性漏洞模式，覆盖安全策略、风险类别和攻击策略。

Conclusion: DTap为AI智能体的安全评估提供了首个大规模可控平台，揭示了系统性安全漏洞模式，为开发下一代安全智能体提供了重要见解。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed across diverse domains to automate complex workflows through long-horizon and high-stakes action executions. Due to their high capability and flexibility, such agents raise significant security and safety concerns. A growing number of real-world incidents have shown that adversaries can easily manipulate agents into performing harmful actions, such as leaking API keys, deleting user data, or initiating unauthorized transactions. Evaluating agent security is inherently challenging, as agents operate in dynamic, untrusted environments involving external tools, heterogeneous data sources, and frequent user interactions. However, realistic, controllable, and reproducible environments for large-scale risk assessment remain largely underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce the DecodingTrust-Agent Platform (DTap), the first controllable and interactive red-teaming platform for AI agents, spanning 14 real-world domains and over 50 simulation environments that replicate widely used systems such as Google Workspace, Paypal, and Slack. To scale the risk assessment of agents in DTap, we further propose DTap-Red, the first autonomous red-teaming agent that systematically explores diverse injection vectors (e.g., prompt, tool, skill, environment, combinations) and autonomously discovers effective attack strategies tailored to varying malicious goals. Using DTap-Red, we curate DTap-Bench, a large-scale red-teaming dataset comprising high-quality instances across domains, each paired with a verifiable judge to automatically validate attack outcomes. Through DTap, we conduct large-scale evaluations of popular AI agents built on various backbone models, spanning security policies, risk categories, and attack strategies, revealing systematic vulnerability patterns and providing valuable insights for developing secure next-generation agents.

</details>


### [166] [Strat-Reasoner: Reinforcing Strategic Reasoning of LLMs in Multi-Agent Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04906)
*Yidong He,Yutao Lai,Pengxu Yang,Jiarui Gan,Jiexin Wang,Yi Cai,Mengchen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Strat-Reasoner，一种基于强化学习的框架，通过递归推理范式、集中式CoT比较模块和分组相对RL优化，提升大语言模型在多智能体博弈中的策略推理能力，平均性能提升22.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在单一推理任务上表现出色，但在多智能体博弈中，由于其他智能体的非平稳性导致推理过程评估困难、信用分配复杂。现有单智能体RL及其多智能体扩展方法未将其他智能体纳入推理过程，无法有效应对这些挑战。

Method: 提出Strat-Reasoner框架：(1) 递归推理范式，将其他智能体的推理过程纳入当前智能体的推理中；(2) 集中式CoT比较模块，评估中间推理序列的质量并提供奖励信号；(3) 混合优势计算与分组相对RL优化方法，用于优化LLM策略。

Result: 在多种多智能体博弈任务上，Strat-Reasoner显著提升了基础LLM的策略能力，平均性能提升22.1%。

Conclusion: 将其他智能体的推理纳入递归推理过程，结合集中式CoT评估与分组相对RL优化，能有效提升LLM在多智能体博弈中的策略推理能力。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel in certain reasoning tasks, they struggle in multi-agent games where the final outcome depends on the joint strategies of all agents. In multi-agent games, the non-stationarity of other agents brings significant challenges on the evaluation of the reasoning process and the credit assignment over multiple reasoning steps. Existing single-agent reinforcement learning (RL) approaches and their multi-agent extensions fail to address these challenges as they do not incorporate other agents in the reasoning process. In this work, we propose Strat-Reasoner, a novel RL-based framework that improves LLMs' strategic reasoning ability in multi-agent games. We introduce a novel recursive reasoning paradigm where an agent's reasoning also integrates other agents' reasoning processes. To provide effective reward signals for the intermediate reasoning sequences, we employ a centralized Chain-of-Thought (CoT) comparison module to evaluate the reasoning quality. Finally, we compute an accurate hybrid advantage and develop a group-relative RL approach to optimize the LLM policy. Experimental results show that Strat-Reasoner substantially improves strategic abilities of underlying LLMs, achieving 22.1\% average performance improvements across various multi-agent games.

</details>


### [167] [A Foundation Model for Zero-Shot Logical Rule Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04916)
*Yin Jun Phua*
